Berserk was a racial magic with an exceptionally high barrier to entry. If its drawback was conquered, its ceiling became extraordinarily high, making it a world-class, top level talent.

The primary reason the Barbarian Fish bloodline bore the 'Barbarian' term was precisely this Berserk talent.

The dragonman youngster attempted the Berserk state in the meditation chamber. Just as it was about to take effect, he sensed he would lose his sanity and decisively halted the process.

The gold level fishman bloodline held little appeal for him.

The Bloodline of the Flame Dragon King was legend level.

The current concentration of the Flame Dragon King bloodline remained at 34%. During their time in Snow Bird Harbor, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps conducted multiple transactions with the city lord, allowing the dragonman youngster to absorb numerous dragon beast materials. However, since most were from white dragon bloodline beasts and wyverns, they offered no benefit to the Flame Dragon King bloodline.

Even so, the boost from 34% Flame Dragon King blood far surpassed that of 52% fishman blood. Whether in defense or recovery, the former vastly exceeded the latter.

After all, the former was legend level blood, while the latter was merely gold level, a full tier below, separated by the saint level.

What intrigued the dragonman youngster more was the 11% fishman bloodline.

Upon activating this bloodline, the dragonman youngster's appearance transformed into what Zhong Yong witnessed—thick layers of fat, not as tall as the Barbarian Fish bloodline form but still standing at 1.6 meters, with deep blue fish scales and black wavy patterns.

“A mere 11% concentration could cause such complete physical transformation—this bloodline is exceptionally potent!”

The dragonman youngster remained unaware that this fishman bloodline surpassed even the Flaming Dragon King's in grade, being the god level Surging Wave fishman bloodline!

War Merchant's database offered no clear matching results.

“With only 11% blood concentration, many bloodline traits haven't manifested yet."

“If Zi Di were here, we might be able to test it with a blood-testing potion."

But that was only a “might.”

Blood-testing potions weren't accurate; they were full of pitfalls.

“Calculating the time, Zi Di should be departing Snow Bird Harbor for the royal capital soon.”

The dragonman youngster sighed at this thought.

He longed to return.

The journey to the royal capital was crucial for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

But there was nothing he could do. He had been unexpectedly teleported into the Ocean Mother Nest. Even now, he didn't know his exact coordinates.

“With the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, Deceptive Disguise spell, and Zong Ge by her side, the mercenary corps should be fine for the short term.”

“Hopefully, the connection with Snow Bird Harbor City Lord will prove useful, allowing the royal capital's network to lend support.”

The dragonman youngster gathered his thoughts and focused on the present.

The Ocean Mother Nest isolated its inhabitants from the outside world, existing almost entirely in isolation. His current predicament could only be overcome through his own efforts, relying solely on himself to break through.

“I must quickly become familiar with this fishman form to prevent the worst-case scenario.”

“The Affinity Aura has already been tested. Next up are Mental Flogging and Mind's Eye.”

The Surging Wave fishman bloodline had granted the youngster four transcendent talents.

In addition to the above three, there was also a healing spell.

The healing spell was the first to be tested, and it proved highly effective.

"I've witnessed the effects of the Affinity Aura. In this form, it's my first meeting with Zhong Yong, yet I managed to draw out the true thoughts of this silver level magician. This transcendent ability significantly enhances my leadership and interpersonal skills, making it an exceptionally valuable support ability."

"However, the only test subject so far has been Zhong Yong. I wonder how effective it would be on races other than fishmen."

"Mental Flogging... involves using one's own mental energy to lash out at the target. The intensity of the lash depends partly on the caster's mental strength and partly on their active control."

Bloodlines contained all the life information of an individual.

Higher level bloodlines carried vastly more data than lower ones.

The dragonman youngster had gained four transcendent talents from the Surging Wave fishman bloodline, each accompanied by detailed information instinctively.

The dragonman youngster did not know the nature of this bloodline yet possessed innate understanding of these four innate spells.

In truth, the information within a higher bloodline naturally identifies its own lineage.

For instance, when a dragon hatchling is born from an abandoned dragon egg. Its bloodline information would inherently let it know it was a dragon. It would also specify whether it belonged to a particular type of dragon, such as a green dragon or a red dragon.

However, the dragonman youngster possessed only an 11% concentration of the Surging Wave fishman bloodline, resulting in incomplete information.

The presence of information for all four extraordinary talents was not mere luck. The fact that he could actually use them indicated that this portion of the information was relatively intact. Even if incomplete, it certainly exceeded 50%.

Thus, the dragonman youngster knew both the names of these four transcendent talents and their general nature.

However, the actual effects when used had to be analyzed on a case-by-case basis.

Given that the youngster possessed only 11% Surging Wave fishman bloodline, the racial magic effects he could manifest would inevitably be far weaker than those of a carrier with 100% bloodline.

The dragonman youngster showed little interest in the Mental Flogging racial magic.

Given a choice, he would prefer to replace this offensive transcendent talent with the defensive Mental Barrier.

That was his true weakness.

For offensive capabilities, he had numerous alternatives, like weapons, equipment, spell scrolls, and so forth.

"The final of the four transcendent talents is Mind's Eye. According to the bloodline records: when the user employs this racial magic, they gain a unique transcendent vision. Within this field of vision, all living beings reveal fragments of their mental secrets."

The first question that arose in the dragonman youngster's mind upon receiving this information was: “What exactly are these so-called mental secrets?”

Unfortunately, he had no way of knowing.

The bloodline information abruptly ended, offering no further explanation.

To test this transcendent talent, the dragonman youngster first considered Zhong Yong as his target.

However, he remembered Zhong Yong was currently engaged in alchemy work. This task was far more important than the youngster experimenting with his newly acquired ability.

Therefore, in the end, the youngster only tested the Affinity Aura ability on Zhong Yong.

“Clearly, Mind's Eye is a reconnaissance skill, likely similar to the ski goggles,” the youngster speculated inwardly.

The ski goggles, forged by Bu Quan, could mark external threats and their relative danger level to the user.

This practical alchemy device had proven immensely helpful to the youngster.

The former dragonman youngster, now a fishman youngster, activated Mind's Eye.

He first scanned his own body, but nothing unusual appeared.

“Does this skill not work on oneself?”

After pondering, the fishman youngster headed to the storage room.

There, he discovered a large quantity of tide snake eggs.

They had gathered many of these eggs during their time on Snake Rat Island. Though they had sold some later, they still retained the majority of them.

Under the Mind's Eye, these tide snake eggs all displayed an almost identical white glow.

This white light wasn't pure white, but rather a milky white. To the fishman youngster, it radiated a sense of young, hopeful vitality.

"Compared to colored light, this mental perception is far more significant. Thus, the essence of the Mind's Eye lies not in vision, but in the mind, it prioritizes spiritual sensation."

“As for why they appear white... could it be because these tide snakes haven't hatched yet, leaving Mind's Eye blank?”

He also keenly observed that the milky white glow emanating from each tide snake egg exhibited subtle variations.

While most appeared spherical, some displayed unusual shapes.

Beyond this, the size of the milky white light clusters also differed. Some tide snake eggs were large, yet their milky white light clusters remained small. Others were quite small, yet the milky-white spheres they projected within the mental vision were substantial in volume.

“What does this mean?”

"Could it be that eggs with larger milky-white spheres hatch tide snakes with richer mental worlds? Or perhaps higher spiritual potential?"

After letting his thoughts wander briefly, the fishman youngster fetched a mirror and examined the eggs through its surface.

Yet the eggs reflected in the mirror showed no unusual signs.

The fishman youngster couldn't discern a single milky-white light cluster.

“So, must we observe the living beings themselves directly?”

To verify this, the fishman youngster made a special trip back to the cockpit. Through the monitoring devices there, he observed the tide snake eggs in the storage room once more.

Still, not a single milky-white light cluster was visible.

Returning to the storage room, the fishman youngster retrieved his ski goggles and donned them.

However, he did not activate the alchemy device, choosing instead to simply look through the lenses.

This time, he still saw countless milky-white light clusters.

The fishman youngster fell deep in thought.

Moments later, he retrieved several pieces of glass. These panes were exceptionally clear and thick. They were spare parts for the Deep Sea Monster Fish's portholes.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish possessed numerous circular portholes. These windows employed exceptionally thick glass, capable of withstanding external water pressure while allowing one-way light transmission.

This meant crew members could observe the exterior from within the ship, yet nothing inside was visible from outside.

The fishman youngster continued observing the tide snake eggs through these panes.

When the glass panels reached eleven in number, the luminous anomaly in his mental vision vanished.

“So, if too many barriers separate me from the observation target, I won't be able to perceive the mental secret either.”

“Could I try observing that saint level sea beast?”

Once this thought surfaced in the fishman youngster's mind, he couldn't suppress it.

To be honest, it was a bit risky.

After all, it was a reconnaissance skill. Using it risked awakening the slumbering saint level sea beast and provoking its wrath.

After careful consideration, the fishman youngster concluded the risk was relatively low.

First, the Mind's Eye was not an offensive skill, merely reconnaissance. The saint level sea beast was currently asleep, making it unlikely to be startled awake.

Second, the fishman youngster had complete confidence in his Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell and Deceptive Disguise spell. These had consumed god level materials. They had consistently provided protection for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps without a single slip-up.

Finally, the fishman youngster realized he had no choice but to take the risk. Because next, he would deploy those dual-eared reconnaissance puppets to conduct a thorough, genuine reconnaissance of the Ocean Mother Nest. Compared to sonic transmission and reception, the Mind's Eye's reconnaissance power was far weaker, making the chance of waking or angering the saint level sea beast much smaller.

Rather than risking greater danger later, it was better to use this innate ability now and see.

After making his decision, the fishman youngster first went to the cockpit to make preparations, bringing the Deep Sea Monster Fish to its maximum operational state.

Then, he moved to a window suitable for direct observation.

Standing before the window, he once again activated his transcendent talent, Mind's Eye.

A blood-red, volcano-like mass of mental light instantly filled his field of vision. The crimson light resembled both fire and blood, radiating profound fury and hatred.

At the base of this volcanic mass lay vast swathes of pitch-black darkness, evoking an unfathomable sense of mental abyss.

Merely observing this anomaly subjected the fishman youngster's mind to a violent assault of rage and world-defying hatred!

The fishman youngster hastily withdrew his mental vision, and in the next instant, his sight returned to normal.

“What on earth is going on?”

“Wasn't the saint level sea beast supposed to grow in isolation, free from want, pure as a blank sheet of paper?”

Doubt and alarm surged within the fishman youngster.

In his estimation, the saint level sea beast should have been a giant sea beast cub. Instead, he discovered a world-weary murderer!

The difference between the two was simply too vast.

“Did I misread it?”

“No, this direct mental perception cannot be wrong.”

“So, what exactly is going on?”

As if responding to the fishman youngster's inner doubts, a powerful disturbance suddenly erupted within the Ocean Mother Nest.

In the next instant, a massive horde of sharp-clawed water monkeys was teleported inside!

The saint level sea beast awoke!

It stretched its limbs, emitting furious mental waves, and charged in while the water monkeys were still dazed.

Only then did the fishman youngster realize the saint level sea beast was a colossal octopus!

The octopus sea beast wildly lashed its tentacles, unleashing a bloody carnage among the water monkeys.

After a brutal slaughter, the water monkeys became the sea beast's meal, swallowed whole, one by one.

Watching the saint level sea beast's savage display, cold sweat beaded on the fishman youngster's forehead.

“It seems this teleportation phenomenon has been ongoing for quite some time, it's not just us this time!”

“That's why this saint level sea beast is so ferocious and brutal.”

“If not for the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell and Deceptive Disguise spell, the Deep Sea Monster Fish would have been torn apart the instant it was teleported in!”

The thought sent a chill down the fishman youngster's spine.

His brow furrowed deeply as he sank into profound distress: “How on earth am I supposed to tame such a ferocious saint level sea beast?”

Chapter 610: Snow Pear Octopus…Slice

The pure slaughter slowly drew to a close before the fishman youngster's eyes.

The sharp-clawed water monkey horde fled in disarray, dying in their escape, ultimately becoming prey for the saint level sea beast.

Blood stained the Ocean Mother Nest crimson, filling the fishman youngster's vision with a pale red hue.

The saint level sea beast opened its massive jaws, sucking in water with tremendous force.

The blood-tinged water was swallowed whole, only to be expelled as colorless seawater.

“This sea beast didn't even spare the blood...” The fishman youngster witnessed this detail, once again feeling the sheer savagery of the saint level sea beast.

Having finished its hunt, the saint level sea beast retreated back into its lair. Just as the fishman youngster had seen before, it curled into a snow-capped mountain.

Soon, the waters within the Ocean Mother Nest grew still once more, as if the bloody slaughter had been nothing but an illusion, as if it had never happened at all.

A chill gripped the fishman youngster's heart.

He grasped a harsh truth - the saint level sea beast sharing his chamber was no helpless, innocent, curious little creature. It was a cunning, vicious predator, a being standing at the apex of the food chain.

“One could say it is the master of this Ocean Mother Nest.”

“Regardless of the origin of the teleportation mechanism, this saint level sea beast has fully adapted to this environment.”

“It was nurtured here, grew here, fought here, and was tempered time and again here. Ultimately, only it survived.”

“Or perhaps... it, like me, was also teleported here?”

The fishman youngster pondered for a moment before venturing to use his transcendent talent, Mind's Eye, on the saint level sea beast once more.

Through the circular porthole, he glimpsed the creature's mental landscape again.

The volcanic shape remained unchanged, but now the crater erupted with vast flows of magma.

The deep black that had pooled at the volcano's base had nearly all transformed into a deep crimson.

In the upper half of the crater, the original deep red had faded to a lighter crimson.

Near the crater's rim, the once-red hues had even shifted toward a faint yellow.

Witnessing this mental landscape, the fishman youngster simultaneously sensed the saint level sea beast's current state of mind.

It was the faint pleasure and relief of venting accumulated hatred and fury after a brutal slaughter.

There was also the lingering dissatisfaction of not being fully sated!

“This saint level sea beast's nature is undoubtedly savage and tyrannical!”

“The Ocean Mother Nest could provide ample nourishment for its growth, yet it remains unsatisfied.”

“Having tasted the savory flavor of blood, how could it possibly settle for bland, insipid fare?”

"In its normal state, it curls into a ball, sinking into slumber, waiting for the teleportation mechanism to activate. The moment a living creature is transported in, it launches its attack without delay. While feasting to its heart's content, it also vents its inner savagery, hatred, and other negative emotions."

Having confirmed the saint level sea beast's nature, the fishman youngster had completely abandoned any notion of taming it.

It was impossible to tame.

The fishman youngster was only silver level, not even reaching gold level. Though the Deep Sea Monster Fish was a gold level magic ship, its strongest ability was stealth and concealment. If it were to engage in actual combat against a saint level sea beast, the ship's combat prowess alone was negligible.

When the fishman youngster had acquired the transcendent talent of the Affinity Aura, he had felt a flicker of secret delight.

This ability could also be applied to the saint level sea beast, potentially increasing his chances of subjugation.

Now, that seemed utterly impossible.

To subdue a sea beast of such nature, one had to deliver a brutal beating, forcing submission through sheer strength.

Even then, it would only be physical submission, not mental, leaving significant risks.

Merely expressing affection, or attempting to lure it with benefits, was virtually impossible.

Of course, if the fishman youngster had a master magician by his side, employing potions, magic formations, or other means, taming it might not be entirely out of the question.

But unfortunately, the only magician currently with the fishman youngster was Zhong Yong. He was merely a fishman, a silver level alchemist at best.

“We must leave this place immediately!” the fishman youngster sighed inwardly, acutely aware of the perilous nature of this Ocean Mother Nest.

He hadn't yet deciphered the teleportation mechanism, but he knew for certain that once a transfer occurred, combat would likely erupt within the Mother Nest.

Though the Deep Sea Monster Fish was well-camouflaged and had evaded detection by the saint level sea beast, the space within the Ocean Mother Nest was finite.

Should fierce fighting break out, the Deep Sea Monster Fish would likely be caught in the crossfire.

If this magic ship were to be destroyed, the fishman youngster's tears would run dry.

"To be on the safe side, we'll need to use the Deceptive Disguise spell to alter the appearance of the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

To the uninformed observer, the Deep Sea Monster Fish would appear as nothing more than an ordinary underwater rock formation

Yet in this environment, the submerged rock stood out starkly against the surrounding sea of pearls, appearing utterly out of place.

Though it had temporarily deceived the saint level sea beast, it offered no lasting security.

What if, in the future, boredom drove this saint level sea beast to toy with the reef?

The fishman youngster harbored another concern: if the teleportation mechanism activated again, might intelligent transcendents arriving through it notice something peculiar about the underwater reef and uncover a fragment of the truth?

“If disguised as a pearl, it shouldn't be a single massive pearl, but rather a cluster of tiny pearls,” the fishman youngster decided.

This teleportation and massacre had given him a solid scare.

Still, he hadn't come away empty-handed.

The saint level sea beast had revealed its true form, and the fishman youngster quickly obtained its intelligence from the tower spirit.

It was a Snow Pear Octopus.

Gentle in nature, it subsisted on feeding upon schools of fish in the sea.

In terms of its bloodline, saint level was not its limit. If its bloodline concentration reached a certain threshold, it could ascend to become a legend level transcendent life form!

The Snow Pear Octopus bloodline was legendary in itself. Yet, historically, any legendary Snow Pear Octopus that had ever existed was at least 1,500 years old.

Judging by its size, the one currently in the Ocean Mother Nest was only around sixty years old.

By the Snow Pear Octopus's standards, it was still a child.

The Snow Pear Octopus's hunt caused significant turmoil. Amidst the relentless churning of the sea, the Deep Sea Monster Fish's hull shook violently.

This disturbance forced Zhong Yong to halt his alchemy temporarily.

The fishman youngster seized the moment to enter the alchemy cabin and relay the latest developments.

Zhong Yong was utterly stunned.

He too possessed knowledge of the Snow Pear Octopus.

Truly befitting a magician of vast learning.

“The Snow Pear Octopus is an exceptionally rare marine creature.”

“It's not typically documented in standard texts.”

“The most memorable book I recall, The Complete Guide to Raw Cuisine, happened to feature a description of it in its later pages.”

"The text records that MasterChef Guo Ren once hunted a Snow Pear Octopus. He sliced the octopus's tentacles into thin, edible slices. Snow Pear Octopus slices are incredibly delicious! The texture is crisp yet slightly chewy, with a flavor that combines the savory richness of seafood alongside a refreshing, fruity sweetness."

“MasterChef Guo Ren directly declared it one of the world's finest ingredients.”

After hearing Zhong Yong's explanation, the fishman youngster was speechless.

“How does this connect to ingredients?”

His curiosity piqued, he inquired about MasterChef Guo Ren's identity.

Zhong Yong explained, "MasterChef Guo Ren is the most renowned snow elf magic chef in the history of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. He's a legendary figure with unparalleled knife skills. His defining battle occurred at the saint level. Within three seconds, he sliced an opponent of equal level into countless thin slices."

"He cherishes peace and isn't bloodthirsty. His greatest passion is creating culinary masterpieces, always identifying as a magic chef. His cooking skills rank among the world's finest, with sashimi being his specialty."

The fishman youngster sighed, “Using Snow Pear Octopus as an ingredient is possible because MasterChef Guo Ren is a legendary magic chef.”

“But here, we are the ingredients for the Snow Pear Octopus.”

Seeing the fishman youngster's troubled expression, Zhong Yong couldn't bear it and offered comfort: “I previously briefed the commander on intelligence regarding the Ocean Mother Nest.”

“Now it seems the actual situation differs greatly from my earlier report.”

“This place is unsafe; it's absolutely no place to linger!”

“Breaking through the Mother Nest and escaping as quickly as possible is the wise course of action.”

The youngster frowned. "But shattering it will create a commotion that will surely provoke the saint level sea beast. We'll be the first targets."

“Not necessarily,” Zhong Yong replied thoughtfully, “It depends on the results from the dual-eared reconnaissance puppets I've refined.”

"If we detect that powerful sea beasts inhabit the area surrounding the Ocean Mother Nest, then even if we shatter the nest, waking and enraging the saint level sea beast, we might still have a chance to escape."

The fishman youngster's eyes lit up at this.

Zhong Yong's proposed plan held some feasibility.

Using the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell and Deceptive Disguise spell, the Deep Sea Monster Fish could disguise itself. Then, the fishman youngster could command alchemy puppets to perform explosive tasks.

The Snow Pear Octopus's nature was savage and tyrannical; once its lair was destroyed, it would inevitably plunge into a fury.

It would vent its rage, and at that moment, the sea beasts on the periphery became the most obvious targets for its fury.

As long as the two sides started fighting, the Deep Sea Monster Fish could take advantage of the chaos to escape.

"Now it all depends on the reconnaissance results. I hope this Ocean Mother Nest is teeming with sea beasts," the fishman youngster silently hoped.

While the fishman youngster on the ocean floor was troubled, Feng Lian on the surface faced his own worries.

The Divine Wind Pirates suffered heavy losses from the sudden attack by the Li Jian Clan leader.

After a swift retreat, Feng Lian immediately ordered a thorough investigation into the mole.

Yet after combing through every detail, even examining the unconscious Lan Zao, no evidence surfaced.

The investigation concluded that everyone was innocent.

After pondering briefly, Feng Lian summoned Bing Xiao and declared bluntly, “The Li Jian Clan leader Mian Li Cang hides vicious treachery beneath a gentle exterior, inflicting devastating casualties on our crew.”

“This debt of blood, I shall repay tenfold!”

“Every second of this hatred feels like torture.”

“Therefore, I won't be sending you to Flower Skirt Island personally.”

“It's not far from here. I'll give you three landing craft, more than enough to get you there.”

Upon hearing this, Bing Xiao instantly grasped Feng Lian's underlying meaning.

Feng Lian still suspected a mole among the Bayonet Gang members!

But he didn't state it outright.

Nor did he expend more effort investigating.

On one hand, it wasn't convenient for him to treat the Bayonet Gang members this way. On the other hand, he didn't have much confidence that any investigation would yield results.

The pirate crew had already given their all during the previous two thorough searches.

Therefore, for Feng Lian, the most hassle-free and effortless approach was to simply get rid of the Bayonet Gang members!

Chapter 611: Departure

The Divine Wind.

Bing Xiao looked at Feng Lian.

The night was deep, and the light in the captain's cabin was dim.

Feng Lian wore a mask, so Bing Xiao couldn't see his expression, but he could sense the latter's tightly furrowed brow.

Feng Lian stated he was in a hurry to seek revenge, so he was leaving the Bayonet Gang members behind that very night. Bing Xiao smiled.

He didn't expose Feng Lian's true motives, instead nodding in agreement. "Lord Feng Lian, the torment of hatred is indeed excruciating. But all that pain will transform into the sweetest wine in the world the moment vengeance is achieved."

“You have already aided us so greatly. Alas, my strength is meager. I can only offer my best wishes here for your complete triumph.”

“Bing Xiao...” Feng Lian sighed. He knew Bing Xiao understood his true intentions, and this display of understanding made him feel a wave of weariness.

“Given the situation, what else can be done?” Feng Lian sighed deeply within.

Bing Xiao's expression remained calm.

As a gang leader himself, Bing Xiao understood Feng Lian completely.

If he were in Feng Lian's shoes, he would have done the same.

It was the logical course of action.

Before the Divine Wind and other pirate ships carried members of the Bayonet Gang, the Divine Wind Pirates had never suffered such ambushes.

Such attacks clearly required precise knowledge of the Divine Wind Pirates' location to execute.

Therefore, it was highly probable that a traitor within the Bayonet Gang possessed some covert means of tracking their location.

That was precisely why Feng Lian, after a thorough investigation yielded no results, had decisively abandoned the Bayonet Gang.

It was the action an excellent leader should take.

For the Divine Wind Pirates, their situation was dire.

Though they had defeated the Kingdom's Second Fleet in their previous battle and escaped successfully, they had gained no spoils, and the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's resources far surpassed those of a mere pirate crew.

The current head of the Li Jian Clan, Mian Li Cang, personally led an ambush against the Divine Wind Pirates.

The pirates suffered heavy losses. They couldn't even mount a defense, let alone fight back, forced to endure the beating passively. This was devastating to morale.

Bing Xiao believed that in a direct confrontation, relying on Feng Lian's strength, they could certainly suppress Mian Li Cang, inflict heavy damage or even kill him. But in the second ambush, Feng Lian's combat prowess was rendered useless, leaving him utterly frustrated.

Bing Xiao looked at Feng Lian. Though he was about to be expelled, he still cautioned, “Lord Feng Lian, if you can find a way to counter Mian Li Cang, revenge is highly achievable.”

In other words, without a means to counter Mian Li Cang, blindly seeking revenge would only lead to certain death.

Feng Lian nodded slightly, “I'm heading out to find the item that counters Mian Li Cang.”

“I know a friend who carries one.”

“I already have follow-up targets and plans. You can leave with peace of mind.”

“But before you go, take some equipment with you.”

“Don't worry, this is a gift of friendship. No payment needed.”

Feng Lian gifted Bing Xiao some weapons and gear. Of course, ample food and water were included as well.

Soon after, the Bayonet Gang members boarded the landing craft.

These three landing craft were essentially Feng Lian's bonus gift, so they were no longer the previous silver level vessels but ordinary ships.

The Bayonet Gang's numbers were already thin, and after the ambush by Mian Li Cang succeeded, they'd conveniently killed off quite a few more of their men.

Still, Feng Lian's donation of supplies meant the three landing craft were packed to the brim, leaving little room to spare.

Standing on the landing craft, Bing Xiao looked up and formally bid farewell to Feng Lian.

The two groups parted ways, heading in opposite directions. Soon, both were swallowed by the thick night, invisible to the naked eye.

The sea stretched out, pitch black.

The sea breeze swept in, carrying a heavy weight that battered the hearts of the Bayonet Gang members.

The Bayonet Gang's morale was visibly low.

They were natives of Snow Bird Harbor. Leaving their homeland behind would make anyone feel melancholy and anxious.

Now, having severed ties with their powerful ally, the Divine Wind Pirates, their sense of disorientation and confusion only deepened

Bing Xiao's brow furrowed slightly.

He scanned the deck.

The human ringleader lay unconscious, his head bandaged.

The comatose Lan Zao rested beside him.

All three landing craft carried wounded members.

The most formidable warrior, Xiong Ju, lay prostrate against the landing craft's gunwale, vomiting incessantly.

As for Spring Fighter, he guarded a black coffin, his entire being radiating an emotion called sorrow.

Within the coffin lay the corpse of Teng Dong Lang.

Without a doubt, this was a band of defeated stragglers.

After the vigorous Teng Dong Lang fell, the aged Bing Xiao had no choice but to take on the responsibility of leadership.

Bing Xiao frequently lowered his head to consult the compass and charts in his hands.

He knew his course was correct.

Yet when he looked up at the vast expanse of dark sea stretching before him, he couldn't help but ask himself: “Even if this is the right direction, can we truly reach our destination?”

As time passed, the darkness gradually faded.

The horizon brightened, the sky was turning pale blue.

The dawn glow spread as the sun rose, casting shimmering reflections upon the sea.

Seabirds chirped overhead in Snow Bird Harbor.

Warships lined the docks in orderly rows, their flags fluttering.

Yue Qian stood at the ship's rail, watching Long Fu and Zi Di depart.

Today marked the departure of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps from Snow Bird Harbor, bound for the capital of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

To solidify their alliance, Zi Di paid a special visit to Kong Pan before leaving.

Zong Ge, who had been posing as Long Fu, also spoke briefly with Yue Qian.

A spatial ripple rippled through the air, and Kong Pan suddenly materialized before Yue Qian. Her gaze followed Zi Di and Zong Ge as they departed. She said, “It seems these days of sparring have brought you and Long Fu remarkably closer.”

Yue Qian nodded, “He was definitely a soldier once. His straightforward nature: perhaps that's why I feel such an instant connection with him.”

Kong Pan acknowledged this with understanding. "That's how it is between people. Some people spend a lifetime together yet never truly reach each other's hearts. But others need only a few days to become kindred spirits."

Yue Qian paused, shaking his head slightly, “We're not quite at that level of friendship yet.”

“Is that so?” Kong Pan glanced at him, neither confirming nor denying.

As an elder in the clan, she knew Yue Qian well.

She had never seen him converse so intimately with anyone before. In the captain's cabin, she and Zi Di chatted while she covertly probed with her mental senses.

Every word of Yue Qian and Zong Ge's conversation reached her ears.

They discussed combat tactics, timing for using battle skills, and the countering relationships between weapons.

Zong Ge and Yue Qian were discussing insights gained from their repeated private sparring sessions over the past few days.

“Tell me,” Kong Pan deliberately tested Yue Qian, “what kind of person do you think Long Fu is?”

She had never been entirely satisfied with this younger generation clansman.

Because compared to a noble, Yue Qian resembled a soldier more.

That was not good.

Without sufficient political acumen, how could Yue Qian shoulder the future of the clan?

Gazing distantly at Long Fu's figure, Yue Qian's tone and expression grew solemn: "He's strong! He'll be even stronger in the future!"

“At least gold level.”

Kong Pan was somewhat surprised; this was already a high assessment. "How did you discern his potential? Are you, at least...implying he could even reach the saint level?"

Yue Qian gave an awkward smile, “It's my intuition.”

Kong Pan's expression instantly grew solemn.

This was the worst possible answer.

Intuition?

What seasoned politician bases judgments on gut feelings?

Noticing Kong Pan's shifting expression, Yue Qian hastily added, "It's not purely intuition. During our sparring, despite using only silver battle qi, he displayed offensive and defensive capabilities on par with mine!"

“His Demolition Fist, Surge, and other battle skills all proved troublesome.”

“When I fought at full strength, I could suppress him, but it was clear his combat power had completely surpassed the normal silver level!”

“I can confirm he wasn't using any powerful equipment.”

"I also saw the magic footage of his battle against Teng Dong Lang. I'm certain his combat strength has surpassed the level he displayed against Teng Dong Lang."

"Such remarkable progress in such a short time indicates exceptional bloodline. Therefore, his is at least gold level!"

After hearing this analysis, Kong Pan's expression finally eased slightly.

“Then let's maintain this relationship with him,” Kong Pan said. "This arms dealer has grand ambitions, aiming to distribute nationwide. I've already established some cooperation with them. If their arms truly match the alchemist's claims, excellent quality, akin to the alchemy crossbows once used by Long Fu, then the Li Jian Clan will become their largest partner."

“Large-scale cooperation with an external arms dealer?” Yue Qian's expression turned skeptical.

Kong Pan sighed, “The winds of change have shifted, Yue Qian. The landscape is different now.”

“The family shouldn't pass up any opportunity to strengthen our forces, even if it means bending the rules.”

“I must admit, the arms dealer backing them has keen insight and excellent timing.”

“Right now is indeed the best chance to enter the kingdom's arms market.”

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord personally saw off “Long Fu”. “Our collaboration was most pleasant. I trust our future cooperation will be even more so.”

The series of events in Snow Bird Harbor had cemented the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord as the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' most reliable ally.

The Li Jian Clan's power surpassed that of the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, but as the partnership was new, everything required initial familiarization.

Though “Long Fu” was not eloquent, Zi Di had prepared him beforehand. While Zong Ge's words weren't polished, he could manage the situation.

Soon after, Zong Ge, Zi Di, and the others formally set off, leaving Snow Bird Harbor behind.

“Finally heading to the royal capital,” Zi Di sighed, a lingering pressure weighing on her heart.

“I wonder how the commander is faring?” The ghost girl couldn't shake her worry for the dragonman youngster either.

“We can't contact him. Any concern is pointless,” Zong Ge said, “What we should focus on now is completing our own tasks.”

“At least, after so many duels with Yue Qian, we've verified that the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell and Deceptive Disguise spell are reliable.”

“Even though I don't know how to use battle skills like Surge or Demolition Fist, Yue Qian was still deceived.”

Zi Di nodded, “This means that even if we face a duel challenge in the capital, we can handle it to some extent.”

“It's just that the consumption of pearl bubbles has become much more intense.”

“We need to use them sparingly.”

Chapter 612: Rescuing Lan Zao

Flower Skirt Island.

The icy sea breeze that swept across this island naturally rose, merging into the clouds above.

Flowers bloom year-round here, creating an eternal spring—a unique oasis amidst the frigid seas.

“Who goes there?!” The patrol fleet intercepted the Bayonet Gang members three nautical miles offshore.

Bing Xiao stepped forward to answer: “We are the Sea Breeze Mercenary Corps...”

After leaving the Divine Wind Pirates, the Bayonet Gang members could not operate under their gang's name. Snow Bird Harbor had undoubtedly issued a wanted notice.

Though not a national level warrant, its impact was significant.

Bing Xiao needed to cover their tracks, buying time for his group.

The patrol fleet comprised only three seagoing vessels. Upon seeing silver level Bing Xiao, Xiong Ju, and Spring Boxer, the captain's expression darkened.

He was the only silver level among the entire fleet.

Though the Bayonet Gang possessed only three landing craft, not a single standard seagoing vessel, they remained a formidable force.

“Present your mercenary credentials!” the captain shouted.

Bing Xiao complied, attaching the certificate to an arrow and shooting it across to the opposing vessel.

The mercenary credentials were, of course, genuine.

This reflected the Bayonet Gang's established standing.

Occasionally, they employed false identities to facilitate their operations.

Of course, this particular certificate was genuine - it belonged to a fictitious Sea Breeze Mercenary Corps, duly registered with the Mercenary Guild.

After verification, the patrol fleet immediately granted passage.

As they neared the dock, Bing Xiao cautioned his companions: "Flower Skirt Island is a neutral territory, a melting pot of all sorts. Once ashore, everyone must be vigilant. Do not stray from the main group. Stay together."

Spring Boxer wore a look of novelty. He rarely ventured out to sea, and this was his first time setting foot on Flower Skirt Island, his first time leaving his country.

Spring Boxer asked, “Boss, what do we do once we're ashore?”

Bing Xiao sighed, “First, tend to their injuries.”

Many members of the Bayonet Gang were wounded.

They had sustained injuries during their escape from Snow Bird Harbor. The sneak attack by the Li Jian Clan leader Mian Li Cang had also inflicted casualties on the Bayonet Gang, adding to the number of wounded. The human ringleader had been injured and fell unconscious as a result.

These casualties had to be prioritized.

Bing Xiao clearly had no intention of abandoning them.

The Bayonet Gang's numbers had already dwindled drastically, leaving only this small group. They were already a candle flickering in the wind. Losing more men would bring them perilously close to complete collapse.

"The Divine Wind was overcrowded with wounded. Their priests can't treat everyone. Flower Skirt Island has many skilled physicians. For a price, we can secure proper care," Bing Xiao stated.

Money wasn't the issue.

Even a broken ship had three nails holding it together, let alone the Bayonet Gang, which had dominated Snow Bird Harbor for years. Besides, they'd brought ample supplies during this escape.

Yet a worry lingered in Bing Xiao's heart.

This worry mirrored Feng Lian's.

He feared a mole.

The mole was likely among the Bayonet Gang members, yet he couldn't identify them.

“If even the demon ringleader has surrendered, subduing ordinary gang members should be even easier for the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.”

"What's more terrifying is that he could swiftly annihilate the entire Bayonet Gang. Such thorough preparation must mean he planted numerous spies beforehand. Such spies are undoubtedly fiercely loyal. If they've infiltrated our ranks, they pose an immense threat!"

Bing Xiao's concern was well-founded.

As they neared Flower Skirt Island, unusual activity began unfolding somewhere on the island.

“Lord Shuang Tu, we've detected Snowflake's life coordinates. He's heading our way and appears to be approaching the island,” a subordinate reported.

Shuang Tu, a snow elf, paused the pounding of herbs upon hearing this, instantly recalling the recent orders from above.

His eyes narrowed slightly. "The Divine Wind Pirates have taken in the remnants of the Bayonet Gang and evaded the Second Royal Navy's ambush. The Bayonet Gang actually made it here? Bring the nautical charts."

The chart was swiftly spread across the table before Shuang Tu.

Shuang Tu traced a slender finger across the map, shifting it slightly.

His brow furrowed as he analyzed aloud: “The Li Jian Clan leader, Mian Li Cang, should have launched the second ambush in this vicinity.”

“Given this distance, Flower Skirt Island is indeed the closest island to them.”

"But I wonder how the second ambush went? Did the Divine Wind Pirates also land on the island?"

Over an hour later, new intelligence arrived.

“Lord, according to reports from the patrol fleet, all three landing craft carried members of the Bayonet Gang—no Divine Wind Pirates!”

Shuang Tu pondered for a moment, then smiled, “It seems Feng Lian abandoned them all.”

“Mian Li Cang must have dealt the Divine Wind Pirates a severe blow. Otherwise, Feng Lian would have at least sent this group to Flower Skirt Island.”

“This is good news.”

“The political pressure on the Li Jian Clan from their defeat will ease considerably. Without the Divine Wind Pirates lurking nearby, our upcoming operations will be much smoother.”

“Make preparations. We're going to retrieve Snowflake.”

"New orders have come down from above. We can develop a new mole within the Bayonet Gang. Hope that unlucky bastard is still alive, heh."

Bing Xiao and the others finally reached shore.

Their arrival caused quite a stir.

A full three silver level individuals, that was considered exceptional strength on Flower Skirt Island.

The island had only one gold level cultivator, who lived in seclusion year-round, detached from worldly affairs. Several other silver level individuals each commanded some influence. Island affairs were typically handled through their collective deliberations.

The island patrol guards and patrol fleet were also formed through contributions of resources and manpower from various factions.

Shuang Tu was one of the silver level leaders among them.

The rendezvous went even smoother than he had anticipated.

Bing Xiao had proactively brought his men to visit him.

The reason was simple: Shuang Tu was the most renowned transcendent healer on Flower Skirt Island. Of course, other transcendent individuals on the island possessed formidable healing abilities as well. But Shuang Tu also ran the largest medical clinic on the island.

With multiple wounded individuals requiring treatment, Shuang Tu's clinic was the most suitable choice.

Shuang Tu personally welcomed Bing Xiao and arranged for the Bayonet Gang's wounded to be settled comfortably within the clinic.

Bing Xiao was generous, offering substantial funds to request Shuang Tu's personal attention.

“Please ensure these two are fully healed,” Bing Xiao hinted to Shuang Tu.

The individuals he prioritized for treatment were the human ringleader and Lan Zao.

The former was a silver level leader of the Bayonet Gang, undoubtedly high-ranking, politically savvy, and capable of coordinating with Bing Xiao.

The latter had saved Bing Xiao's life, possessed unwavering loyalty, and showed promising potential with awakened bloodline abilities.

Shuang Tu immediately pledged full effort in their treatment, though inwardly found it somewhat absurd.

The reason was simple.

The organization member codenamed Snowflake was none other than the human ringleader!

Furthermore, the new target to nurture into a mole was none other than Lan Zao from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!

Chapter 613: Beneath The Glory, Shrouded in Shadow

Flower Skirt Island.

Shuang Tu established his medical clinic here. The clinic occupied a large area, comprising four small buildings and a central garden featuring a fountain.

He rarely attended to patients personally. Under his command are numerous druid apprentices who serve as physicians, pharmacists, and other medical professionals. These individuals form the core of the treatment team.

Among the four buildings, the largest served as the main treatment wing.

Two buildings housed patients. The final structure contained Shuang Tu's private residence, research laboratory, and personal greenhouse.

The silver level human ringleader now lay in a superior hospital room.

As he slowly opened his eyes, Shuang Tu's voice reached him: “You're awake?”

The human ringleader's pupils contracted slightly. Regaining his bearings, he began discreetly surveying his surroundings.

The ward was simply furnished with basic amenities, bathed in bright light. An open window offered a view of the hillside beyond. Bathed in brilliant sunlight, the hillside was carpeted in lush green grass, dotted with countless wildflowers swaying in the breeze, a dazzling tapestry of vibrant colors.

This was indeed an ideal place for recuperation; the natural scenery alone was enough to lift one's spirits.

Shuang Tu stood by the window, leaning against the wall, a smile on his face as he watched the human ringleader.

He introduced himself: “This is Flower Skirt Island. I am Shuang Tu, a snow elf druid.”

The human ringleader looked at Shuang Tu and saw that he indeed bore the features of a snow elf - tall and slender, with striking good looks, and hair and eyebrows as white as snow.

Information about Flower Skirt Island flashed through the human ringleader's mind.

Flower Skirt Island was not large, merely a small island.

Originally an uninhabited wasteland, it had been transformed after a legendary druid settled there.

Even though the legendary druid was long gone, her influence persisted, preserving Flower Skirt Island's unique status.

Flower Skirt Island was a neutral island. Its environment, altered by the legendary druid, remained spring-like year-round. The frigid winds from beyond could not bring the bitter cold characteristic of the iceberg sea. Year-round, the island is covered in lush green grass and blooming flowers, brimming with vibrant life.

The inhabitants of Flower Skirt Island were a diverse mix. There were mercenaries, pirates, and many races, but the largest group was the druids.

The primary reason druids were drawn to settle here was, of course, that legendary druid. She reshaped the natural environment here, which had a profound impact on those seeking druid cultivation.

Rumors also suggest this legendary druid left behind a legacy, hidden somewhere on Flower Skirt Island, awaiting those destined to find it.

The human ringleader looked at Shuang Tu and voiced his confusion: "I should be on a sea vessel. How did I end up on Flower Skirt Island? Lord Shuang Tu, can you enlighten me?"

The human ringleader's mind was filled with confusion, yet he remained composed.

He sensed he had received excellent healing and detected clear goodwill emanating from Shuang Tu.

“Where are my companions?” the human ringleader pressed further. Bing Xiao and Spring Boxer were crucial to his secret mission.

Shuang Tu shrugged slightly, “Actually, Snowflake, I am your companion.”

The human ringleader's heart jolted upon hearing his own codename, yet he feigned confusion, as if he hadn't caught it clearly: “What?”

His expression was perfectly measured, bewildered yet tinged with doubt, utterly unmoved by the word “Snowflake.” He found Shuang Tu's address baffling, while simultaneously questioning whether he'd misheard.

A deeper smile spread across Shuang Tu's face.

Next, he revealed his identity through action.

The human ringleader watched as Shuang Tu extended the back of his right hand toward him.

Gradually, a tattoo emerged on the once-smooth skin.

It was a black sun. Within the sun, a dagger's outline was traced in white lines.

The human ringleader froze for a moment, the bewilderment and confusion on his face quickly vanishing, replaced by solemn seriousness.

He stared intently at Shuang Tu, then slowly extended the back of his hand.

A matching tattoo gradually emerged on his own hand.

In a deep voice, the human ringleader declared, “Beneath the glory.”

Shuang Tu immediately responded, completing the phrase: “Shrouded in shadow.”

The human ringleader exhaled a faint breath.

Shuang Tu continued, “Snowflake, my codename is Physician. I'm in charge of the base here on Flower Skirt Island.”

"Flower Skirt Island is one of the most renowned neutral islands in the seas surrounding the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Every year, numerous mercenaries, pirates, and others come here for medical treatment."

“The organization assigned me to this location.”

“Not long ago, I received orders from above to stand ready to assist you.”

"Unexpectedly, early this morning, we detected your life signals. It was Bing Xiao who brought you here for treatment."

The human ringleader relaxed.

The tattoos served as their organization's insignia and also functioned as a powerful means of identity verification.

This was a remarkable feat of human alchemy, standing at the forefront of the world's alchemy advancements.

The tattoos could indicate identity, enable short-range covert communication, and transmit vital signs over long distances.

It was precisely this latter capability that allowed the Li Jian Clan to pinpoint the Divine Wind Pirates' exact coordinates, enabling them to orchestrate two ambushes.

No matter how thoroughly Feng Lian and his men investigated, they found no traces of alchemy within the human ringleader's body.

Thus, the greatest advantage of this alchemy lay in its concealment!

At least, the human ringleader had never encountered anyone who could expose his identity through his tattoo.

The reason the human ringleader trusted Shuang Tu so quickly was also due to the secret training he had received.

In critical moments, during unseen operatives, one had to instantly gain an ally's trust to relay intelligence.

With matching tattoos and codes verified, the human ringleader had no choice but to trust the figure before him.

After confirming his identity, Shuang Tu immediately asked, “What happened after you boarded the Divine Wind pirate ship?”

The human ringleader gave him a concise account of the events that followed: "Two ambushes. The cover was blown, and the decoy was executed."

"During the second ambush, I was wounded and lost consciousness. When I woke up, I found myself here with you."

This answer caught Shuang Tu off guard: “I thought you'd feigned injury and unconsciousness to clear yourself of suspicion.”

The human ringleader offered a wry smile, “This time was perilous. I nearly died during the ambush by Mian Li Cang.”

Shuang Tu offered comfort. "But misfortune brought you fortune. When delivering your group of wounded for treatment, Bing Xiao personally instructed me to prioritize two individuals, one of whom was you."

“You've earned Bing Xiao's trust. Keep it up, and you might uncover Tranquil Hill's exact location.”

The human ringleader shook his head, his tone pessimistic: "If it were that easy, the organization would've found Tranquil Hill long ago. The reason Bing Xiao singled me out is because, within the current Bayonet Gang, I alone truly understand his intentions and can best assist him in rebuilding the organization."

“This kind of assistance is something neither Xiong Ju nor Spring Boxer can provide him.”

The human ringleader possessed a crystal-clear understanding of his position and role.

He confided in Shuang Tu: "Truthfully, I have little confidence in the upcoming operation. I believe there's a high probability Bing Xiao won't take me to Tranquil Hill. To accomplish this mission, I alone won't suffice. I need more reinforcements!"

Shuang Tu nodded slightly, “We already have one reinforcement.”

A flicker of joy crossed the human ringleader's face. "Oh? Who is it? What's their strength?"

But the next moment, his brow furrowed as he analyzed gloomily, “Given the current situation, it's difficult to let anyone else infiltrate the group.”

Shuang Tu smiled as he relayed the information about Lan Zao.

The human ringleader's face darkened like storm clouds. His voice dropped with anger as he snapped, “Are you joking?”

“You expect this newcomer, not even a full member of the organization, to be my reinforcement?”

“This kind of backup will get me killed!”

"Not only will you nearly get me killed, but you will also cause the mission to fail. Who's going to take responsibility for that?"

Shuang Tu sighed. "Didn't you just say it yourself? Given the current situation, we can't deploy any more members. The Bayonet Gang is down to just these few members now. Their numbers are too thin, we can't take advantage of the chaos."

“Calm down, okay?”

“I think having someone is better than having no one at all.”

"The person covering you is already dead, right? Using this new recruit as your cover is just recycling waste."

“Besides, this isn't a discussion, it's an order!”

“I have two video clips you need to review.”

The magic footage Shuang Tu first showed the human ringleader depicted the Bayonet Gang's escape from Snow Bird Harbor's dock.

The focal point, naturally, was Lan Zao.

His consecutive blunders that unexpectedly earned him Bing Xiao's trust were replayed before the human ringleader.

“He's an idiot, and a complete unlucky bastard,” the human ringleader fumed after watching, speechless, “Hard to believe such a man is my reinforcement!”

“I'd call him a lucky bastard!” Shuang Tu shrugged, “Did you know his bloodline has awakened?”

The human ringleader nodded. He was aware of that fact.

Shuang Tu continued, "I've already examined him. His bloodline awakening is progressing exceptionally well. The bloodline concentration is high, and the characteristics are strikingly pronounced."

“He appears to possess the Fish Bladder bloodline. With cultivation, he could reach the silver level.”

The human ringleader froze.

Learning that Lan Zao might attain the same cultivation level as himself caused a subtle shift in his perception of this bronze level individual.

“Seems his luck is indeed favorable... provided he survives until the end.”

Shuang Tu then showed him a second magic projection.

The magic projection showed three figures: the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, Long Fu, and Magician Yao Ma.

They had signed a contract and issued orders to their subordinates.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's directive was clear: Lan Zao was an unexpected variable. Develop him. Use him. Complete the mission. Ensure his safety whenever possible, provided it didn't conflict with the mission's objectives.

“It seems the leader has other plans for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!” After pondering for a moment, the human ringleader looked up at Shuang Tu again. "Have you uncovered any intelligence on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps? Who exactly is the arms dealer behind them?"

Shuang Tu shook his head slightly. "We've been investigating. Without a doubt, the arms dealer behind them is deeply concealed. We haven't even discovered their communication methods!"

"The timing of that Magician Yao Ma's appearance was highly suspicious. She suddenly materialized aboard the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' ship. Her appearance was too timely, proof that the arms dealer behind them possesses real-time intelligence on the mercenaries' situation."

“This lead holds no value,” the human ringleader shook his head, “Our analysis suggests that Magician Yao Ma likely departed with the group all along, merely concealing her presence.”

“If I were the arms dealer, it would be perfectly logical and normal to conceal one or two trump cards like this Magician Yao Ma among the vanguard sent to develop the market!”

At this point, the human ringleader sighed: “...”

“I won't make direct contact with that individual.”

“I must continue concealing my identity.”

“I'll need you to step in and develop him into my cover.”

"And throughout this process, I must observe him covertly. I need to see more of this partner's actions, understand his character and habits."

“Of course.” Shuang Tu agreed without hesitation. "Based on my observations, the awakening of his bloodline has stabilized and is nearing its end. It won't be long before he awakens."

Chapter 614: Brave Hero and Tranquil Hill

Just as Shuang Tu had anticipated, Lan Zao regained consciousness that very afternoon.

Lan Zao was also placed in a private ward, which made it convenient for Shuang Tu to have a private conversation with him later.

“Where am I? And who are you?" Lan Zao asked upon waking.

Shuang Tu introduced himself: "This is Flower Skirt Island. I am Shuang Tu, the island's physician, a snow elf druid, as you can see. You were brought here for treatment by Bing Xiao's group."

Hearing this, Lan Zao felt a wave of disappointment washing over him.

When he had regained consciousness in this unfamiliar place, a faint glimmer of hope had flickered within him.

He had hoped that during his unconsciousness, some unexpected event had occurred, allowing him to escape the perilous situation aboard the Divine Wind.

But he was disappointed.

“Now I have more important matters to discuss with you,” Shuang Tu said, his expression turning serious.

Lan Zao stared at him.

Shuang Tu continued, "First, please rest assured and try to relax. A magic formation has been activated in this room. Any conversation we have from here on out will be completely confidential."

“Second, what I'm about to say may be emotionally jarring. Please don't get worked up, stay calm.”

He paused here, giving Lan Zao time to process, before continuing, “I know you're part of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

Clang!

The next instant, Lan Zao leaped violently from the bed, arms outstretched, lunging toward Shuang Tu.

Several deep green vines suddenly sprouted, snatching him mid-air and binding him tightly.

Lan Zao was tightly bound, suspended in mid-air, unable to move.

He struggled desperately but couldn't break free from the vines. He glared fiercely at Shuang Tu, his eyes burning with murderous intent.

Shuang Tu's expression darkened, the earlier smile vanishing.

In the adjacent hospital room, the human ringleader witnessed this scene through the magic projection and couldn't help but sigh.

Lan Zao's impulsiveness deeply disappointed him.

Then he heard Lan Zao speak: "I've never heard of any Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps! Don't think you can use me! Bing Xiao! Lord Bing Xiao!"

Lan Zao cried out for help.

This action caused the human ringleader to lower his head, covering his face with his palm.

Lan Zao was being foolish. Denying it now was pointless; his earlier extreme actions had already spoken volumes.

His attempt to summon Bing Xiao revealed his desperate attempt to rectify his mistake, his bid to break the deadlock through external forces.

Clearly, it was all self-defeating!

Shuang Tu manipulated the vines with his mind, slowly pulling Lan Zao toward him.

His face was clouded with worry as he sighed, “I already told you, this room is sealed by a magic formation. It's completely isolated and hidden. No matter how loud you scream, no one outside can hear you.”

“Calm down. I'm on your side.”

“Hah! Don't even think about using me!” Lan Zao, immobilized and choked red by the vines, struggled desperately.

Suddenly, he snapped his jaws shut and bit through his own tongue!

He intended to bite his tongue off and kill himself.

This scene stunned both Shuang Tu and the human ringleader.

Neither had anticipated Lan Zao's ruthless resolve!

They had underestimated Lan Zao's loyalty to the dragonman youngster. According to the intelligence they had obtained, in their view: Lan Zao was merely a mercenary recruited by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps on Snake Rat Island. Having served under the dragonman youngster for only a short time, it was a series of unfortunate coincidences that led to his misunderstanding by Bing Xiao and subsequent boarding of the Divine Wind.

“This guy's real ruthless!” The human ringleader suddenly recalled the magic footage he'd seen earlier. Lan Zao had indeed fought with extraordinary bravery and utter disregard for his own life during the Bayonet Gang's escape battle.

The human ringleader was unaware of Lan Zao's state of mind at the time.

Lan Zao had always believed he had consumed a fatal potion, that his days were numbered and his body was collapsing. He wanted to contribute as much as possible before dying.

That was his mindset then, and it remained unchanged upon waking.

Thus, he lunged at Shuang Tu without hesitation, thinking to take down one more enemy if possible.

Now, biting his tongue to end his life, he showed no hesitation either. He feared some mysterious magic means might force him to reveal secrets like Mystifying Monster Island, thereby endangering the dragonman youngster.

Shuang Tu watched Lan Zao spewing blood from his mouth and hastily cast healing spells upon him while showing him a magic projection.

The magic projection showed the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, Zong Ge disguised as Long Fu, and Zi Di posing as Magician Yao Ma, signing a secret magic pact.

The pact's core concern was Lan Zao.

At the projection's end, Zong Ge, as Long Fu, gazed at Lan Zao through the image and left a message: "Don't give up hope. Hold on. Trust me. When the time is right, I'll come for you."

“Commander...” Lan Zao murmured inwardly, his eyes quickly reddening. He had no doubt about the “Long Fu's” promise. For he knew the dragonman youngster was precisely such an outstanding, reliable, and admirable leader!

Lan Zao failed to recognize that the "Long Fu" in the magic image was actually Zong Ge in disguise.

Zong Ge had assumed the role of the dragonman youngster after Lan Zao was forcibly taken aboard the Divine Wind and carried away.

He had once again employed the Deceptive Disguise spell to alter his appearance. This was a fact unknown to Lan Zao, so he could not discern Zong Ge's true form.

But Lan Zao could recognize the figure of Magician Yao Ma in the magic image.

In his eyes, Yao Ma appeared as Zi Di.

He knew the secret that Zi Di was impersonating Yao Ma. Thus, this Deceptive Disguise spell on Zi Di was ineffective against Lan Zao.

Such is the peculiar nature of the Deceptive Disguise spell.

Zi Di also left words for Lan Zao: "Staying alive is what matters most. Don't dwell on it too much. Everyone is waiting for your return."

Her words held profound meaning, though Lan Zao failed to grasp it.

After confirming the magic image's authenticity, Lan Zao suddenly calmed, his entire body relaxing.

“Then who are you?” Lan Zao stared at Shuang Tu and demanded.

The human ringleader who had been spying on this exchange frowned, letting out another sigh.

Lan Zao had let down his guard and trusted this stranger, leaving the human ringleader deeply disappointed.

“He's far too gullible!”

“Didn't he consider that this magic image could be forged?”

“How can someone like this cooperate with me during future undercover missions?”

“How can I possibly dare to work with someone like that?”

Shuang Tu's estimation of Lan Zao dropped another notch.

Neither realized Lan Zao had already verified the image's authenticity through the Deceptive Disguise spell's properties.

Shuang Tu showed Lan Zao a second magic image.

This was the same one the human ringleader had viewed earlier that morning.

It captured the Bayonet Gang's desperate battle as they fled Snow Bird Harbor's docks. The footage had been edited to focus solely on one protagonist, Lan Zao himself.

Upon seeing this, Lan Zao suddenly recognized Shuang Tu’s identity.

He had long known that the mage tower at Snow Bird Harbor’s docks possessed the ability to record history.

He deduced, “So you’re one of Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s men.”

Shuang Tu’s face softened into a faint smile as he nodded, “You’ve guessed correctly.”

"So I can go back now? When can I return?" Lan Zao grew excited.

Shuang Tu shook his head slightly, then nodded. "You've already seen the first magic image. You're also aware of the magic contract's signing."

“Once you complete your mission, you may return.”

“Not only that, but you'll receive a generous reward!”

"Though your acceptance by Bing Xiao was a tremendous surprise, it turned out to be a fortunate mistake, giving us a glimmer of hope."

“To put it plainly, the Bayonet Gang members were deliberately released by the city lord.”

Lan Zao was utterly astonished, “Why would the city lord do such a thing?”

“Because of Tranquil Hill.”

“Tranquil Hill?” Lan Zao furrowed his brow, utterly perplexed, “What in the world is that?”

At this point, the human ringleader could no longer bear to watch.

He rolled his eyes, flopping back onto his sickbed, utterly drained.

Lan Zao hailed from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, an organization of pure outsiders.

Thus, he knew nothing of place names like Tranquil Hill.

Shuang Tu proved far more patient than the human ringleader. He briefly recounted the legend of Tranquil Hill to Lan Zao.

It happened during the Barbarian and Dragon Era.

The pact between the barbarians and the dragons had shattered.

On the Frost Continent, two leaders emerged.

One was the hero Man Ying, who led the barbarians. The other was the warrior Xue Yong, who commanded the snow elves.

Man Ying and Xue Yong were the two strongest beings on the Frost Continent. Both harbored the grand vision of unifying the world and bringing peace to the land.

Through their encounters and clashes, they recognized shared ideals and aspirations, gradually developing mutual respect and forging a deep bond.

The cunning Black Dragon King, unwilling to see such a powerful alliance, struck first. Using deceit and trickery, he orchestrated a series of misunderstandings, successfully driving a wedge between the two races.

As the barbarians and snow elves stood on the brink of all-out war, Man Ying and Xue Yong found themselves compelled to fight with all their might, determined to win the weapon for the person they most admired in this world.

Man Ying, wishing to avoid a large-scale conflict, proposed a personal duel to Xue Yong.

Xue Yong, equally reluctant to see his people shed excessive blood, accepted the challenge. They agreed that the outcome of their duel would determine the fate of their respective tribes.

The barbarians urged Man Ying to gather their bravest warriors in advance and launch a surprise attack on the snow elves, striking first.

Man Ying refused, rebuking them: “That dishonors our fighters!”

The snow elves proposed a scheme to Xue Yong: to poison and assassinate Man Ying. Snow Yong rejected it: “That tramples upon our friendship.”

On the day of the duel, both Man Ying and Xue Yong brought an ice coffin.

Man Ying said to Xue Yong: "Though I must fight you, my will is as transparent and pure as this ice. My dear friend, I vow to fight this battle without resorting to any underhanded tactics."

Xue Yong also declared solemnly: "My friend, our friendship is as unbreakable as this ice coffin. Yet our people must contend, and the Frost Continent can have but one royal lineage. I pledge to you: no schemes, no poison. I will fight with all my strength and accept whatever outcome befalls us."

The two further agreed: should either fall in battle, the survivor would seal the fallen's remains within an ice coffin and carry them to Tranquil Hill for burial.

A breathtaking duel commenced.

They fought for three days and three nights, both perishing in the clash.

Countless black dragons descended like a storm, slaughtering all in their path.

The Black Dragon King seized the opportunity to slaughter the barbarians and snow elves. With Man Ying and Xue Yong dead, no one remained on the Frost Continent to challenge the Black Dragon King.

The Black Dragon King placed their bodies in two ice coffins, displaying them as trophies in his cavern to flaunt his brutal conquests.

Raging ice and snow from the Frost Continent surged into his lair, carrying the two ice coffins away.

The coffins slid swiftly down the snowy mountains, bouncing over endless glaciers and snowdrifts before finally landing in a valley.

Not a hint of cold wind touched this valley, where spring lingered year-round, adorned with blooming flowers and lush foliage. A rainbow of colors arched high in the sky, while diamond-like springs tinkled softly.

This was Tranquil Hill.

Here, the two heroes found their final resting place, yet they could not rest in peace. They awoke simultaneously.

Though Tranquil Hill offered a serene environment, it could not grant them eternal slumber.

For their kin were being slaughtered by the black dragons. Peace hung precariously over the Frost Continent, blood staining nearly every patch of snow-white land crimson.

Returning from Tranquil Hill, the two led their respective people to unite and slay the Black Dragon King, nearly exterminating the entire black dragon race.

Only two black dragons escaped by sheer luck.

After finishing the mythical tale of Tranquil Hill, Shuang Tu explained to Lan Zao, “This is the legend of Tranquil Hill, and also the origin of the custom of the heroic duel.”

Lan Zao realized, "No wonder the challenger in the heroic duel carries ice. Does ‘heroic’¹ refer to Man Ying and Xue Yong?"

"Does Tranquil Hill truly exist in this world? Isn't this just a mythical tale?"

Shuang Tu smiled: “This isn't mere hearsay.”

“According to the intelligence we've gathered, Spring Boxer is Teng Dong Lang's coffin bearer.”

Chapter 615: I, Lan Zao, am a Reasonable Person

Lan Zao froze upon hearing Shuang Tu's words, then furrowed his brow.

He had seen Teng Dong Lang's corpse in the black coffin with his own eyes. He knew full well that Teng Dong Lang's body was sealed within an ice coffin.

Now, upon hearing the mythical tale of Tranquil Hill's heroic duel, Lan Zao immediately connected the two events.

“I understand!”

“Why would the Bayonet Gang, fleeing for their lives, still carry Teng Dong Lang's corpse back?”

“Spring Boxer was designated as Teng Dong Lang's coffin bearer. This means they intend to carry his ice coffin to Tranquil Hill for burial. Is it to resurrect him there?”

Such suspicions inevitably arose in Lan Zao's mind.

For him, this was something he must do everything in his power to disrupt.

Because the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had been deeply involved in the battle that destroyed the Bayonet Gang, the enmity between the two sides ran deep as the sea. They were utterly mortal enemies.

"Oh, I see!"

“Why did the commander enter into this secret collaboration with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord?”

"The commander has always deeply loved President Zi Di, but she is dead. If Tranquil Hill truly possesses the power to resurrect the dead, it might be Zi Di's most promising path back to life."

Resurrecting Zi Di would be exceedingly difficult.

No cleric would willingly resurrect an undead soul.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would most likely not pursue this route.

Two primary reasons accounted for this reluctance.

First, those who mastered resurrection magic were typically deeply devout individuals unlikely to betray their faith.

Second, the risk of identity exposure. Though the Deceptive Disguise spell was highly reliable, especially when combined with the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, the survivors lack confidence in deceiving the gods.

Resurrection was an unquestionably high level divine spell. Every use of divine magic risked drawing a deity's attention. The higher the spell's level, the greater this probability.

Zi Di understood the difficulty of resurrecting herself, and the other survivors were equally aware.

Lan Zao was no exception.

However, the truth was not so straightforward.

The mission to infiltrate Tranquil Hill and pinpoint its exact location was highly classified. The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had not informed Zong Ge or Zi Di of this aspect.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps agreed to cooperate with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord and sign a magic contract for one primary purpose: to leverage the city lord's manpower and resources to protect Lan Zao's life as much as possible.

Lan Zao believed he understood.

Though he harbored suspicions about Zi Di, he remained utterly loyal to the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster deeply loved Zi Di and was willing to participate in this endeavor. Lan Zao, in turn, was willing to ease the dragonman youngster's burdens.

This misunderstanding prompted Lan Zao to agree without hesitation: “I understand.”

“Next, I'll remain by Bing Xiao's side and follow them into Tranquil Hill, correct?”

Lan Zao's decisive agreement surprised Shuang Tu.

The human ringleader who was next door, however, harbored doubts: “Does this fellow truly possess the capacity for rational thought?” Had it been him, he would have demanded more proof from Shuang Tu or secured greater assurances.

Shuang Tu's expression betrayed no hint of doubt. He regarded Lan Zao with an admiring gaze and praised, “Congratulations! You've made an absolutely wise choice!”

Before his words had fully faded, he mentally directed the green vines to gently lower Lan Zao back onto the hospital bed.

The vines swiftly retracted.

Shuang Tu approached Lan Zao's bedside and handed him a document.

“This contains your new identity. You must memorize every detail.”

Lan Zao gave him an awkward smile, “I can't read it.”

Shuang Tu nodded, accepting this fact quickly.

Not everyone could recognize written characters.

It was perfectly normal.

Lan Zao added, “I only know my own name and can write it, but I only know human words, not your snow elf writing.”

In this world, the vast majority of people were illiterate, unable even to write their own names. When names were needed, they typically pressed their fingerprints instead.

Shuang Tu then read the contents of the materials to Lan Zao: “I'll read this once. You must remember it well.”

"Your name is Hen Fa. You are not a member of the Bayonet Gang. You committed a crime in Blood Kin City and fled all the way to Snow Bird Harbor, seeking an opportunity to join the Bayonet Gang for protection."

"On the eve of your escape with the Bayonet Gang, transcendents from Blood Kin City tracked you down and attempted to capture you."

"You broke into Snow Bird Harbor's docks, hoping to escape amid the chaos. In the process, you unexpectedly saved Bing Xiao's life. Afterward, you intended to conceal your identity further, using the Divine Wind Pirates to leave Snow Bird Harbor and start a new life."

Hearing this, Lan Zao's brow furrowed even deeper.

He sat up, fixing his gaze on Shuang Tu. "Couldn't you just assign me the identity of a Bayonet Gang member? Isn't this outsider identity too troublesome? If Bing Xiao discovers my background, he'll struggle to trust me. How can I remain by his side in the future?"

Shuang Tu shook his head, “This identity was carefully selected for you after thorough consideration.”

“It's the optimal choice for your situation.”

Considering Lan Zao was a complete novice, Shuang Tu patiently explained: We certainly have the capability to design a Bayonet Gang identity for you, but you wouldn't be able to pull it off."

“The Bayonet Gang members know each other well. Even if none of Bing Xiao's current crew recognize you, they'll investigate.”

"Snow Bird Harbor still has many former Bayonet Gang members. All of them have surrendered, and the city lord would never kill them."

"So, if Bing Xiao decides to verify your background, it'll be easy, just send someone to Snow Bird Harbor. That way, they'll quickly uncover your flaws."

"You've already gained Bing Xiao's initial trust. According to our intel, he intends to cultivate you and entrust you with important responsibilities. The main reason is the awakening of your bloodline."

"I must congratulate you on this, you've awakened the Fish Bladder bloodline. This is a silver level bloodline, allowing you to leapfrog bronze level and iron level directly to silver."

At this, Lan Zao gasped, his entire focus instantly drawn to the revelation.

"Fish Bladder bloodline? Silver level?!"

“So does this mean the magic potion I took earlier took effect?”

“Did I misjudge Zi Di, or was I just incredibly lucky?”

The news of bloodline awakening was too overwhelming, leaving Lan Zao momentarily dazed.

Seeing his blank expression, Shuang Tu paused his speech: “Hey, did you hear what I just said?”

“Huh? What?” Lan Zao's mouth opened slightly, his train of thought interrupted.

Shuang Tu had no choice but to repeat herself: “I've already explained your current situation clearly.”

“Bloodline awakening has granted you great potential. Combined with your previous rescue of Bing Xiao, you've earned his initial trust.”

"Most crucially, we're backing you and providing reinforcements. In the coming days, your strength will rise steadily, and your standing within the Bayonet Gang will climb step by step."
