"With each rung you ascend, the scrutiny you face intensifies. Bing Xiao will undoubtedly step up his investigation into you. As the former leader of the Bayonet Gang, he possesses extensive leadership experience and will never overlook this critical detail."

"Therefore, your identity must be flawless. Though the identity of Hen Fa is slightly troublesome, it is actually the most convenient, worry-free, and effortless option."

"There is indeed a fugitive in Blood Kin City named Hen Fa. However, he was secretly apprehended by us and is now dead."

"He was an orphan from a young age. Growing up, he became a bodyguard, a solitary figure with a straightforward identity and minimal social connections."

“Hen Fa's background file is the simplest and most concise of all options. You must commit it to memory, this is your first step in infiltration.”

“Understood.” Lan Zao took the documents, then frowned and asked, "How long will my infiltration last? I want to return to the mercenary corps."

“Such impatience is unwise,” Shuang Tu frowned, “But I can tell you this: the infiltration won't last long.”

"Teng Dong Lang's soul won't hold out much longer. Bing Xiao must deliver him to Tranquil Hill swiftly."

Lan Zao was puzzled. "Teng Dong Lang died days ago at my commander's hands. Has his soul still not departed?"

Typically, a deceased soul was guided away by the deity they worshipped in life.

If they lacked faith or their devotion was shallow, the soul would be cast into the abyssal hell for another cycle of reincarnation

Since Lan Zao already knew about the Tranquil Hill's mission details, Shuang Tu saw no point in withholding further information and continued his explanation: “In fact, this is precisely one of the leads we uncovered regarding Tranquil Hill.”

"Before his death, Teng Dong Lang had made preparations and modified his body. Even if he perishes in battle, his soul will remain temporarily bound within his physical form, it won't dissipate nor be guided away."

“This state cannot last forever.”

“Based on our estimates, his time is nearly up. That's why he must be brought to Tranquil Hill.”

“So that's it. I understand now.” Lan Zao realized.

Shuang Tu continued his guidance: "Your illiteracy is actually an advantage, since Hen Fa also cannot read. But I advise you to learn the elven language diligently while serving Bing Xiao. For often, intelligence transmission relies on written words as tools."

“You must engrave every detail in this document deeply into your mind.”

Should Bing Xiao ever suspect you, these details might help clear your name temporarily, or even save your life."

Shuang Tu spoke with solemn earnestness.

When his own survival was at stake, Lan Zao's expression turned grave and determined. He nodded firmly, “I understand!”

Chapter 616: Information Transmitted

Shuang Tu left Lan Zao's hospital room and approached the human ringleader.

The two stood in silence for a moment.

Having interacted with Lan Zao, Shuang Tu now deeply understood the immense burden resting on the human ringleader's shoulders.

He sighed and offered reassurance: “Look on the bright side, your identity hasn't been exposed yet.”

"That boatswain from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is highly capable and ruthless toward himself. Though... he is indeed a bit simple-minded. But looking at it another way, that very trait makes him easier to manipulate, wouldn't you agree?"

“You make a valid point,” the human ringleader replied weakly, “For now, that's the only comfort I can offer myself.”

“Continue recuperating. I'll report the situation here first.” Shuang Tu took his leave.

The human ringleader's injuries remained severe. Had it not been for Bing Xiao's timely treatment, he likely would have bled to death.

Now at Shuang Tu's clinic, he received proper care, though his abdomen remained tightly bound in layers of bandages.

Druidic healing often took time to take effect. By comparison, clerical healing spells were far more direct and brutal, capable of restoring a wounded person to health in mere seconds.

However, this did not mean clerical healing was vastly superior to druidic methods.

Both approaches had their distinct advantages and disadvantages.

Divine healing drained the patient's life force; the more treatments administered, the greater the reduction in the patient's lifespan.

Druidic healing, while slower to take effect, was gentle and did not diminish the patient's lifespan.

Of course, druids were not without rapid healing methods, but they simply lacked the speed of divine healing.

Precisely for this reason, members of the clergy were the preferred healers on the battlefield.

Commanders also favor divine healing.

Its swift efficacy allowed wounded soldiers to be restored to combat readiness in short order, preserving the army's fighting strength.

Though it drained life force, without rapid healing, fallen soldiers risked being finished off by enemies, losing their lives entirely.

Weighing two evils, sacrificing some future lifespan to guarantee survival on the battlefield was the lesser choice.

Shuang Tu's adoption of this healing method was also calculated.

He needed to buy more time for the human ringleader and Lan Zao.

During their recovery period, the human ringleader and Lan Zao, especially Lan Zao, would have more opportunity to adjust and adapt to their new identities.

Shuang Tu swiftly relayed the situation here.

This intelligence, rigorously encrypted, first reached the relay alchemy device.

These relay devices were typically installed on uninhabited islands, some even concealed at fixed points on the ocean floor.

After all, a considerable distance separated Flower Skirt Island and Snow Bird Harbor.

Following several relay points, Snow Bird Harbor swiftly received the intelligence.

Professionals swiftly decrypted it, restoring the original content.

Soon, the intelligence was delivered to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord’s desk.

He was handling administrative affairs.

Upon seeing the urgent intelligence, a faint smile appeared on his face.

“Oh, interesting.”

"Who'd have thought that unlucky bastard actually survived. That backup plan actually worked."

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had been rather pessimistic about the mission to spy on Tranquil Hill.

Previous attempts had all ended in failure.

This time, he saw an opportunity in Teng Dong Lang's death. He deliberately let the Bayonet Gang leader go to create this chance, though he himself didn't hold much hope.

Despite this mindset, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would still exert maximum effort.

Thus, upon discovering Lan Zao's existence, he set his sights on the latter.

Subsequently, he successfully forged a secret pact with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, hoping Lan Zao could assist with this mission.

Beyond that, he also sought to use this step to deepen his covert ties with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. He was even more interested in the arms dealer behind Long Fu.

Clutching this intelligence, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord fell into deep thought: “Long Fu has already left Snow Bird Harbor. Judging by the time elapsed, they should now be beyond the maximum communication range from my location. This intelligence cannot be delivered directly into their hands.”

After pondering for a moment, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord summoned one of his subordinates and ordered him to deliver this intelligence to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' detachment.

Shortly thereafter, Tripleblade received the intelligence.

This goblin was the core figure and true decision-maker within the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ sub-unit.

On the surface, the sub-unit established in Snow Bird Harbor was led by Men Shi as the chief and Chi Lai as the deputy chief.

After discussion between Zong Ge and Zi Di, they decided to revise the original plan in the absence of the dragonman youngster. They would lead the expedition to the Ice Sculpture Capital, leaving Men Shi, Chi Lai, and others behind in Snow Bird Harbor.

Joining them were Tripleblade, Mu Ban, the big guy, and others.

Men Shi and Chi Lai would primarily handle daily mercenary assignments and training.

Their main training targets were the Quick Knife Squad and Backwater Battle Squad.

Both squads remained stationed in Snow Bird Harbor.

The reason for leaving them behind was simple: both squads possessed limited combat strength and offered little assistance to Zi Di and Zong Ge. If they encountered real trouble, they would only become cannon fodder, ultimately sacrificing themselves in vain.

Both Chi Lai and Men Shi were trustworthy.

Chi Lai signed a contract with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. The terms of the contract guaranteed his loyalty. Moreover, Chi Lai's background and character naturally inspire trust.

As for Men Shi, there's no question.

He joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to repay a debt of gratitude. This dwarf possesses an exceptionally strong sense of duty. He risked everything to rescue his clan from the Two Eyes Island auction, fighting tirelessly for them even amidst the chaos.

Simultaneously, Men Shi embodies profound self-sacrifice.

He once shielded his comrades from a bomb's blast with his own body. During the Bayonet Gang's escape, he recklessly attempted to block the swamp oil amalgamation, despite the slim odds of survival.

Of course, the survivors remained the true core. The trust earned by Chi Lai and Men Shi fell far short of granting them access to core secrets like Mystifying Monster Island.

Therefore, Tripleblade had to be left behind.

With the dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, Zi Di, Cang Xu, and Lan Zao all gone, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' fleet needed a true insider to watch over it.

On one hand, many confidential matters had to be kept from Chi Lai and Men Shi; they couldn't be allowed access.

On the other hand, if an accident occurred, only a true insider could make the most reasonable choice, one that truly served the survivors' interests.

However, Tripleblade's personal combat strength remained weak.

He was only iron level.

But there was no choice. The current situation demanded he fill such a crucial position.

"Lan Zao isn't dead? I'm sure the commander will be delighted to hear this news.” Receiving this intelligence, Tripleblade sighed inwardly. “I just wonder when the commander will be able to return."

Tripleblade was privy to the truth, he knew the dragonman youngster had vanished while pursuing Lan Zao, and that Zong Ge was impersonating him to infiltrate the royal capital.

Tripleblade immediately set about relaying this intelligence.

Yet the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were outsiders; their communication capabilities paled in comparison to those of Snow Bird Harbor's city lord.

Typically, long-distance message delivery relied on alchemy devices like the magic messaging unit.

But such devices consumed vast amounts of energy, required costly maintenance, and were prone to losing intelligence, or even having it intercepted mid-transmission.

They offered no guarantee of secrecy.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's intelligence network employed superior communication devices with greater stealth capabilities, but the investment required was enormous.

Even with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' current finances, such extensive deployment costs were difficult to bear.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had no intention of doing so either, it was both too wasteful of resources and time-consuming.

Moreover, they lacked trained communication beasts. Tripleblade reasoned that since the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had sent the intelligence and it wasn't urgent; there was no need to use the city lord's communication routes to contact Zong Ge and Zi Di.

Therefore, Tripleblade opted for a more down-to-earth method of contact. He directly dispatched a subordinate, entrusting him with the encrypted intelligence to travel via teleportation formation to the next town.

Teleportation formations existed in every major city of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Using these formations allowed swift travel to destinations.

However, the range was limited, direct travel wasn't possible, requiring at least five relay points.

Moreover, the cost was exorbitant. The higher the item level and the greater the quantity being transported, the steeper the price.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps desperately wanted to use the teleportation formations to “jump” city by city to reach the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. But the financial burden was too heavy.

There was also a more crucial reason.

That was the limited number of teleportation slots.

The teleportation formation's capacity was limited. Every town had countless matters to attend to, with numerous people and goods urgently needing to use the formation, yet they had to queue up.

For the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to utilize the teleportation formation, they not only had to incur substantial costs but also face long waits. In Snow Bird Harbor, they could cut in line through connections with the city lord, but this wasn't possible elsewhere.

All things considered, using the teleportation formation was less efficient than simply traveling on foot.

“If Lord Zong Ge's route hasn't changed since departure, their first stop should be Blood Kin City.”

After calculating, Tripleblade estimated that Zong Ge and his group hadn't yet reached Blood Kin City.

He issued precise orders for the messenger to teleport to Blood Kin City and await Zong Ge and Zi Di's party in advance.

Cold Wind Valley.

Zi Di, Zong Ge, and their group trudged through the terrain.

This valley wasn't large. Though not a major thoroughfare, numerous caravans passed through annually.

Thus, no high level magic beasts roamed here, and the low level ones were no match for Zong Ge and Zi Di. The more alert low level beasts fled at the sight of the group. The less intelligent ones had already become game for Zong Ge and his companions, or alchemy ingredients.

Xu Ma reported: "Commander, beyond the narrow pass ahead lies the exit from Cold Wind Valley. The route is clear, not a single low level beast remains."

As a thief, he had been assigned to the royal capital's escort to scout the path.

Yet Zi Di whispered telepathically to Zong Ge: “Ambushes lie in wait on both sides of the pass!”

Chapter 617: Zong and Zi Disagree

Xu Ma's rank was simply too low.

He was only bronze level.

This was the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, though not on par with the Sheng Ming Empire, it far surpassed Snake Rat Island and Two Eyes Island.

Nearly every major town within the kingdom had gold level guards stationed, and crucial trade ports like Snow Bird Harbor had more than one gold level figure at the pinnacle of power.

Cold Wind Valley was considered wilderness territory, but those who operated there were naturally formidable.

Bronze level was the lowest possible transcendent.

A bronze level like Xu Ma might hold minor status on Snake Rat Island or serve some purpose on Two Eyes Island, but in the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, he was utterly insignificant.

The same applied to Tripleblade.

Tripleblade was iron level. At this level, he held a fairly solid position on Snake Rat Island.

But with the addition of Chi Lai and Men Shi, Tripleblade's importance diminished significantly.

Now in Snow Bird Harbor, someone of his rank simply couldn't become the overt leader of a mercenary company's sub-unit. He had to retreat into the background, ceding the positions of leader and deputy leader to Men Shi and Chi Lai.

Silver level individuals like the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge, however, fit quite well.

Should they advance further to gold level, it would greatly hinder their future operations.

Globally, gold level signified an extraordinary core force. In this world where one individual could match an entire army, gold level commanded far greater respect than silver level.

Operating abroad with gold level strength would attract intense scrutiny, suspicion, apprehension, and heightened vigilance, along with targeted surveillance.

Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had previously collaborated with the dragonman youngster to eliminate the Bayonet Gang precisely because the youngster wasn't gold level. Had he been of equal standing, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord would never have felt such ease.

Zong Ge did not blame Xu Ma for failing to detect the ambush as it was entirely reasonable.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' journey to the Ice Sculpture Kingdom and entry into the royal capital had been meticulously planned, with ample preparations made.

On the surface, Xu Ma, a bronze level scout, served as the reconnaissance lead.

In truth, the primary reconnaissance force consisted of scouting alchemy puppets.

After Bu Quan, Zhong Yong, and several other silver level individuals joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, they hastily produced several scouting puppets alongside their alchemy preparations.

These puppets were vastly superior to those previously crafted by Zi Di.

Zi Di's alchemy skills were far inferior to theirs.

Additionally, Bu Quan had gifted all four of her alchemy workshops to the dragonman youngster, so the tools at their disposal were far superior to those Zi Di had used.

The blueprints for these alchemy puppets all came from the War Merchant, guaranteeing top quality.

Zi Di had even coated the sole silver level alchemy puppet she'd rushed to complete with the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell.

After Zong Ge, Zi Di, and their group departed Snow Bird Harbor, Zi Di dispersed all the reconnaissance puppets under her command to scout the surrounding area. During rest stops along the way, she recalled them to replenish their magic power crystals before sending them out again.

The silver level reconnaissance puppet model, named the Stealth Breaker, boasted longer endurance and significantly superior reconnaissance capabilities compared to other scouting puppets. This time, it was precisely this puppet that detected the ambush.

Zi Di elaborated to Zong Ge: “There are two ambushers, one silver level and one gold level.”

“The gold level is a fighter, while the silver level is a magician.”

Zong Ge's expression grew stern as he fell deep in thought.

Though only two ambushers, their ranks were far too high.

A gold level could command an entire city. Why would such a powerful individual hide here, engaging in petty banditry? What could their purpose be?

As for the silver level... they might prove even more troublesome than the gold level. Magicians possessed far too many devious methods!

"While Cold Wind Valley does have trade routes, it's not a major thoroughfare. The valley itself isn't large, it's difficult to hide in, and no bandits maintain a permanent presence here. What are these two ambushing here for?" Zi Di voiced her opinion.

“They likely aren't targeting us specifically.”

“To be safe, we could take a detour.”

Zong Ge remained silent. He stared at the valley exit before slowly shaking his head.

“The exit is right before us. To detour, we'd have to turn back, leave Cold Wind Valley first, then make a wide loop around it. That would waste too much time.”

“Besides, how can you be certain they aren't ambushing here specifically for us?”

Zi Di frowned slightly: "The Bayonet Gang has been wiped out. The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord is our ally, and we've secured cooperation with the Li Jian Clan. Thanks to the commander, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is renowned throughout the entire kingdom. Even if someone were to cause trouble for us, they'd likely be targeting ‘you’, the commander."

“After all, you defeated a gold level opponent as a silver level commander. For fame and fortune, you're bound to attract many challengers.”

"But this ambush here clearly isn't about friendly sparring or challenges, it's far more malicious. We're new here. We don't have enemies like this."

The survivors knew they were outsiders and had always strived to atone for their past. Especially Zi Di, who had endured the tragedy of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance's collapse, thus prioritizing the mercenary group's external relations. Thanks to her efforts, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps maintained relatively smooth external relations.

After forming an alliance with the Li Jian Clan, their external standing had improved considerably.

Zong Ge glanced at Zi Di before shifting his gaze back to the valley exit.

He spoke calmly, “My thoughts are quite the opposite of yours, Magician Yao Ma.”

"Your analysis is sound. But that doesn't mean these two ambushers aren't our enemies. We have no evidence to prove otherwise."

"Often, we make enemies without even realizing it. Many reasons enemies give for targeting us are laughable. Enemies lurking in the shadows might remain invisible to you."

“I can't recall when we offended an unknown gold level fighter or an unknown silver level magician.”

“But it's entirely possible these two ambushed us precisely for that reason!”

"Our route was classified, yet our movements were easily tracked. With only two of them, both transcendents, not a large force, relocating is effortless. Setting up an ambush ahead of us would be no trouble at all."

"Retracing our steps and bypassing the valley is pure evasion. If these two are targeting us specifically, even if we take a detour, they'll strike again. This time we were lucky to spot them—what about next time? They'll be more cautious, hide deeper, and our disadvantage will grow."

“If they weren't targeting us, wouldn't detouring just waste our time?”

“Therefore, what we should do is verify.”

Zi Di: “How do you propose to verify?”

“Use the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell and Deceptive Disguise spell to augment those alchemy puppets. Dress them up to look like our group and have them leave the valley.” Zong Ge suggested.

Zi Di shook her head immediately, “Impossible.”

She continued telepathically, “We only have 63 pearl bubbles. Empowering that many alchemy puppets would consume too much.”

“We don't know when the commander will return, nor how long this trip to the capital will take.”

“We must conserve them as much as possible, saving more pearls for unforeseen emergencies.”

Zong Ge frowned. "Supplies are meant to be used. If we don't arm ourselves to the fullest, a major battle could wipe us out, and all those supplies would become enemy spoils!"

"How many pearls would enchanting those alchemy puppets even use? At most, three."

Zi Di merely shook her head, her stance unwavering.

Once Zi Di refused, Zong Ge found himself at a loss.

After all, he wasn't a magician.

Among the survivors, only two possessed magic abilities. With Cang Xu having left the group, Zi Di was the sole one capable of manipulating the Deceptive Disguise Formation to perform enchantments.

Faced with this reality, Zong Ge could only concede, his tone growing colder: “If you're unwilling to part with them, then enchant me instead.”

“Let me lead the assault, strike first to gain the advantage!”

In that moment, Zong Ge displayed the true bearing of a formidable commander.

As an exceptional military leader, if he discovered ambush troops ahead while marching his forces, and remained uncertain of their intentions, what course of action would he take?

There was no need for deliberation.

Lead the charge immediately and eliminate all potential threats!

Zong Ge would never gamble on whether the ambush was accidental or malicious. An ambush ahead, even unintentional, posed a threat.

Eliminate the threat outright!

Zi Di gasped in shock, staring at Zong Ge in disbelief, “Are you mad?”

To her, it was utterly unnecessary.

Taking a detour could easily avoid this risk. Even if it delayed them, they could pay exorbitant teleportation fees to save time and make up for the lost distance.

Why risk life and limb when money could solve the problem?

Money could be earned again if lost, but what good was wealth without life?

Now Zong Ge not only refused to avoid the risk but actually wanted to provoke trouble? This was utterly absurd!

Zong Ge shook his head, utterly disappointed in Zi Di, “Miss merchant, do not fear hardship or challenge.”

"If they set this ambush for us, they are undoubtedly our enemies. Now that they don't know they've been exposed, I can use the Deceptive Disguise spell to launch a surprise attack. It'll give us a huge advantage and catch them off guard."

"If they aren't our enemies, after my surprise attack, they won't fight us to the death. They'll most likely retreat voluntarily. Because they haven't found their target, and now they've been exposed."

Zi Di cut him off: "You said it yourself, it's only probable. So, it's also possible they'll fly into a rage and fight you with everything they've got. After all, you disrupted their plans. And you're only a silver level, not the commander. How could you possibly fight them?"

"I don't understand. What benefit would we gain from this? We'd likely just make enemies for no reason!"

Zong Ge sneered coldly: "The benefit is obvious! No one will dare to ambush us on our path again. Even if they do, they'll be far more cautious."

“That's the benefit?!” Zi Di glared at Zong Ge. "Once a battle breaks out, maybe you and I could escape, but the others? They'll all die!"

Zong Ge snorted coldly, “Miss Zi Di, did you think our journey to the capital would be smooth sailing?”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is already renowned throughout the kingdom. Who knows how many are waiting for a chance to engage me in a heroic duel?”

“Only by demonstrating our strength can we inspire fear and eliminate many troubles before they arise.”

“A commander must look to the future!”

Zi Di snapped impatiently, “All I know is you're courting trouble right now, big trouble!”

“You have a point. We must demonstrate our strength and clear some obstacles early.”

“But facing a gold level fighter and a silver level magician teaming up, do we have the strength for that?”

“We can fight,” Zong Ge mused. "Even if we lack strength, we must appear sufficiently formidable. In this foreign land, we are outsiders, yet our reputation precedes us."

"Only by demonstrating sufficient resolve can we make others realize that even if they defeat us, they'll pay a heavy price. They'll think twice, and we'll reduce our enemies significantly."

Zi Di sneered coldly: "Your resolve will likely cost us heavy casualties. If the commander were here, he'd never recklessly sacrifice subordinates like pawns."

Zong Ge frowned. "Therefore, I believe our commander was overly sentimental. Sacrifice is often unavoidable. Sometimes we must even sacrifice proactively to secure a better tactical position."

“If you truly care for those pearls, then only have the reconnaissance puppets disguise themselves as us two to lead these men out of the valley.”

"If we trigger an ambush, it confirms they're enemies, then we can engage then. If not, we can keep moving and avoid conflict."

Hearing Zong Ge dismiss her beloved, Zi Di instantly felt resistance.

Then, as Zong Ge “coldly” proposed using other mercenaries as bait, a chill gripped her heart.

Her anger flared: “Tripleblade isn't here, and the two squads you trained so hard are left behind in Snow Bird Harbor. Does that mean you can sacrifice others at will?”

Zong Ge drew a deep breath, his own temper simmering.

He never liked verbal negotiations and was never good at this sort of interaction.

He suppressed his rage and made one final attempt at communication: "You misunderstand me. Even if Tripleblade were here, I'd make the same decision. Those two squads aren't irreplaceable. Not even myself! As long as the sacrifice holds value...

“Enough!” Zi Di snapped, her voice low and furious, “I absolutely refuse to accept your plan. It's utterly foolish! We turn back immediately!”

Zong Ge closed his eyes, then opened them.

He glanced down at Zi Di before barking an order: “Hold your positions! Full alert!”

Then he turned and walked away.

Zi Di froze for a moment before calling out to stop him.

But Zong Ge had no intention of wasting words. He broke into a run, reaching the final stretch of the valley.

Xu Ma and the others were bewildered: "Why did the commander argue with Magician Yao Ma? Did the commander just storm off in anger?"

But the next moment, the ambushers struck without warning.

They charged at Zong Ge, their intent to kill palpable.

A blinding radiance of magic enveloped the valley's exit.

Within the piercing light, a figure cloaked in gold battle qi charged straight toward Zong Ge.

Zi Di's heart sank instantly.

It was clear these two were targeting the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!

Chapter 618: Cold Wind Valley Ambush

With a formidable enemy at hand, Zi Di had no time for anger. She immediately drew her staff and joined the fray.

Currently disguised as Yao Ma, she appeared to be a silver level fire magician.

Her staff was also silver level.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ standing in Snow Bird Harbor, coupled with their financial resources, made acquiring silver level equipment no problem. Even for gold level gear, they had secured considerable quantities through connections with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

The staff, named the Soaring Flame Staff, came pre-imbued with three spells: Fireball, Fire Wall, and Ring of Fire. Fireball was an offensive spell, while Fire Wall, when used skillfully, served both offense and defense. The Ring of Fire was purely defensive. It was precisely because of this spell that the Soaring Flame Staff was undoubtedly a premium silver level staff!

With the staff's aid, Zi Di didn't need to chant at all. She simply channeled her spirit and infused it with magic power, launching a fireball.

The silver level fireball aimed straight for the top of the hillside.

That was precisely where the hidden scouting puppet had detected the enemy's position.

The gold level fighter had already charged down, but the silver level magician remained atop the slope.

The silver level magician spotted the incoming fireball and was taken aback, surprised their precise location had been revealed so quickly. They hadn't seen Yao Ma cast any scouting spells at all.

The silver level magician immediately flashed their bracelet.

The bracelet, a silver level magic artifact, nearly instantly cast Light Shield.

The shield materialized as a square, approximately 1.8 meters in length and width, immediately soaring forward to collide with the fireball.

Boom!

A sudden explosion erupted as the fireball struck the shield, detonating mid-air and unleashing intense heat and shockwaves.

The silver level magician successfully defended, and the fireball failed to hit its target.

Zi Di drew a scroll from her bosom and continued casting Fireball.

The silver level scroll hadn't been purchased but sourced from the War Merchant's arsenal.

Truth be told, much of that stockpile had already been depleted. Since leaving Mystifying Monster Island, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had endured numerous skirmishes along their journey. Of the twelve crates of explosive dumbbells, ten were used up in the battle against Rou Zang. When the remnants of the Bayonet Gang fled from Snow Bird Harbor dock, they exhausted all water-elemental and ice-elemental arrows and bullets in their arsenal to counter the swamp oil elementals.

The original stock of 138 healing scrolls had been depleted by more than half, but after restocking at Snow Bird Harbor, the count had risen back to 97.

Fireball scrolls were the most abundant, once numbering 250. Though many were consumed in successive battles, the initial stock was largest, leaving over a hundred remaining.

Scrolls were naturally faster than chanting spells. Zi Di knew their side was at a disadvantage and had to rely on equipment to compensate.

Zi Di, whose magician level fell short of silver, tore open three Fireball scrolls in succession and unleashed them toward the hilltop.

This forced the silver level magician to abandon her position.

A gem ring on her little finger emitted an eerie glow.

The light lingered behind her, rapidly expanding into an elliptical halo.

The silver level magician retreated backward, stepping into the elliptical sphere of light before vanishing.

The next moment, a similar white line of light appeared atop the opposite hillside. The silver level magician retreated, stepping out from within it.

Boom! Boom! Boom!

Three consecutive explosions echoed as the mountaintop where she had stood moments before was levelled.

The silver level magician then pulled a scroll from her robe.

She tore it open, unleashing a Dazzling Light spell.

The spell did not target Zi Di, but instead shot toward Zong Ge.

“No!” Zi Di tried to intercept it, but was powerless to do so, forced to watch helplessly as Zong Ge was struck.

Zong Ge was already locked in combat with the gold level fighter and was losing ground.

After being hit by another Dazzling Light spell, his vision instantly flooded with blinding white light, making it impossible to discern his enemy.

“Die!” The gold level fighter roared, launching a fierce assault.

This mysterious foe was tall and agile, wearing an iron mask and wielding a long-handled war hammer with a barrel-shaped head, radiating intense fury.

In the critical moment, Zong Ge closed his eyes and raised his Gold Flower Hacksaw!

Clang!

The hammerhead aimed for Zong Ge's head, but he crouched slightly and shifted his stance. The steel saw, raised just in time, blocked the blow.

The mysterious foe's war hammer was merely silver level, but Zong Ge wielded a gold level Gold Flower Hacksaw.

This weapon had originally belonged to Xiong Ju, ringleader of the Bayonet Gang. However, the Bayonet Gang was wiped out in a single day by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord. Xiong Ju and his men were captured, and their weapons and equipment fell into the city lord's hands.

The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord had long conducted multiple transactions with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Through these deals, the equipment had ultimately ended up in the hands of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Not only the Gold Flower Hacksaw, but other gear like the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer, the Qi Boosting Syringe, the Root Boots, and Teng Dong Lang's gold level whip were all traded to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The only regret was that defensive emblem - the Flowing Wave Armor.

It was gold level armor, and the design was ingenious!

With the Ice Sculpture Kingdom enforcing an armor ban, such defensive accessories were exceptionally rare in the absence of formal gold level armor.

Thus, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord kept it in his possession. Despite the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps inflating the price several times, it remained unsold.

Seeing Zong Ge lose his vision, Zi Di grew even more tense.

In her view, Zong Ge was already in danger; now, without vision, the situation had become dire!

But Zi Di lacked a dispelling spell, so she chose to continue attacking. She immediately wielded her staff, unleashing Fire Wall.

The spell swiftly spread across the hillside crest, forcing the silver level magician to take flight.

Zi Di tore open another scroll, casting Fireball.

The silver level magician couldn't use her ring to draw a light circle for teleportation again so quickly. Such magic items usually had restrictions, requiring a period of time before they could be used a second time.

The silver level magician still had other cards up her sleeve.

She revealed the necklace hanging around her neck. The pendant was a rectangular tag.

The pendant flashed, scattering light into the air and releasing the sealed magic pet within.

The silver level magician clearly specialized in light magic; her pet was a silver level light elemental.

The light elemental had two clawed paws, a bear-like head, and a lower body tapering to a sharp point like a cone. Flight was its instinct. The moment it was released, it received its master's command and immediately charged at Zi Di.

Zi Di took a deep breath, refusing to be distracted by the enemy's magic pet.

“I have a magic pet too!” she whispered, releasing Lan Lu's ghost.

The two headed ogre's ghost was obtained during her time on Snake Rat Island. It had been severely damaged on Two Eyes Island, rendering it nearly unusable.

However, through Cang Xu's efforts, Zi Di acquired a batch of soul crystals and numerous potent undead spells, granting her the ability to heal the ghost Lan Lu.

Now, Lan Lu not only returned to peak condition but was also imbued with the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell and Deceptive Disguise spell, masking its undead nature.

To outsiders, the ghost Lan Lu appeared as a formidable combat-type alchemy puppet.

Being a ghost, Lan Lu could naturally float through the air.

The light elemental familiar was also silver level, matching Lan Lu's level.

The two magic pets roared at each other before lunging into a fierce clash, locked in an intense stalemate.

The ambushers numbered only two, but the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps fielded more than just Zong Ge and Zi Di.

“Engage!” roared Xu Ma, swiftly drawing his alchemy rifle and firing a signal flare skyward.

This was an unmistakable call for backup.

The silver level magician naturally moved to intercept, firing a precise light spear that shattered the flare before it could fully ascend.

But in the next instant, the other accompanying mercenaries followed Xu Ma's lead, drawing their alchemy muskets and firing their own signal flares.

The silver level magician was momentarily speechless, having never imagined such signal guns were standard issue for nearly everyone.

She intended to continue her interception, but Zi Di wouldn't let her succeed. A barrage of fireballs forced her to constantly shift positions.

Xu Ma then directed the mercenaries to concentrate their fire.

Their alchemy crossbows and muskets, sourced from the War Merchant's arsenal, now utilized alchemy arrows and bullets.

In an instant, bullets and arrows converged into a dazzling yet deadly barrage, forcing the silver level magician to frantically dodge while maintaining her defensive spells. She found it nearly impossible to support her allies or strike at Zong Ge.

“Zong Ge, hold on!” Zi Di shouted. Relying on her gear and high-frequency advanced spells, supported by other mercenaries, she had managed to suppress the silver level magician.

Yet she remained unable to aid Zong Ge, offering only anxious encouragement.

Zong Ge, however, remained steadfast.

No matter how fiercely the gold fighter attacked, he parried every strike with his Gold Flower Hacksaw.

Zong Ge's combat experience was immense. Even temporarily blinded, he could pinpoint enemies by sound alone.

Had the enemy been an assassin type, he might have been in danger. But the gold level foe before him fought with a sweeping, aggressive style, much like Xiong Ju, charging headlong into battle, relying on sheer force to overwhelm.

Beyond his precise recognition, Zong Ge held another advantage: the Gold Flower Hacksaw.

The Gold Flower Hacksaw possessed an exceptionally broad blade. As a weapon master, Zong Ge often utilized the saw as a shield after identifying incoming attacks, securing himself in a solid defense.

The gold fighter, frustrated by his prolonged inability to break through, grew dissatisfied with his silver level war hammer and unleashed his battle skill.

Zong Ge immediately sensed the enemy's aura surge, recognizing the use of a battle skill. He responded by deploying his own defensive battle skill.

Clang! Clang! Clang!

The gold level adversary swung his long-handled war hammer like a torrential downpour, but Zong Ge blocked left and right, steadily retreating while maintaining a solid defensive line.

“He's clearly only silver level...” The gold level foe's eyes blazed with bloodshot fury.

Were it not for the glow of silver level battle qi, he might have suspected he was clashing with an equal.

Zong Ge weathered the most perilous phase through sheer defense. As time passed, the Dazzling Light spell's effect gradually faded, allowing him to see again, albeit dimly.

He opened his eyes and simultaneously drew the flying hammer from his waist, hurling it forward.

It was the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer!

Originally forged by the dwarf ringleader of the Bayonet Gang, it was a replica of the legendary weapon, the Great Thunder God's Flying Hammer.

Both the hammer body and shaft radiated a silver level aura, but the gem embedded at the very tip emitted a gold level aura, flickering with threads of lightning.

It could be considered a near-gold level heavy throwing weapon.

Controlled by mental will, it was extremely convenient and had a low learning curve.

After Xiong Ju obtained it, he trained with it for just one night and wielded it in the next day's heroic duel, causing considerable trouble for the dragonman youngster.

The Small Thunder God Flying Hammer spun toward the gold level fighter.

The gold fighter raised his long-handled war hammer, knocking it away with the hammerhead. But it spun once in midair and charged toward the gold fighter again.

Meanwhile, Zong Ge shifted from defense to offense, swinging his Gold Flower Hacksaw to unleash a series of dangerous blade shadows.

The gold fighter, engaged in combat with Zong Ge while also guarding against the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer, gradually became overwhelmed, exposing more and more openings.

"This damn thing!" The gold level fighter roared in fury, unleashing a fierce battle skill to smash the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer.

Under Zong Ge's mental control, the hammer shot upward, soaring into the air.

The gold level fighter tried to pursue, but Zong Ge had already closed in before him.

Zong Ge channeled his battle qi into the Gold Flower Hacksaw.

The hacksaw's teeth whirled at breakneck speed, while the gem embedded in its handle emitted an earthy yellow glow.

The gold fighter instantly felt his body grow heavy, his movements slowing.

Battle skill—Spinning Horizontal Slash!

Zong Ge gripped the saw's handles with both hands, slightly bending his waist. Using his heels as pivots, he straightened his arms, driving the Gold Flower Hacksaw to spin forcefully in a full circle.

The saw's speed and power surged dramatically.

Its surface blazed with silver light as it slashed viciously toward the gold fighter's waist.

The gold level fighter had no time to retreat gracefully. In desperation, he summoned his battle qi to unleash a defensive battle skill, fortifying his double-handed war hammer.

He blocked the horizontal slash with the middle section of the hammer's shaft.

Had Zong Ge wielded a greatsword, this attack would have ended there. But in his hands was the Gold Flower Hacksaw.

In the next instant, the saw teeth savagely tore into the barrel-shaped handle, erupting in a torrent of sparks.

These sparks coalesced midair into blossoms of golden light.

The sharp edges of the golden flowers whirled and danced, diving toward the gold fighter's vulnerable points, like the eyes, nose, and throat.

The gold fighter naturally possessed defensive battle skills to protect himself, but the relentless stream of golden flowers swiftly eroded his defenses.

Bang! Bang! Bang!

The Small Thunder God Flying Hammer struck again, hitting his back, waist, and the back of his head, delivering another heavy blow.

The gold fighter roared in fury, unleashing a powerful battle skill. The surging shockwave kicked up a cloud of dust, nearly knocking Zong Ge off his feet.

Zong Ge adjusted his stance and charged forward once more.

Amidst the swirling dust, his and the gold fighter's figures blurred, appearing and vanishing abruptly.

Outsiders heard only the clashes and explosions.

The dust was soon blown away by the battle skills' aftereffects. To everyone's surprise, Zong Ge held the upper hand!

In Zong Ge's hands, the Gold Flower Hacksaw and the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer were wielded with astonishing skill.

The two weapons complemented each other with perfect synergy, leaving the gold fighter scrambling to respond. Occasional flashes of brilliance and masterfully coordinated strikes kept the gold fighter reeling from relentless blows.

Zi Di couldn't help but breathe a huge sigh of relief.

The key to this battle rested squarely on Zong Ge's shoulders. All she could do was cheer him on from the sidelines.

The other mercenaries roared in approval, their morale soaring. This surge of energy fueled an even more intense barrage of arrows and bullets.

“Long Fu, just you wait!” The gold fighter spat out a threat before retreating.

The silver level magician brought up the rear, tearing open a spatial scroll. Instantly, a swarm of alchemy puppets poured forth.

Over a hundred automatons swarmed toward the main force of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Instead of pursuing the gold level fighter, Zong Ge joined Zi Di in confronting the automatons to protect their subordinates.

Lacking aerial combat skills, the Gold Fighter was swiftly carried away by the silver level magician, vanishing from the Cold Wind Valley in moments.

Chapter 619: Cultivating a Fishman Battle Skill

The battle in Cold Wind Valley gradually drew to a close.

From the very start, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had detected the ambush. By the time hostilities commenced, Zong Ge's overwhelming dominance over the gold level Fighter had already sealed the outcome.

Zong Ge and Zi Di chose not to pursue the gold level Fighter or the silver level magician. Ultimately, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps joined forces to destroy all the alchemy puppets holding back the enemy.

After the battle, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps suffered three deaths and seventeen injuries. Among them, eight sustained minor injuries, while nine were gravely wounded.

The final stretch of Cold Wind Valley, now a battlefield, lay utterly ravaged.

The slopes on either side were bombarded beyond recognition. The narrow exit was nearly buried by collapsed hillsides.

Traces of scorching flames were everywhere. Fragmented remains of alchemy puppets littered the ground, while towering ice pillars over a meter high stood scattered about. Some sealed within them the shattered limbs of puppets, while others froze solid members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

These mercenaries had frozen to death, their faces still frozen in the terror of their final moments.

Some alchemy puppets possessed self-destruct capabilities. The instant of their explosion generated powerful cold air, freezing everything nearby.

When the explosions first occurred, Zi Di and Zong Ge were momentarily flustered.

But Zong Ge quickly made the right call, deploying the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer.

It must be said, the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer proved its worth once more, completely countering these ice-exploding alchemy puppets. Its paralyzing attack effect destroyed them prematurely, preventing the ice explosions.

Otherwise, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' casualties would have risen significantly!

Zi Di led the mercenaries in clearing the battlefield, melting ice blocks, and salvaging as many alchemy puppets as possible.

These puppet fragments held significant value, some even retaining intact cores. More crucially, Zi Di sought clues within them, hoping to trace the trail and uncover the identity of the mysterious enemy.

Zong Ge inspected the battlefield.

He focused on examining the combat traces left by the gold level fighter.

Studying each hammer mark and the deep footprints gouged into the ground, Zong Ge recalled the battle's intensity. His voice grew solemn as he addressed Zi Di: “The enemy deliberately concealed their identity. The gold level fighter I faced did not use his primary weapon.”

“He temporarily wielded a long-handled war hammer, but during the fierce clash, he mostly employed slashing and cutting motions. Thrusting attacks like those were only used in the early stages of our exchange.”

“Based on my assessment, his primary weapon should be a blade, sword, or axe.”

The mysterious gold level fighter, in an effort to conceal his identity, had not unleashed his full strength.

Zi Di's heart sank: “His primary weapon is likely gold level, with distinct characteristics, so he deliberately avoided using it.”

"In their eyes, I'm Yao Ma, a silver level magician. You blocked the gold level fighter's relentless assault and even gained the upper hand by leveraging weapons. They understand that defeating us would demand a heavy price and take considerable time."

“The signal flares Xu Ma and the others launched earlier also cleverly amplified their psychological pressure.”

“Since they wish to conceal their identities, they naturally don't want this fight drawing other onlookers.”

“Therefore, they chose to retreat.”

“What puzzles me is why these two targeted the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?”

Zong Ge recalled the battle scene: "The gold fighter gritted his teeth, radiating intense hatred and fury. Perhaps their target wasn't the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, but me."

“To be precise, ‘Commander Long Fu’!”

Zi Di grew even more puzzled: "If this hatred isn't feigned but genuine, then these two harboring such intense animosity toward the commander likely have ties to the Bayonet Gang."

“But we've purchased all available intelligence on the Bayonet Gang.”

"It's merely a local gang with minimal connections to forces outside Snow Bird Harbor. Would someone ambush us to avenge Teng Dong Lang?"

"Though Teng Dong Lang died by the commander's hand, it was in a heroic duel. By local custom, seeking vengeance would only tarnish the glory of his valiant death."

"Perhaps enemies drawn by the Bu Quan affair? But that doesn't make sense either. Even if the Alchemy Guild refuses to let go, they should know the terms of our contract with Bu Quan. Killing us isn't a wise strategy to reclaim Bu Quan."

“Perhaps some faction saw our business plan and wanted to crush us to seize the kingdom's mount potion market for themselves?”

"No, our potions aren't distributed nationwide yet. They're only selling well in Snow Bird Harbor. That profit margin shouldn't justify deploying a gold level to ambush us. More crucially, this won't hinder our business plans either."

Zi Di speculated endlessly but couldn't fathom where the enemy came from or why they harbored such intense hatred.

“Regardless, we should leave this place as soon as possible.” Zong Ge withdrew his gaze, which had been scanning the battlefield for clues.

Zi Di's heart sank.

They had no idea how many hidden enemies lurked. If these two had escaped to send word and summon reinforcements, lingering here was even more perilous.

Thus, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps hastened their pace. After leaving the Cold Wind Valley, they rode without pause toward the next town.

Fortunately, the town wasn't far. The closer they got, the more carts and travelers filled the roads.

By evening, the group formally arrived at Blood Kin City.

At the city gate entrance, they underwent inspection by the guards.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, dusty and bearing injuries, quickly drew attention.

When Zong Ge presented the identity papers issued by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord to the guards, someone excitedly asked, “Excuse me, are you Lord Long Fu?”

Zong Ge nodded impassively.

This subtle gesture immediately sparked a chorus of gasps and admiration.

“Lord Long Fu, you're incredible, you slayed a gold level as a silver level!”

“I bought your magic portrait! You wiped out the Bayonet Gang—you're a true hero!”

“How did you manage it?”

“Would you take me as your disciple?”

The crowd at the city gate swelled, the scene growing chaotic.

Even some guards were stirred, casting admiring glances.

After verifying his credentials, the veteran guard promptly returned Zong Ge's ID: "Blood Kin City welcomes you, sir, and your group. But please hurry on."

Zong Ge led his mercenaries swiftly into the city.

Before nightfall, they found lodging at an inn.

The wounded were settled first. Hired priests entered their rooms to cast healing divine magic.

The innkeeper was thrilled. Meeting Zong Ge and Zi Di, he voluntarily waived their lodging and meal fees, declaring it an honor to host the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Late into the night, a messenger who had arrived early at Blood Kin City sought a secret audience with Zong Ge and Zi Di.

Sent by Tripleblade via a teleportation formation, he delivered intelligence concerning Lan Zao.

Thus, Zong Ge and Zi Di learned of Lan Zao's current situation.

Zong Ge's tone was flat: "Lan Zao is fortunate to have survived, but from here on out, he must rely on himself. He's merely a bronze level mercenary. I don't hold much hope for him becoming a competent spy."

“Lan Zao didn't die. Commander, are you aware of this? Where are you now?” Zi Di's longing overflowed.

After settling down, she missed the dragonman youngster especially keenly.

Before reaching the next town after leaving Snow Bird Harbor, Zi Di and Zong Ge clashed, their differences escalating sharply.

The ambush battle erupting in Cold Wind Valley temporarily overshadowed their discord. After the fight, neither Zi Di nor Zong Ge tacitly brought up the previous conflict again.

But both knew the truth.

Zi Di, relying on her status and abilities as a magician, sought to seize control.

Yet Zong Ge had his own agenda.

He lacked Zi Di's exceptional diplomatic skills and eloquence, but his decisive actions spoke louder than words.

His actions at the valley exit particularly demonstrated his resolve - if cooperation isn't possible, then I'll act alone!

It was precisely his deliberate exposure that triggered the enemy ambush as they departed Cold Wind Valley.

Zi Di had no choice but to join the fight, effectively accepting Zong Ge's course of action.

“If only the commander were here...” Zi Di sighed inwardly.

This situation gave her a headache.

The disagreement in Cold Wind Valley made her fully realize how vastly different she and Zong Ge were. Their personalities, values, and approaches to action. Both sought dominance, yet neither was willing to yield.

This journey had only just begun.

“Plus, there's that mysterious enemy lurking in the shadows, waiting to strike... sigh.” Zi Di felt helpless and anxious.

Ocean Mother Nest.

Inside the landing bay, the fishman youngster practiced battle skills alone.

He practiced a new battle skill called Swift Swim. When activated, this battle skill amplified the thrust generated by his limbs and fishtail through water, dramatically increasing his swimming speed.

20

The War Merchant's database listed over twenty battle skills named Swift Swim. These battle skills themselves differed little in core mechanics. The one the fishman youngster had chosen was specifically tailored for fishmen.

Battle skills represent methods of wielding battle qi. Each race adapts them according to their unique physical structures.

For instance, the Surge battle skill and Demolition Fist previously used by the dragonman youngster were both tailored for dragonmen.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish only had a meditation room, no combat training area, making it extremely inconvenient for the fishman youngster to practice his battle skills.

Space on the Deep Sea Monster Fish was severely limited, and the situation here was actually worse than it seemed. The survivors had gathered a great deal of treasure from Two Eyes Island, piling it high throughout the Deep Sea Monster Fish. Even the passageways were cluttered, making it difficult to walk.

Fortunately, many of these antiques and treasures were disposed of at Snow Bird Harbor. Some were transferred to other ships, and trading with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord consumed a significant portion, finally freeing up space.

Even so, the fishman youngster still lacked suitable training space, as most compartments remained too small.

The largest compartment was the landing bay.

Located in the belly of the ship, it possessed both water-filling and drainage capabilities.

When receiving visitors entering the ship from the sea, the landing chamber opened to swallow both the visitors and the seawater.

Afterward, the chamber closed, expelled the seawater, and opened the door to the inner ship's hold, allowing the visitors to enter.

The chamber was actively filled with water mostly when the Deep Sea Monster Fish was submerged. By absorbing vast quantities of seawater, it also increases the vessel's weight.

Since he was practicing the Swift Swim battle skill, the fishman youngster simply flooded the landing compartment halfway, transforming it into a temporary enclosed swimming pool.

He splashed about freely in the water.

At first, his mastery of the Swift Swim battle skill was still rudimentary. Unable to control his speed, he collided painfully with the surrounding metal walls.

Later, he gained much more proficiency, yet the dragonman youngster still found that after activating Swift Swim, his speed fluctuated erratically, too fast or too slow, difficult to control.

After some investigation, he pinpointed the issue: Swift Swim battle skill was incompatible with the Bursting Explosion battle qi.

The Bursting Explosion battle qi cultivated through the Bursting Explosion battle qi technique possessed a signature explosive nature.

When paired with explosive offensive battle skills like the Demolition Fist, they complemented each other perfectly. However, when combined with the Swift Swim battle skill, it often caused the fishman youngster's swimming speed to fluctuate erratically, making it difficult to control.

This was because the Bursting Explosion battle qi itself could not be refined with precision.

“Does this mean I have to switch my battle qi?” The dragonman youngster hesitated inwardly, unable to make this decision for the time being.

He now faced a dilemma.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish was trapped within the Ocean Mother Nest. Considering the worst-case scenario, he might need to battle the saint level sea beast underwater. In such circumstances, the fishman form was far more reliable than the dragonman form, as the latter couldn't breathe freely underwater. The deep ocean floor wasn't the dragonman form's primary battlefield.

But clearly, the fishman form's combat power was nowhere near as strong as the dragonman form's.

Although it came with four innate racial magic covering healing, reconnaissance, attack, and support, its defensive capabilities were drastically reduced, offering the youngster no sense of security.

Moreover, the Demolition Fist, Machine Gun Fist, and Surge battle skills the dragonman youngster had mastered lost much of their potency in fishman form.

The reason was obvious: these battle skills were designed to synergize with the bloodline of the Flame Dragon King, completely incompatible with his current fishman lineage.

The fishman youngster had already undergone a transformation in his battle qi before, shifting from White River battle qi to Bursting Explosion battle qi. He knew this process was quite arduous.

To enhance the combat effectiveness of his fishman form, he wouldn't just need to change his battle qi formula; he'd also have to cultivate corresponding battle skills.

To develop well-rounded combat prowess without glaring weaknesses, relying solely on the Swift Swim battle skill was utterly insufficient. He would need at least three or four battle skills.

This demanded extensive practice from the fishman youngster.

Though he now possessed shortcuts like the Grand Arena Medal for cultivation, he lacked suitable opponents to gain combat experience. Starting from scratch, even with his exceptional warrior talent, it would take considerable time to see results.

The most hesitant point for the fishman youngster was this - he couldn't abandon his dragonman form.

Assuming he escaped the Ocean Mother Nest, his most frequently used form would still be the dragonman form. Therefore, if his battle qi transformed into another type, he would have to convert it back after escaping.

This would be quite troublesome.

Now, the fishman youngster pinned his greatest hope on the reconnaissance results from the alchemy puppets.

Those dual-eared scout puppets had already been deployed one after another.

The fishman youngster had slowly amplified their sonar signals, pushing them to the brink of nearly awakening the saint level sea beast.

Chapter 620: A Big Secret Hidden in Transmission

The dual-eared scouting puppets had been conducting reconnaissance for some time.

"I hope the area surrounding the Ocean Mother Nest is teeming with powerful sea beasts. That way, when I shatter the Ocean Mother Nest, it will trigger a massacre among the sea beasts. The more chaotic it gets, the better my chances of escaping."

The fishman youngster harbored deep confidence in escaping amidst the chaos.

His confidence didn't stem from himself or the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the alchemy magic ship, but from the Deceptive Disguise spell Technique and the god level material, the Pearl Bubble!

However, the reality was: the dual-eared detector had been running for some time, yet no results had been detected.

The fishman youngster had sought Zhong Yong's opinion on this matter.

Zhong Yong replied: "The walls of the Ocean Mother Nest must be too thick and heavy, absorbing the sound waves themselves. The waves not only had to penetrate outward but also bounce back, requiring the alchemy puppet to operate continuously for an extended period."

With no other choice, the fishman youngster reluctantly accepted this reality.

To address this situation, Zhong Yong remained active, carefully selecting a specialized reconnaissance puppet model.

The precision eel driller!

This alchemy puppet resembled an eel in form yet was far slenderer and more compact than a typical eel. They excelled at tunneling, drilling through rock to explore mineral deposits.

Zhong Yong selected this alchemy puppet model specifically to allow them to silently burrow into the walls of the Ocean Mother Nest. They would create imperceptibly fine, deep holes, facilitating sound transmission without alerting the saint level sea beast.

In other words, the precision eel drillers primarily complemented the earlier dual-eared detectors.

After practicing the Swift Swim battle skill for a while, the fishman youngster paused and began training in another battle skill - Wave Treading.

This too was a fishman battle skill.

It generated powerful thrust from the soles of the fishman's feet, enabling rapid movement over short distances.

For the fishman youngster, the power generation method of this battle skill felt far more familiar than that of Swift Swim. The sensation resembled unleashing the Surge battle skill from his soles.

Currently, the fishman battle skills the youngster focused on were all for movement.

His imaginary battles took place in the deep ocean depths, clashing with the saint level sea beast. In such encounters, the youngster's goal was certainly not to defeat the enemy, but to escape.

In truth, he knew escape would be difficult.

Based on his observations, even if he simultaneously employed Swift Swim, Wave Treading, and Surge, he couldn't outpace a saint level sea beast at its speed.

The fishman youngster's current goal was pragmatic: within a confined space, he sought to enhance his mobility, evade lethal attacks, and buy himself a precious second or two to survive.

Though a second or two seemed brief, it often meant the difference between life and death.

After mastering two mobility-enhancing fishman battle skills, the youngster began training in a third - Hardened Scales.

This, too, was a fishman battle skill.

Its effect was straightforward, evident from its very name. Upon activation, every scale covering the fishman's body would become tougher and thicker. His defensive capabilities would receive a significant boost.

However, this substantial increase was relative to a fishman's baseline.

The fishman youngster had compared it: even after activating Hardened Scales, his defense still fell far short of the dragon scales in his dragonman form.

The concentration of the Flame Dragon King's bloodline slightly exceeded 34%, while his fishman form's bloodline concentration was only 11%. Beyond this primary factor of concentration, inherent strengths and differences existed between species.

Fishmen bloodlines were fundamentally not adept at defense.

In truth, the fishmen race simply could not compare to the dragons.

That said, having the Hardened Scales battle skill was still better than having nothing at all.

The fishman youngster planned to cultivate more battle skills in the future, striving to enhance his defensive capabilities.

Having grown accustomed to the protection of dragon scales, he always felt a pang of unease and insecurity whenever he shifted into his fishman form.

"If I could purchase the Nomadic Armor, it would be immensely helpful to me now. Unfortunately, the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord refuses to sell this gold level equipment."

The fishman youngster possessed another gold level armor piece: the Golden Silk Chainmail. However, this equipment had sustained significant damage during his battle with Teng Dong Lang and remained unrepaired.

This remained the case even after alchemists like Bu Quan and Zhong Yong joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The reason was quite simple.

This piece of equipment was imbued with the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell and Deceptive Disguise spell. Even if placed before Zhong Yong or Bu Quan, they wouldn't be able to see it!

It wouldn't be wise to reveal this secret to them either.

Doing so would indirectly expose the existence of the Pearl Bubbles, greatly increasing the risk of the survivors' core secrets being compromised.

Thinking of the Golden Silk Chainmail, the fishman youngster couldn't help but recall Zong Ge and Zi Di.

“Wonder how they're doing now.”

“By now, they should have left Snow Bird Harbor and be heading toward the Ice Sculpture Capital.”

“For Zong Ge, I left behind the Gold Flower Hacksaw, the Root Boots, the Qi Boosting Syringe, the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer, and a gold level longbow, among other things.”

“Perhaps, when we parted ways, I should have also entrusted the Golden Silk Chainmail to them for use.”

The survivors now possessed considerable wealth, enabling them to purchase large quantities of silver level equipment externally. At this level, such items were no longer scarce.

The issue was how much they cost.

But obtaining gold level gear was far more challenging. Often, money alone wasn't enough. Rare and unique gold level equipment was something one either found or missed out on.

Taking gold level armor, for instance, the entire mercenary corps possessed only one piece of Golden Silk Chainmail. Both the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge desperately needed it, yet timely borrowing proved impossible.

When one party used it, the other inevitably went without.

Though Bu Quan had joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, she remained only silver level. Her alchemist rank held her back, leaving her far from crafting gold level gear.

Yet the future held promise.

Once Bu Quan reached gold level, mass-producing gold level equipment would become feasible.

The fishman youngster now felt the Golden Silk Chainmail was of little use to him personally.

Against a saint level sea beast, a single piece of gold level defensive gear offered negligible protection. The difference between having it and not having it was negligible.

But giving it to Zong Ge was a different matter.

"My heroic duel with Teng Dong Lang has made my name known throughout the Ice Sculpture Kingdom. Yet, with great fame comes great scrutiny. For Zong Ge to take my place will bring many challenges."

“But even without the Golden Silk Chainmail, he can handle it!”

"Zong Ge is an exceptional commander, sharp-eyed, adept at solving complex problems, and capable of breaking through any stalemate. With him leading the team, success is assured!"

"His personal combat prowess is formidable. a weapon master whose silver battle qi now carries a golden sheen. Combined with that gold level equipment, he should be more than capable of meeting any challenge."

"Though Zi Di's magician rank is lower, she carries ample spell scrolls and potions. Her true talent lies in commerce. Her skill in negotiating with outsiders far surpasses my own."

"Both are exceptional talents among the survivors. Having endured hardships themselves, their characters show no flaws, they're both steadfast. Heading together to the Ice Sculpture Capital, they should be able to establish a foothold there."

The fishman youngster attempted to analyze and speculate on the situation with Zong Ge and Zi Di.

At the same time, this was also a way to comfort himself.

Trapped within the Ocean Mother Nest, worrying about others was less important than finding a way to escape this place as quickly as possible!

Suddenly, a powerful spatial fluctuation erupted.

The fishman youngster's expression changed drastically.

He recognized this disturbance well.

“It's a teleportation fluctuation.”

“The third teleportation has occurred!”

Realizing this, the fishman youngster abandoned his battle skill training practice.

He immediately activated his captain's authority, commanding the magic ship to drain the seawater from the landing pod.

Then, he entered the Deep Sea Monster Fish's bow through the opened metal hatch.

Entering the cockpit, he quickly surveyed the external situation through the magic projection.

The next moment, a look of astonishment spread across his face.

This time, numerous sea beasts had been transported in, and their varieties were chaotic. There were dolphins, sharks, finfish, and even water monkeys, among others.

“Fishman too?”

“Wait, these sea beasts seem controlled by the fishman.”

“What are they doing?”

The teleportation had roused the saint level sea beast.

Yet it didn't immediately attack, seemingly because it had spotted the fishman.

A silver level fishman, the leader, plucked up his courage and bellowed at the mountainous Snow Pear Octopus: “Mengl, awngml, ealnmwgm, nlga, nlma.”

He was actually communicating with the saint level sea beast!

The saint level sea beast soon lost patience and launched an attack on the other sea beasts, but refrained from harming the fishman.

For a moment, the waters of the Ocean Mother Nest churned violently, the air thick with the scent of blood.

The saint level sea beast feasted with relish, its savage, ferocious nature laid bare.

The fishmen retreated to the corners of the Ocean Mother Nest. Amidst such a bloody hunt, they closed their eyes one after another, murmuring prayers.

Upon witnessing this scene, a bolt of lightning flashed through the fishman youngster's mind.

He suddenly realized these fishmen had come prepared!

Looking closer, he discovered these were no ordinary fishmen, they were transcendents.

They ranged from bronze level to silver level, with no gold level among them.

He counted.

The number of transcendent fishmen present definitely exceeded 200. Though the silver level were few, only about 20 to 30, it was still a rare occurrence.

After all, the fishmen were a lesser race, with a probability of producing transcendents far lower than that of humans.

For over 200 fishman transcendents to gather suddenly like this, how vast must the fishman clan behind them be?

Another peculiar detail: these fishmen displayed wildly varied appearances. They weren't of a unified bloodline, nor did any dominant bloodline traits dominate.

This meant they hailed from all corners of the world, not originating from a single massive clan.

“What on earth is going on here?”

"This group of fishmen gathered together, teleported in, and brought a large number of sea beasts to feed the Snow Pear Octopus. Yet they are praying here?"

The saint level Snow Pear Octopus swiftly devoured all the sea beasts. It sucked up the blood-tinged water, consuming every drop of blood within, then spat out clean seawater, wasting not a single drop.

It stretched its soft, massive tentacles, slowly advancing toward the fishmen huddled in the corner.

Even without activating his mental vision, the fishman youngster knew this saint level sea beast remained fiercely predatory.

To its eyes, these praying fishmen were nothing but delicacies. The greedy instinct to prey stirred within it, making it restless.

Undoubtedly, the saint level sea beast's approach brought immense psychological pressure to the praying fishman.

The vast majority trembled, instinctively amplifying their prayers.

They all prayed in the fishman language. The fishman youngster brought shame upon his bloodline, he truly did not understand a single word.

Yet the sound of their prayers reached the saint level sea beast's ears, causing its movements to grow increasingly sluggish.

The fishman youngster observed all this, sensing the saint level sea beast's hesitation.

The Snow Pear Octopus finally extended its massive, snow-white tentacles, slowly closing in on one praying fishman.

Terrified, the fishman watched the tentacle advance, his psychological pressure mounting until he finally broke.

With a shriek, he frantically swam backward.

In the next instant, the snow-white tentacle moved with lightning speed. The venomous snake, already poised, shot out.

The fleeing fishman was instantly bound, his piercing screams echoing.

With a slight squeeze, the Snow Pear Octopus crushed the fishman into a pile of flesh and bone fragments, blood spreading everywhere.

The octopus opened its mouth wide and sucked in a torrent of water. The fishman's corpse was pulled into its belly without a single drop wasted.

After devouring the fishman, the octopus displayed an unprecedented sense of delight, its soft tentacles waving about wildly, a sight the fishman youngster had never witnessed before.

“Mglnalmn...”

“Gnal, mga...”

“Lanm, wgln...”

This brutal scene instantly amplified the fishmen's prayers.

The saint level sea beast continued to extend its tentacles. Each tentacle was snow-white and supple, vast like elongated white clouds, beautiful yet deadly.

The tentacles gently brushed over the fishmen's heads or passed close by them.

Many fishmen's faces were contorted with terror, their bodies shaking uncontrollably like sieves.

Yet a few remained resolute, even serene, their prayers unbroken as they ignored the octopus tentacles.

The fishman youngster watched this strange spectacle in silence.

At regular intervals, the tentacles would strike with lightning speed, killing fishmen and devouring them.

The fishman youngster gradually noticed the peculiar behavior of the saint level sea beast.

It seemed to be slowly tormenting the praying fishmen, toying with them, driving them to collapse under extreme terror. Yet it also appeared to be wary of some unseen presence, hesitating to act with full abandon.

“It attacks and devours individual fishmen, seemingly selecting them according to some hidden pattern...” The fishman youngster watched intently, sensing a profound mystery unfolding.

About half an hour passed like this when suddenly, the life force of one fishman underwent a drastic change!

Originally, he was merely a bronze level fishman fighter. But soon, his life force surged from bronze level to iron level.

“What in the world is going on?!” The fishman youngster nearly doubted his own eyes at the sight.

Yet moments later, a similar transformation unfolded in another fishman.

One after another, more fishmen saw their life levels rise.

The fishman youngster also noticed carefully: some fishmen remained at their original life levels, yet their faces lit up with intense joy, clearly, they had gained something else!

“So, what on earth is going on?” The fishman youngster was utterly astonished.

According to his understanding, life levels couldn't undergo such sudden mutations.

They required extraordinary cultivation, slowly accumulating through quantitative changes before triggering a qualitative leap.

It was a gradual process.

Yet the scene unfolding before him now shattered the fishman youngster's previous understanding.

These fishmen had managed to achieve a qualitative leap in their life levels within a remarkably short time.

It was utterly unbelievable!

But the fishman youngster's astonishment didn't end there.

Approximately three hours later, spatial fluctuations erupted once more.

The praying fishmen vanished entirely from their original positions.

“They've all been teleported away!” The fishman youngster was utterly disoriented by this sudden upheaval.

He remained rooted to the spot for a long while before gradually regaining his senses.

His mind began racing.

“There's a great secret behind this teleportation!”

“How on earth was this teleportation achieved?”

“Is it natural, or man-made?”

"What exactly is going on with these fishmen? How could their life levels advance so abruptly?"

After pondering for a while, the fishman youngster's eyes burned brightly as he stared intently at several corners within the magic projection.

Hidden in these corners were dual-eared detectors.

Without exception, each bore a layer of Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell. To outsiders, they appeared merely as pearls.

“The great secret of teleportation might be uncovered through these puppet scouts!”

Now, the fishman youngster yearned to know what lay beyond the Ocean Mother Nest.

Chapter 621: Cang Xu’s Limit

Deep in the sea.

A massive school of grouper fish raced forward in fury, relentlessly pursuing their enemy ahead.

They had only one adversary, another grouper fish.

Yet this silver level grouper was utterly bizarre, possessing only a fish head. Its body and tail were gone.

Not only that, but this grouper head radiated an intense undead aura.

It was precisely this unmasked negative energy that provoked the grouper school's fury and pursuit.

The grouper head charged forward, plunging headlong into a white underwater forest.

This was White Hair Trench.

Within the White Hair Trench thrived a unique species of coral polyps. These polyps could parasitize the surfaces of living organisms, multiplying and growing. Their accumulated excretions formed a soft, white coral.

These soft white corals formed long strands that swayed with the currents, resembling strands of white hair.

The white hair coral polyps typically parasitized sea beasts. But when a massive sea beast perished on the ocean floor, vast numbers of white hair coral polyps continued to draw life-sustaining nutrients from the carcass.

In underwater graveyards where many sea beasts died together, large colonies of coral polyps often thrived.

The soft corals produced by these polyps gathered to form forests of white hair.

Generally, these white hair forests were coral clusters posing no threat to marine life that entered them.

However, the grouper head, radiating an aura of the undead, was an exception.

The white coral was the excretion of coral polyps, still under their control and possessing a certain degree of offensive capability.

Being living organisms, the polyps naturally abhorred the negative energy emanating from the grouper head.

Even though this silver level grouper head charged into the white hair forest, it immediately triggered a violent uprising among countless polyps.

White hair coral lashed out from all directions, overwhelming the grouper head.

The grouper head couldn't withstand even two seconds, exploding on the spot.
