The leader of the Heroism Pirates – the gold level fighter - Ba Qi!

Ba Qi sat with his legs spread wide, and even in that posture, he towered over Lan Zao by a full two heads.

Lan Zao could tell at a glance that Ba Qi must be over two meters tall.

He was bald, with a square jaw and a robust, muscular build. His upper body was bare, his chest like a white rampart. Below, he wore gray prison pants so plain their uneven hems hung well above his ankles.

His bare feet were at least three times the size of Lan Zao's.

Lan Zao's limbs had grown significantly larger than average after his bloodline awakened.

Most striking, his massive soles didn't touch the deck. Instead, puffs of white vapor rose from his feet, suspending them slightly above the surface.

Upon closer inspection, Lan Zao noticed white vapor also constantly emanating from the corners of his eyebrows.

This vapor faintly radiated a golden halo.

The golden hand that had previously captured Lan Zao slowly floated onto Ba Qi's shoulder. Its golden sheen rapidly faded, transforming into a cloud of white vapor that merged into Ba Qi's body from his shoulder.

“Bad Weather bloodline!” Information about Ba Qi flashed through Lan Zao's mind.

The leader of the Heroism Pirates possessed the Bad Weather bloodline. This bloodline's origin lay in the saint level air element. Theoretically, Ba Qi's limit as the Heroism Pirates Captain should be the saint level.

However, his bloodline concentration was insufficient. The gold level had become his ceiling, and his fighter rank had stagnated for over a decade.

Air Element Transformation!

This innate racial magic came with the Bad Weather bloodline.

Thus, even at the bronze level, the Heroism Pirates Captain could diffuse his body and transform into elemental energy to attack foes. This advantage was immense. Typically, only gold ranked fighters could project their battle qi outside their bodies to form projectiles for ranged attacks.

Upon reaching gold level, the Heroism Pirates Captain's Air Element Transformation grew even more potent. He could now transform his flesh and blood into air elements, infusing them with gold level battle qi to strike distant foes.

The massive golden hand that had just captured Lan Zao was forged using this very ability.

The benefits were manifold, the most obvious being a drastic reduction in gold level battle qi consumption.

"Air Element Transformation... This racial magic allows vital organs like the heart and throat to elementalize at critical moments. This means that Ba Qi possesses no conventional human weaknesses," Lan Zao mused inwardly.

Compared to this innate talent of Air Element Transformation, the Fluid Perception and Levitation abilities inherent to Lan Zao's bloodline immediately paled in comparison.

The Heroism Pirates Captain leaned back in his chair, his gaze cold and indifferent as he looked down at Lan Zao.

Lan Zao spoke up: “Lord-lord Ba Qi... I mean you no harm. Is there some misunderstanding between us?”

“Hahahaha...”

The pirates on deck roared with laughter, surrounding Lan Zao with savage, mocking stares.

Surrounded by enemies, Lan Zao had no chance of escape. Cold sweat beaded on his forehead and back.

He no longer carried the same “ferocity” as before.

The old Lan Zao had mistakenly believed the magic potion had caused a bloodline conflict, leaving him with only days to live. Thus, he fought with reckless disregard, always thinking, “Take one down if I can, and two is a bonus.”

But now, things were different.

He discovered his bloodline had awakened, and the potion's effects were perfect. He now possessed a brand-new future, poised to achieve great things. Yet, facing a formidable opponent like Ba Qi, his heart trembled, weighed down by immense psychological pressure.

Amidst the pirates' raucous laughter, Ba Qi spoke indifferently: "What bloodline is yours? Related to the fishmen, I presume? I sense you have a strong affinity for my battle qi technique."

Lan Zao froze, a crucial insight flashing through his mind.

In that instant, he seemed to grasp why Ba Qi had attacked him.

Just as he opened his mouth to speak, Ba Qi chuckled: “Regardless of your bloodline, encountering me today is your luck.”

“But whether it's good luck or bad luck, ha ha, depends entirely on your own destiny!”

With that, he extended his fan-like hand, fingers splayed wide, unleashing a powerful suction force.

Lan Zao was utterly powerless to resist and was sucked straight toward him.

Ba Qi seized Lan Zao by the head. Lan Zao tried to struggle, but soon, air currents flowed from Ba Qi's body, shimmering with golden light. Like thick golden ropes, they bound Lan Zao tightly, rendering him completely immobile.

The Heroism Pirates Captain let out a savage laugh and abruptly intensified his battle qi infusion.

A powerful surge of qi erupted from his palm, violently forcing its way into Lan Zao's body.

Lan Zao let out a piercing scream as his body swelled outward, distorting like an inflated human balloon.

Ba Qi's gold level battle qi made contact with Lan Zao's Water Grass battle qi.

Though Lan Zao had recently ascended to iron level, his entire body's battle qi still required time to fully transform from bronze level to iron level.

In truth, even iron level battle qi stood no chance against gold level battle qi.

Thus, Lan Zao's Water Grass battle qi collapsed upon contact. His defenses crumbled rapidly, overrun by Ba Qi's gold level battle qi as it relentlessly pressed forward, forcing him to retreat and huddle defensively in a corner.

Ba Qi's gold level battle qi, being a different type of energy source, clashed violently with the Water Grass battle qi. This caused Lan Zao to bleed from all seven orifices, his screams growing shrill.

It was like a magic imbalance, the collision of two battle qi from different origins inflicted horrific damage within Lan Zao's body. To save his life, Lan Zao immediately dispersed his Water Grass battle qi outward.

Being bronze level in essence, the Water Grass battle qi couldn't coalesce outside the body and thus dissipated rapidly.

Soon, only the enemy's gold level battle qi remained within Lan Zao's body.

Lan Zao's head was gripped by Ba Qi's hand, his entire body entwined by gold level battle qi currents like ropes. He hung trembling in midair, shaking like a sieve.

Though he had wisely expended all his Water Grass battle qi, years of cultivating this technique had made his body deeply attuned to it. Even after complete depletion, it still automatically generated Water Grass battle qi throughout his body.

But the moment this qi materialized, it was crushed into dust by the omnipresent gold level battle qi.

The gold level battle qi violently pierced through Lan Zao's internal organs, muscles, and bones.

Lan Zao endured excruciating agony, as if countless steel needles formed a brush, repeatedly scraping every inch of his body.

He soon screamed himself hoarse, his voice becoming utterly raspy.

He shook violently, his internal organs severely damaged, massive internal bleeding occurring, his bones strained beyond endurance, cracks spreading across his entire body, and blood seeping through his skin everywhere.

The pirates around him instinctively wiped the smiles from their faces.

Though they'd witnessed similar scenes countless times, each occurrence sent a chill through them, deepening their fear of their captain.

“Not bad. You're still alive.” Ba Qi ceased channeling his battle qi and offered Lan Zao a rare compliment.

Lan Zao's vision darkened, teetering on the edge of unconsciousness.

He screamed inwardly with every ounce of strength, urging himself to stay awake. If he lost consciousness, death would surely follow.

“Live on with my gift. If you seek revenge, you're welcome anytime.” Ba Qi swung his arm, flinging Lan Zao like a ball hundreds of meters into the sea.

He cast him aside like discarded trash, quickly withdrawing his gaze to focus ahead, “How far to Flower Skirt Island?”

The first mate hurriedly replied, “We're almost there, Lord Ba Qi. In less than two hours, we'll reach it.”

“Hurry. We have business to attend to.”

"Yes!"

The Heroism Pirates' ship surged forward at a sudden burst of speed, rapidly pulling away. Left behind, Lan Zao lay unconscious in a pool of blood, his body slowly sinking into the sea.

Ocean Mother Nest.

The fishman youngster adjusted the effects of his Deceptive Disguise spell. To the untrained eye, the Deep Sea Monster Fish no longer appeared as a reef, but as a pile of pearls.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish had seamlessly blended into its surroundings, no longer standing out as before.

The landing pod, now flooded with seawater, had served as the fishman youngster's training chamber for days.

Submerged in the water, the youngster wasn't practicing battle skills. Instead, he kept his mouth open, continuously swallowing and expelling the surrounding seawater.

With each swallow and expulsion, a fresh surge of battle qi surged within him.

It was no longer the Bursting Explosion battle qi.

After careful deliberation, the youngster had finally decided to convert his battle qi. Heeding the tower spirit's advice, he had meticulously selected a fishman battle qi technique called the Raging Tide Surging Wave Technique.

The practice of this technique involved continuous inhalation and exhalation underwater, using the compression of muscles in the chest and other areas to generate an endless flow of wave-like battle qi.

“The Raging Tide Surging Wave Technique truly suits my new form.”

Even though he had only just begun the conversion, the fishman youngster could already sense the superiority of his new battle qi.

This superiority was primarily evident in the compatibility between the battle qi and his body.

When the Bursting Explosion battle qi circulated in his fishman form, it felt like his throat was filled with chili powder. Yet, as the Surging Wave battle qi flowed through him, it brought waves of refreshing coolness that soothed his soul. He even experienced a strange illusion, as if every cell in his body was rejoicing.

“It will still take some time to complete the conversion.”

“Sigh, changing battle qi technique is such a hassle.”

“If a master proficient in this type of battle qi could infuse it into my body, the conversion time would drastically shorten, perhaps even completing within minutes.”

As the fishman youngster's thoughts drifted, the tower spirit's voice interrupted: “Captain, the sonar reconnaissance has yielded preliminary results. Please review them immediately.”

"Oh? Has the situation outside the Ocean Mother Nest been detected?" The fishman youngster grew impatient.

He immediately left the landing pod and returned to the cockpit, where he viewed the reconnaissance intelligence.

"This is...!" Staring at the reconstructed magic image, the fishman youngster felt his heart tremble violently.

Chapter 636: Undersea Army

Ocean Mother Nest.

Inside the cockpit of the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

A magic projection unfolded before the fishman youngster's eyes, revealing the landscape beyond the Ocean Mother Nest.

The fishman youngster's expression shifted dramatically!

The projection depicted an undersea valley.

In dim light, a colossal magic formation occupied the entire valley. Even the cliff walls were carved with countless magic runes, tracing intricate lines of the formation.

Ocean Mother Nest lay concealed within one cliff face, the one bearing the most intricate patterns of runes and lines.

Beneath this wall stood an altar constructed from countless bones.

These skeletal remains belonged to various sea beasts, the massive bones of whales, the fearsome teeth of sharks, turtle shells, and more.

This was no simple assembly. Alchemy techniques had been applied, harmonizing the essence of every bone into a unified whole, achieving a gold level quality.

A gold level altar.

Upon it stood an octopusman priestess clad in ceremonial robes.

Her body resembled that of a human, yet her neck bore an octopus head. She had no hair, two eyes, no distinct nose bridge, but a large mouth. From her chin as the horizontal line, a ring of numerous fine octopus tentacles grew around her face, extending to the back of her head, serving as a beard.

The octopusman priestess's head was milky white, her eyes pink. Her chest was swollen, marking her as a female octopus.

What unsettled the fishman youngster most was the aura emanating from this octopusman.

Her life aura was unconcealed; she was unmistakably a saint level being! Not only that, but her life aura also carried traces of divine spells and magic. This indicated the saint level octopusman cultivated both divine spells and magic power.

At that moment, she was inspecting the altar, her expression utterly focused and serious.

Standing beside her was another octopusman.

This octopusman was very small, seemingly underage, barely reaching 1.5 meters in height.

The fishman youngster observed: This octopusman's body had a grayish-white skin tone, but his octopus head was a deep, dark green.

Though short, his body was stocky, with bulging, rock-hard muscles that gave the impression of solid marble. His chin tentacles were particularly robust, though far fewer in number than those of the saint level octopusman priestess.

This octopusman made no effort to conceal his life aura, he was a gold level fighter.

His octopus head was enormous, especially when the tentacles spread out like an umbrella canopy, creating a disproportionately large head on a small body.

His tentacles were clearly well-trained; the four largest were each coiled around a weapon, an axe, a halberd, a knife, and a spear.

Fully armored and heavily armed, his gaze swept constantly around him. He was likely the bodyguard of the saint level octopusman.

Besides the octopusman priestess atop the altar and the octopusman guards below it, numerous other intelligent sea creatures surrounded the altar.

Fishmen were the most numerous, followed by octopusmen. The fishman youngster also glimpsed nagas, mermaids, and even sea giants within the magic projection.

It was hard to imagine so many different races of undersea sentient beings gathered in one place.

Some had constructed coral dwellings, others lived in caves within the valley walls, while still others simply sat cross-legged on the ground or slept under the open sky.

Mortals comprised roughly half the gathering, while transcendents were exceptionally numerous! Bronze level, iron level, silver level, and even gold level.

From the magic projection alone, the fishman youngster had already spotted three other gold level beings!

Most of these intelligent sea creatures were gathered around the altar, kneeling in prayer. Their number was approximately 300.

Gradually, more and more gathered around the altar, joining the ranks of the praying.

When the number reached about 500, a sudden clamor erupted.

Then, ripples spread through the water as a massive school of fish and sea beasts were driven into the valley.

The fish included groupers, lanternfish, manta rays, whales, sharks, and more. Among the sea beasts, the most numerous were the sharp-clawed water monkeys.

The intrusion of the fish and sea beasts did not cause chaos in the valley. The inner circle of believers remained utterly still, immersed in their prayers, undisturbed.

This massive swarm of fish and sea beasts was driven directly onto the altar.

Presiding over the scene was yet another octopusman.

Compared to the previous two, his form was the most colossal.

His octopus tentacles were exceptionally thick, two in front and two behind, extended downward like four massive arms.

His true arms resembled tree trunks, their dark skin adorned with vivid red patterns that bloomed like countless flowers.

These crimson floral designs covered not only his arms but also the surfaces of his four octopus tentacles.

This octopusman who had driven the fish and sea beasts into the valley bowed to the saint level octopusman, seemingly reporting something: “Jlglu...”

“Gulujili,” the saint level octopusman replied with a slight wave of her hand.

The escorting octopusman then bowed and withdrew.

The fishman youngster couldn't understand them, let alone the lesser-known octopus language. Fortunately, their conversation was recorded by the Deep Sea Monster Fish, and the tower spirit could translate it later.

He continued observing.

The sea beasts and fish schools were herded toward the altar.

The massive magic circle activated, its blinding radiance ultimately surging into the altar.

The altar erupted with a powerful gravitational pull, drawing the sea beasts and fish toward it. They slammed violently against the altar, instantly crushed to death.

The gold level altar was exceptionally sturdy.

The sea beasts and fish surged like waves, shattering into countless blood-red splatters, while the altar stood immovable as a reef.

The corpses of the slain sea beasts and fish rapidly dissolved, flesh and blood vanishing, leaving only bones behind.

The diverse bones began moving of their own accord, assembling themselves with the golden altar to make it even larger and more solid.

Occasionally, ghostly apparitions of fish or sea beasts appeared, only to be instantly scattered by the divine light radiating from the altar.

“This is a blood sacrifice!”

“Moreover, this isn't a pure magic formation.” The fishman youngster's expression grew grave as the formation activated, surging with countless sacred runes. He even discerned a prayer inscribed in layers of divine script.

“A formation combining divine spell and magic!”

“Five gold level octopusmen and a saint level priestess!”

“Over thirty silver level, more than a hundred iron level, and at least two hundred bronze level!”

“This is a mixed-species undersea army.”

Racial barriers were commonplace; under normal circumstances, such a large-scale coalition would never be formed. But considering they appear to share the same faith, their gathering becomes plausible.

“But what exactly are they doing here?”

“Why the blood sacrifice?”

"And to whom is it addressing? Could it be the Charming Blue Goddess?"

Watching the magic projection before him, the fishman youngster felt his doubts lessen significantly, only to be replaced by many more.

“Regardless, I'm fortunate I didn't transform into a ghost and leave the Mother Nest.” The fishman youngster felt relieved.

Had he done so earlier, he would likely have been discovered the moment he surfaced.

A massive magic formation surrounded the Ocean Mother Nest, guarded by a united army of undersea races. What the fishman youngster feared most was the saint level octopusman priestess.

Her clergy job must be at least that of a bishop.

The fishman youngster harbored no confidence that his Deceptive Disguise spell could conceal him from such an entity.

Since emerging from the Mystifying Monster Island, the survivors had never faced such a trial; they had never attempted it.

In truth, the octopusman priestess posed a far greater threat than usual. The reason was simple: she was orchestrating a blood sacrifice!

This was an offering and ritual to a deity.

A ceremony of such immense scale would inevitably draw the attention of the gods.

The fishman youngster had no confidence in deceiving a saint level priestess, let alone a deity.

“Is there a connection between this saint level priestess and the saint level sea beast in the Mother Nest?”

“The teleportation must be related!”

The fishman youngster spotted some “familiar faces” among the praying crowd. These familiar faces were precisely the fishman who had teleported in during the previous incident and ultimately departed via teleportation.

“So, what should we do next?”

With this intelligence, the fishman youngster suddenly felt his situation was dire.

He had previously envisioned shattering the Ocean Mother Nest to distract the saint level sea beast with potential external sea beast swarms, allowing the Deep Sea Monster Fish to escape.

But now, that plan was suicide!

The sea beast swarms might be low-intelligence creatures, but the mixed naval forces surrounding them were almost entirely sentient beings.

Chapter 637: Ba Qi vs Cai Jing

Lan Zao slowly lifted his heavy eyelids.

The familiar ceiling met his gaze. Without needing to look around, he knew he had been rescued and returned to the ward of the Shuang Tu Clinic.

“I remember Ba Qi grabbing me and forcing his battle qi into my body.”

Though I managed to hold on aboard the pirate ship and barely retained my senses, I fell unconscious after plunging into the water."

“It must have been Shuang Tu who saved me...”

Though Lan Zao wasn't cunning or skilled in strategy, this conclusion wasn't difficult to deduce.

After each training session, he used teleportation to return to the underground plaza beneath Shuang Tu's clinic for recuperation.

When he failed to appear at the appointed time, Shuang Tu would inevitably investigate.

Lan Zao examined himself.

He discovered his entire body was nearly wrapped in white bandages, his head included.

Layer upon layer of bandages left only slits exposed for his eyes.

Simultaneously, the pain was slowly intensifying.

This was because he had been unconscious for so long; now that he had awakened, his senses had sharpened considerably.

However, alongside the pain came waves of coolness.

Lan Zao could smell the scent of magic ointment. Clearly, he had once again received proper treatment from Shuang Tu.

Just as Lan Zao realized this, the ward door opened quietly, and Shuang Tu entered.

Lan Zao wasn't surprised.

Having seen the alchemy components in the clinic's underground plaza, he knew the seemingly ordinary clinic concealed numerous hidden facilities. Shuang Tu's control over the clinic far exceeded appearances. Thus, shortly after Lan Zao regained consciousness, Shuang Tu learned of his situation and came to his side.

“Thank you, Physician Shuang Tu. You saved me once again,” Lan Zao offered his gratitude.

Shuang Tu smiled. "It was indeed perilous. Though your bloodline has awakened, it still requires considerable training. At least when I found you, your gills weren't functioning."

“Normally, even unconscious, your breathing ability should remain intact.”

"But you hadn't internalized gill respiration as instinct. You survived temporarily only through your fish bladder and residual air in your lungs. Had I arrived ten minutes later, you likely would have drowned."

At this point, Shuang Tu shifted his tone: "But as the saying goes - fortune and misfortune are two sides of the same coin.”

“This encounter with Ba Qi nearly cost you your life, yet it also transformed your battle qi.”

"The Water Grass battle qi you previously mastered was unsuitable for your awakened bloodline. The Air Pressure battle qi from Ba Qi suits you perfectly."

"If you were to convert this battle qi technique alone, it would take at least ten days to half a month. But after Ba Qi's help, your entire body is now filled with Air Pressure battle qi. Of course, this is a foreign type of battle qi. You'll need to cultivate it independently to generate your own Air Pressure battle qi."

“Even so, you'll only need a day or two to adapt.”

Lan Zao heard this and gave a bitter smile. "If possible, I'd rather convert the battle qi myself. I never imagined I'd suddenly encounter the Heroism Pirates. I nearly died at the hands of Ba Qi!"

"When the chance comes, I will take my revenge!"

Shuang Tu nodded. "Until that chance comes, I advise you to bide your time. I haven't told you yet, the Heroism Pirates have already arrived at Flower Skirt Island."

“What?” Lan Zao was stunned.

Nearly ten pirate ships were moored at Flower Skirt Island's docks.

The pirate flags fluttering in the wind drew countless gazes.

When Ba Qi led the crew, striding down the gangplank first and setting foot on Flower Skirt Island, the entire dock erupted.

Cheers broke out from the crowd gathered to watch the spectacle.

“It's the Heroism Pirates!”

“It really is them.”

"Ba Qi... I saw Ba Qi with my own eyes! They're members of the Fire Beard Pirates!"

Though not a regular in these waters, Ba Qi had long been a renowned pirate whose fame had spread for years. His formidable personal combat prowess was matched only by his most legendary feat: escaping the Abyssal Hanging Tower. His eccentric, one-of-a-kind behavior cemented his status as a pirate superstar.

The Abyssal Hanging Tower was the number one super prison in the Main Plane.

Its construction dated back over a thousand years.

When the demons invaded the Main Plane, allied races fought countless arduous battles before finally reaching the Skywell.

This colossal teleportation device, constructed by the demons within a natural spatial passageway, reached the legend level, capable of transporting legendary demons.

At the Skywell, the two sides engaged in a decisive battle.

The demons were defeated, and the allied forces emerged victoriously.

The allied commanders nearly destroyed the Skywell but were halted by the dwarven divine blacksmith Huo Jiao Bian. This legendary female dwarf possessed unparalleled skill in crafting divine artifacts, her achievements unmatched and irreplaceable.

Huo Jiao Bian explained to the assembly: Simply destroying the Skywell would prove counterproductive. It was a meticulously prepared demonic trap, designed to transform the spatial passage into a vortex of chaotic energy. This vortex would persist for tens of thousands of years, endangering the Main Plane.

Huo Jiao Bian proposed instead: “Why not remodel the Skywell? Build an inverted tower upon its foundation.”

She then enumerated the plan's numerous advantages, successfully persuading the allied commanders.

All races joined forces to construct the Abyssal Hanging Tower. Despite mobilizing nearly every craftsman and alchemist from the Main Plane and gathering building materials from across the world, it still took over a decade to complete the tower.

After its completion, the tower was garrisoned by rotating main armies from each race. But the abyss's aura proved impossible to shield against, steadily eroding the tower. After several major rotations, the races agreed to repurpose the tower as a prison, confining the most heinous criminals to guard it as penance.

Ba Qi was one such prisoner held within the tower.

However, he managed to escape. Counting back 500 years, he was the only one to succeed!

At the time, he was merely a bronze level child.

This legendary feat set him apart from the rest. It was said that Fire Beard personally recruited him, leading him to command the Heroism Pirates under Fire Beard's banner.

Ba Qi strode forward with long strides.

Wherever he passed, crowds parted automatically.

Many shouted and cheered, seeking Ba Qi's approval to join the Heroism Pirates.

Others stood arms-crossed at a distance, their expressions wary.

Several silver level pirates appeared before Ba Qi.

“Captain of the Heroism Pirates, Lord Ba Qi, welcome to Flower Skirt Island,” one of the silver level members addressed him stiffly.

Ba Qi stood tall, surveying the group with a condescending gaze. "So, you lot run this island? Your strength is a bit lacking."

The silver level member replied with unwavering composure, " Lord Ba Qi, Flower Skirt Island is a land of freedom. We welcome anyone who comes here without ill intent."

“We are merely fortunate to have earned the trust of others, allowing us to temporarily serve as the island's administrators.”

“The truth is, the silver level isn't the highest on Flower Skirt Island. The gold level Master Cai Jing has been living in seclusion here for many years now.”

Ba Qi fixed his gaze upon him: “What is your name?”

“Sang Shu,” the silver level snow elf who had addressed Ba Qi earlier hastened to reply.

“Is the one standing behind you your son?” Ba Qi inquired further.

The snow elf named Sang Shu flinched visibly, his response evasive: "Lord Ba Qi, Master Cai Jing and I have maintained close collaboration. My son is even a potion apprentice taken under Lord Cai Jing's wing..."

“Hahahaha!” Ba Qi suddenly burst into laughter, cutting off Sang Shu, “Your son, I've taken a liking to him!”

As he spoke, his fingers spread wide, unleashing a powerful suction force.

The young man behind Sang Shu couldn't resist and was sucked into Ba Qi's palm.

His head was gripped firmly in Ba Qi's palm, while his body was bound by swirling white currents of air, suspended mid-air.

The youngster struggled desperately, screaming frantically, yet could not break free.

“Save me, Father! Save me!”

"Lord Ba Qi, I beg you, show mercy!" Sang Shu dared not intervene, pleading urgently.

But the next moment, his entreaties were cut short by his son's agonized screams.

Massive amounts of Air Pressure battle qi were forcibly pumped into the young man's body by Ba Qi, causing him to scream in agony. His body swelled like an inflated balloon, quickly becoming unrecognizable.

Sang Shu pleaded relentlessly, unable to bear it any longer. He unleashed a battle skill, attacking Ba Qi.

The pirates surrounding Ba Qi watched the spectacle, making no move to intervene.

The silver level battle skill was effortlessly blocked by Ba Qi, who then waved his hand dismissively at Sang Shu.

Sang Shu was sent flying into the air, utterly powerless to resist.

Bam!

In the next instant, the young man in Ba Qi's grasp exploded. Blood, brain matter, chunks of flesh, and bone fragments splattered everywhere, staining the scene crimson.

Blood stained Ba Qi's face and chest crimson.

He shook his hand, murmuring disappointedly, “Failed. This youngster seemed promising. Never thought he wouldn't even last this short a time.”

Dead silence fell over the scene.

The crowd, who had been cheering moments ago, now stared in stunned silence. As they watched Ba Qi treat human life as cheap as grass, a chill crept into their hearts.

Sang Shu, witnessing his son's brutal death, was consumed by despair and seething hatred.

“You killed my son! You killed my son!” he roared, drawing his crossbow and unleashing a relentless barrage of bolts at Ba Qi.

But the silver level crossbow lacked sufficient power. Even paired with silver level alchemy arrows, it couldn't scratch Ba Qi.

Ba Qi's body swayed slightly as a mass of air element separated from him.

The air element coalesced into a massive hand, radiating the golden glow of battle qi, and lunged toward Sang Shu.

Terror washed over Sang Shu's face as he scrambled backward.

The golden hand pursued relentlessly.

Sang Shu fled all the way to the southern tip of Flower Skirt Island, shouting, “Master Cai Jing! Master Cai Jing! Please intervene! Ba Qi has killed your apprentice! Save my life!”

Boom!

A spell shot forth, shattering the golden hand.

Master Cai Jing appeared, bellowing at Ba Qi, “Captain of the Heroism Pirates, Flower Skirt Island is no place for your reckless actions!”

Ba Qi roared with laughter once more: "Cai Jing, this isn't your territory either. Do you even have the right to speak to me like this? I seriously doubt it!"

With that, Ba Qi soared into the air and charged toward Cai Jing.

Cai Jing stood his ground, unfazed, wielding his staff as he engaged Ba Qi in fierce combat.

Both ascended into the air with tacit understanding, ensuring their clash spared Flower Skirt Island.

Both gold level experts fought fiercely, locked in an evenly matched battle.

The onlookers watched, eyes glued to the spectacle.

After over a dozen exchanges, Cai Jing faltered first, crashing back to the ground.

Battle skill - Multi-Layered Air Pressure!

Ba Qi thrust both arms forward, unleashing waves of explosive air pressure.

Master Cai Jing swiftly raised his staff, conjuring a blue magic shield.

Each wave of air pressure, heavy as mountains, slammed Master Cai Jing downward, sending debris and smoke billowing into the sky.

Soon, dust and smoke obscured Master Cai Jing's figure.

When the haze cleared, Master Cai Jing was covered in dust but had avoided serious injury.

His magic shield had shattered, but the giant trumpet flowers surrounding him provided protection.

Massive trumpet flowers erupted from the ground—some as large as carriages, others towering as tall as a man.

Aiming at Ba Qi, they sprayed streams of multicolored potion.

Ba Qi attempted to block them, but his expression quickly changed. He realized the multicolored potion was highly toxic, capable of penetrating his battle qi defenses. It mixed into his battle qi, transforming it into other types.

Different battle qi clashed violently with one another.

“Cai Jing, your combat prowess is quite impressive.” Ba Qi voluntarily halted his attack and descended to the ground.

Master Cai Jing's face was pale as he stared at Ba Qi, “Leave now. Flower Skirt Island does not welcome the Heroism Pirates.”

Ba Qi laughed heartily, waved at him, and turned to depart, “I don't fancy staying here either. Once I've resupplied, I'll be on my way.”

Master Cai Jing remained silent, watching until Ba Qi's figure vanished before disappearing underground himself.

The two gold level experts had clashed, each demonstrating their prowess while recognizing the other's tenacity. Both chose to call it quits.

Late that night.

Beneath the clinic.

The teleportation light faded as Ba Qi arrived, “Long time no see, Physician.”

He greeted him with a casual wave.

Shuang Tu remained composed, nodding slightly before gesturing toward the examination room where Lan Zao had been checked earlier. "Go inside and get your body checked first. Make it quick. The fewer secret meetings we have, the fewer openings we give."

“You're still as cautious as ever.” Ba Qi entered the room, lying flat as the magic formation's light scanned his entire body.

“Your condition has worsened again.” Shuang Tu's tone was grave.

Ba Qi shrugged it off: "Escaping that hellhole of the Hanging Tower was already a huge win for me. All I want now is to fulfill my master's dying wish before I die, to spread the Air Pressure battle qi technique across the land."

"Speaking of which, that kid you sent me is truly a promising talent. He's perfectly suited for the Air Pressure battle qi technique. I almost felt reluctant to plant the Air Knot on him."

Shuang Tu replied, "He was an ‘accident.’ Your Air Knot is necessary to ensure the final outcome favors us."

Ba Qi asked, “Do you plan to nurture him?”

Shuang Tu stated, "We will indeed invest resources to train him intensively. We hope he can contribute to the Tranquil Hill espionage mission."

“Tranquil Hill...” Ba Qi chuckled, “That mission has failed over a dozen times, hasn't it?”

“More than that,” Shuang Tu shook his head, “even if it fails a hundred times, it remains necessary to execute!”

“Hey, I've done you a favor by testing Cai Jing this time. Now you owe me one,” Ba Qi said abruptly. "Pull that kid out of the Tranquil Hill mission. Honestly, I see real potential in him."

Shuang Tu shook his head, then nodded, “Even if the Tranquil Hill mission fails this time, Hen Fa might not die.”

“I've already made arrangements for what comes next. He'll be coaxed over to us and placed within the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. We're quite interested in the arms dealer backing that mercenary group. Personally, I think investigating the arms dealer's identity is far more reliable than investigating Tranquil Hill."

Ba Qi chuckled: "Hehehe, I agree with your assessment. An arms dealer... that would indeed be a major achievement."

Chapter 638: Air Cannon

Flower Skirt Island.

The underground plaza beneath Shuang Tu Clinic.

Lan Zao was training.

Each battle qi technique had its own cultivation method. The Water Grass battle qi technique involved swinging the arms, while the Air Pressure battle qi technique required patting.

Lan Zao, wrapped in layers of bandages, repeatedly slapped various parts of his body with his palms.

After each strike, strands of fresh battle qi emerged from the struck area.

Through continuous slapping, these strands accumulated, coalescing into streams of newly born battle qi that coursed through Lan Zao's body.

Ba Qi had also infused him with Air Pressure battle qi, but that had been generated within Ba Qi's own body. It was the same type of battle qi, so Lan Zao could barely use it. But ultimately, it wasn't his own; it was fundamentally a foreign battle qi.

Only battle qi generated through his own body's training could be fully harnessed by Lan Zao.

Before long, crimson bloodstains seeped through the white bandages binding Lan Zao's body.

His injuries hadn't healed. Ba Qi's forceful infusion had inflicted particularly severe damage within him.

In truth, each strike to his own body triggered excruciating pain, without exception.

Yet Lan Zao's expression remained resolute, his gaze unwaveringly firm, showing not a trace of fear or compromise.

He was genuinely provoked.

Earlier, while training normally, he had been ambushed by Ba Qi, subjected to a barrage of battle qi, and barely escaped with his life. After regaining consciousness, he witnessed the clash between Ba Qi and Cai Jing, two gold level cultivators, at the medical clinic.

Though confined to his hospital bed and unable to see their exchange clearly, the sheer scale of a gold level clash sent his anxiety skyrocketing.

“Even with my awakened bloodline and advancement, I'm still only iron level.”

“Compared to gold level, I'm utterly defenseless!”

"According to Shuang Tu's assessment, I should be able to reach silver level. Once I reach silver level, even if I can't defeat Ba Qi, I might be able to escape. I could gain some initiative against such an existence."

The old Lan Zao never possessed this kind of fighting spirit. Bloodline awakening didn't just raise his ceiling; it also provided a powerful mental boost!

“You can rest now.” Shuang Tu had been observing the entire time, monitoring Lan Zao's physical condition in real-time through the alchemy components.

Having developed a degree of trust in Shuang Tu, Lan Zao immediately halted his cultivation upon receiving the reminder.

Shuang Tu began unwinding his bandages.

Once removed, Lan Zao's body was covered in a gruesome mess of blood and gore.

Shuang Tu guided him into the magic formation, activated it, and bathed him in healing light.

Both his internal and external injuries received some treatment, though they were far from healed.

Shuang Tu explained, “Today marks a critical phase in your battle qi conversion. If you were fully healed before completing it, your newly formed body wouldn't fully align with your new battle qi.”

“Thus, your injured state actually benefits you.”

"The residual Air Pressure battle qi from Ba Qi will severely suppress your bodily instincts, preventing the emergence of the Water Grass battle qi. This will greatly aid your conversion going forward."

"Next, repeat the previous process. After five more cycles, your conversion should be complete."

Lan Zao nodded, committing Shuang Tu's words to memory and following the instructions.

After five cycles, the battle qi within his body had completely been converted. Not only was it all Air Pressure battle qi, but this battle qi was now generated entirely by his own body. The foreign battle qi infused by Ba Qi had either been consumed or channeled away.

“You've succeeded, but this is only the beginning. Here.” Shuang Tu handed Lan Zao a crystal ball.

“What is this?” Lan Zao asked as he took it.

“It contains records on the Air Pressure battle qi,” Shuang Tu explained.

Lan Zao cut him off: "Have you forgotten? I can't read. Especially not snow elf words."

“I know,” Frost Tu nodded. "That's why the crystal ball contains magic projections. When the projections play, they'll also emit sound. You must continuously channel battle qi into it to activate it. The moment you stop channeling, the orb ceases to function."

“Though this alchemy artifact is rudimentary, its contents were carefully selected for you. They hold great value.”

"Your training regimen will stem from this material. Study it thoroughly. Pay special attention to the final section, it holds a surprise."

“Train diligently.”

Shuang Tu patted Lan Zao's shoulder and departed.

He harbored no concerns about Lan Zao's training progress.

Left alone in the underground plaza, Lan Zao began examining the crystal ball's contents.

The magic projection revealed a human discussing and demonstrating the cultivation methods and basic applications of the Air Pressure battle qi technique.

Compared to other battle qi techniques, Air Pressure battle qi possessed one distinct advantage: its exceptional proficiency in compressing air.

Upon seeing this, Lan Zao experienced a sudden flash of insight, connecting it to the fish bladder organ within his own body.

The magic projection continued, introducing another human.

This individual bore a striking resemblance to Lan Zao's current form, clearly another fighter possessing the Fish Bladder bloodline.

The human fighter meticulously taught the intricate coordination between Air Pressure battle qi and one's physical structure.

One explanation aligned perfectly with Lan Zao's earlier insight: compressing air within the fish bladder.

This approach offered numerous advantages.

The first advantage was that while the fish bladder's maximum volume remained unchanged, the stored air volume increased severalfold. In airless environments, this dramatically extended one's survival time.

The second benefit was that it expanded the fighter's upper and lower limits for buoyancy control.

The third advantage was enabling deeper integration with other related battle skills.

The human fighter stated plainly, “The Air Pressure battle qi technique complements the Fish Bladder bloodline exceptionally well, it's practically the perfect pairing.”

As Lan Zao watched the final segment of the magic projection, he discovered the “surprise” Shuang Tu had mentioned.

Battle skill - Air Cannon.

This battle skill proved exceptionally practical for the Fish Bladder bloodline. To be precise, once the Fish Bladder bloodline was combined with the Air Pressure battle qi technique, the Air Cannon battle skill became an essential component! Its recommended rating was the highest possible: 100.

Most fighters using this technique had to bend their arms, palms pressed together, continuously compressing air within the spherical space formed by their hands.

Once compressed, they enveloped it with battle qi to form an air cannonball, which was then hurled to strike the enemy.

For fighters, only those at the gold level could manifest battle qi directly from thin air. Thus, the minimum requirement for this Air Cannon battle skill was gold level.

But for Lan Zao, it's different.

He could continuously compress air within his fish bladder, far more convenient than using external palms, highly covert, and with the massive advantage of pre-storage.

Most crucially, compressed air stored long-term within the fish bladder would become an integral part of Lan Zao's body.

When Lan Zao released this compressed air, it's akin to hurling his own hair or arm - this was the important point!

This meant that Lan Zao could fill compressed air with battle qi and launch it even at the bronze level.

The long-range battle qi strike capability possessed by fighters only at the gold level battle qi was available to Lan Zao at the bronze level.

The magic projection clearly demonstrated how the human fighter practiced shooting air cannons, detailing specific techniques and precautions.

What truly astonished Lan Zao was the human fighter's display of numerous projectile cores.

These were alchemy weapons.

Projectile cores were much smaller than conventional bullets, with numerous varieties. When paired with the Air Cannon, they produced various attack effects.

For instance, combining a signal projectile with an Air Cannon shell created a signal flare.

Similarly, pairing a guidance projectile with an Air Cannon shell allowed Lan Zao to craft air missiles capable of turning mid-flight, adjusting direction, and tracking targets.

Finally, the human fighter elaborated on how to utilize these projectiles and how to practice for optimal results. He even demonstrated the combat effectiveness of some projectiles.

After watching the entire demonstration, Lan Zao fell silent.

Regarding the Air Pressure battle qi technique, he felt he could master it through War Merchant's database, it wasn't particularly rare.

The same applied to the Air Cannon battle skill.

But what Shuang Tu offered went far beyond that.

He also provided Lan Zao with extensive foundational knowledge, the most effective training methods, comprehensive precautions, and insights from predecessors.

He even demonstrated the projectiles themselves. Undoubtedly, these alchemy artifacts would vastly enrich and enhance Lan Zao's capabilities.

None of this was available through the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

“The Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's intelligence network is incredibly powerful!”

“They managed to find the most suitable cultivation materials for me in such a short time.”

“And they were all demonstrated by a human fighter.”

"They'll likely provide me with the projectile cores they showed at the end too. Otherwise, there'd be no point in them showing it."

“Perhaps this organization wasn't established by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord alone. Behind it, there should be other nobles and powerful figures from the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.”

Lan Zao wasn't a strategist, but even he sensed the formidable strength of the underground organization Shuang Tu belonged to through this crystal ball.

“Our cooperation with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord was truly wise. At least for me, the assistance I've received has been immense."

“Once I've mastered my cultivation, I'm sure I'll be able to offer even greater support to the commander.”

With renewed anticipation, Lan Zao composed himself and began a new training regimen, following the demonstration within the crystal ball.

This scene was transmitted through the alchemy apparatus, landing squarely within Shuang Tu's field of vision.

“Seems he's picked up on a hint of something unusual,” Shuang Tu murmured with a smile.

This was intentional.

Through the crystal ball, he “accidentally” revealed the organization's formidable power to Lan Zao.

People were drawn to strength.

In the future, Lan Zao's frequent use of projectile cores would also forge a strong material connection with Shuang Tu's organization, deepening his dependence.

Following this trajectory, Shuang Tu was confident he could cultivate Lan Zao as an asset, later infiltrating him into the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps for reconnaissance and other covert operations.

Shuang Tu remained unaware of the survivors' true connections. To him, Lan Zao was merely a low-ranking member recruited on Snake Rat Island, possessing only tenuous loyalty to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, easily swayed.

Chapter 639: Gratifying Progress

Seeing Lan Zao immersed in the new training regimen, Shuang Tu summoned another magic recording to study repeatedly.

The footage captured the entire battle between Ba Qi and Cai Jing.

Shuang Tu paused the magic recording at the final scene, his brow furrowing as he scrutinized it with heightened focus.

As the dust settled, giant trumpet flowers stood towering around Magician Cai Jing, continuously spewing streams of multicolored potions. Beneath where the flowers took root, the earth's surface cracked open, revealing a hidden subterranean chamber.

Shuang Tu recognized Cai Jing's trumpet flowers and understood the effects of the mixed potions.

“Each giant trumpet flower costs at least 100 gold to create, with daily maintenance expenses of roughly one gold.”

“Cai Jing has planted over a dozen here, and there should be more underground.”

“After arriving on Flower Skirt Island, he traveled across the island for over a month before settling in the southern region. Since then, he's lived in seclusion, publicly claiming to conduct alchemy experiments.”

“But even an alchemy laboratory wouldn't require cultivating this many giant trumpet flowers.”

"Investing such immense resources into defenses... it seems my earlier suspicions were correct. Cai Jing has discovered that legacy and has been secretly developing it all along."

Shuang Tu had been lurking on Flower Skirt Island even before Cai Jing arrived.

Though expelled from the Alchemy Guild, Cai Jing remained the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's most notorious forger, every move he made drew scrutiny.

His disguise was convincing; to avoid suspicion, he even recruited a local youngster as his potion apprentice.

Yet he could not deceive the intelligence organization to which Shuang Tu belonged. Through subtle clues, the organization had long suspected Cai Jing was probing the Flower Skirt Island's legacy.

This legacy originated from a legendary druid, it was the druid who transformed the uninhabited wasteland into the present-day Flower Skirt Island.

Approaching Cai Jing and attempting to seize this legacy was Shuang Tu's most significant mission.

Thus, this time, Shuang Tu employed Ba Qi to probe Cai Jing's true intentions, conveniently eliminating Sang Shu's son in the process.

The latter was Cai Jing's sole apprentice on Flower Skirt Island!

“Now, we simply await Cai Jing recruiting a new apprentice,” Shuang Tu murmured, his eyes gleaming with a sinister light.

He had been preparing for this moment for a long time.

As the proprietor of Flower Skirt Island's largest medical clinic, Shuang Tu commanded the largest pool of druid and magician apprentices.

This was the inherent racial advantage of elves.

Their innate aptitude for magic was far more widespread than among humans, dwarves, or other races.

According to Shuang Tu's calculations, Cai Jing wouldn't recruit silver level apprentices, such a rank already posed a threat to gold level practitioners.

But bronze level and iron level apprentices? Shuang Tu had the most under his command. Not only that, he had secretly cultivated and supported several promising seeds outside his direct influence.

Shuang Tu gazed toward the southern part of the island, murmuring, "If there's no opportunity, create one. May this time yield favorable progress."

At the same time.

"The opportunity has been presented. Whether you seize it rests solely with you. You may leave." Cai Jing delivered his final instruction to Sang Shu.

“Yes, sir.” Sang Shu withdrew, “I shall see this matter through!”

Among all the silver level individuals on Flower Skirt Island, he maintained the closest ties with Cai Jing.

This was primarily because his son had been chosen by Cai Jing to serve as a potion apprentice for a period.

During this time, Sang Shu also procured vast quantities of alchemy materials and magic plant seeds for Cai Jing.

Backed by the gold level expert, he gradually rose to become the highest-ranking silver level administrator on Flower Skirt Island.

But now, his son was dead!

He himself had narrowly escaped death at Ba Qi's hands, had Cai Jing not intervened.

He witnessed the battle between Cai Jing and Ba Qi. Ba Qi hadn't been defeated; ultimately, both Cai Jing and Ba Qi had ceased fighting. This outcome left Sang Shu deeply disappointed, but as a silver level, he could only bide his time and remember his hatred.

When Cai Jing summoned him, he dragged his wounded body and immediately accepted the new orders.

His son was dead, his enemy stood before him, yet he was powerless to avenge him.

Instead, he had to fulfill the task Cai Jing had assigned him: recruiting new potion apprentices.

"This time, Master Cai Jing wants to recruit twenty apprentices. Twenty... and that's the minimum requirement. As Master Cai Jing said, the more potion apprentices, the better!"

Sang Shu knew he couldn't accomplish this task alone.

Therefore, he decided to contact other administrators first.

The foremost among them was Shuang Tu, who ran the largest medical clinic on Flower Skirt Island.

“Twenty potion apprentices, and the more the better?” Upon meeting Sang Shu, Shuang Tu's face darkened at the news.

“On Flower Skirt Island, only you, Shuang Tu, have the largest number of druid apprentices and magician apprentices,” Sang Shu explained. “On the account of our long friendship, I earnestly seek your help.”

Sang Shu assumed Shuang Tu would refuse.

In his view, if Master Cai Jing lured away these apprentices under Shuang Tu's command, they'd likely never return to work at the clinic.

Little did he know, Shuang Tu was genuinely thrilled inside.

He'd assumed Master Cai Jing would recruit just one apprentice, maybe two or three at most.

“I never imagined he'd recruit so many at once... It seems Cai Jing is growing impatient.” After a moment's thought, Shuang Tu grasped the reason.

Cai Jing had been secretly probing the inheritance site, but after his recent clash with Ba Qi, he sensed his cover was blown. Now he was racing against time, desperate to seize that inheritance.

Though he grasped this, Shuang Tu didn't immediately agree. Instead, he shook his head in refusal: "Every apprentice here has undergone my rigorous screening and long-term cultivation. The entire clinic's operations depend on them. I won't agree to this partnership; it offers me nothing but harm."

Sang Shu hurriedly explained.

Shuang Tu remained unyielding at first, but as Sang Shu kept raising the stakes, his tone softened.

Sang Shu, energized, pressed on, bargaining with Shuang Tu.

He knew securing Shuang Tu's cooperation meant completing over half the mission!

Half an hour later, Sang Shu and Shuang Tu reached an agreement. Though the price paid was steep, Sang Shu had ultimately achieved his objective.

Sang Shu left the clinic with a sense of triumph.

Shuang Tu was equally pleased. Sang Shu's initiative to negotiate had played right into his hands: “This time, we've made truly substantial progress.”

Meanwhile, in the distant Blood Kin City, Zi Di had also made breakthroughs in her research on the alchemy puppet fragments.

These fragments and shards originated from the battle in the Cold Wind Valley.

After their ambush failed, the gold level fighter and light magician had retreated, abandoning numerous alchemy puppets to hinder the enemy's advance.

After these puppets were destroyed, Zi Di collected all the remaining fragments.

Upon arriving in Blood Kin City, Zi Di first took over the shop, then established a partnership with the Axe Gang. Following that, she observed the market and began researching these spoils of war.

“This is a magic model for the Freezing Spell...” Zi Di held a small, round bead in her hand.

The bead was ice-blue and translucent, revealing countless intricate magic runes shimmering faintly within.

“It's hard to imagine what kind of alchemy technology could condense a Freezing magic model into such a tiny space,” Zi Di murmured in genuine awe, despite her limited alchemy knowledge.

“This is the very culprit responsible for our heaviest casualties!”

When the alchemy puppets were destroyed, they often detonated, instantly freezing enemies caught off guard within blocks of ice.

Even shattering the ice proved futile. For in the instant of the explosion, they were frozen to death.

As for resurrection methods, Zi Di possessed none.

The most basic Resurrection required at least a bishop clergyman to cast.

"Thankfully we acquired the Small Thunder God Flying Hammer. Its attack effect prevented all the alchemy puppets from detonating. Otherwise, our casualties would have been far heavier."

“If my guess is correct, this should be a modified version of the Ice Explosion Orb,” Zi Di's eyes flashed with sharp insight.

The Ice Explosion Orb was a throwing weapon roughly the size of an adult's fist. It could create an explosion, instantly freezing and killing enemies within a certain range.

Although the orb in Zi Di's hand was only the size of a baby's fist, the similarities between the two were striking.

Over time, Zi Di had gained deeper insight into the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's affairs.

She understood: the kingdom's military alchemy technology stemmed almost entirely from frost-based magic.

The Ice Explosion Orb's origin lay in the Freezing Spell.

“The Thin Ice Wall Shield used by Teng Dong Lang in his heroic duel evolved from the Ice Wall Spell.”

"And the Ice Spike Bayonets widely issued throughout the kingdom are alchemy weapons developed from the Ice Spear Spell. In peacetime, they function as spearheads or daggers for thrusting attacks. In critical moments, they can be launched as projectiles. Upon striking a target, they often inflict penetrating wounds."

The Ice Explosion Orb, Thin Wall Ice Shield, and Ice Spike Bayonet are all signature equipment of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's military.

"So, this small Ice Explosion Orb in my hand is actually a military product? Developed from the Ice Explosion Orb specifically for alchemy puppets? Was it developed specifically for alchemy puppets, based on the Ice Explosion Orb?” Zi Di couldn't help but speculate.

“Could the ones ambushing us be military personnel?”

Zi Di shook her head slightly, dismissing the possibility.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Ice Sculpture Kingdom's military not only lacked conflict but actually collaborated.

The Li Jian Clan, currently their partner, controlled the Second Navy.

“If not the military, then the most likely scenario is that the enemy obtained this military level item through some other means.”

“Perhaps certain black markets?” Zi Di speculated silently.

If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could leverage the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord's connections to procure “decommissioned warships” from the kingdom's black market, why couldn't others obtain these small Ice Explosion Orbs?

“Another possibility is that these small Ice Explosion Orbs are privately modified,” Zi Di's thoughts raced.

“We need to investigate...”

For her, the best outcome would be that no such goods were sold on the market, and the small Ice Explosion Orbs were indeed privately modified products.

In that case, this lead would be highly valuable.

Given future opportunities, Zi Di and her companions could follow the trail to uncover the true identity of the enemies who ambushed them.

But if these small Ice Explosion Orbs were already circulating widely on the black market, then this lead would be useless.

Chapter 640: Zi Di’s Advancement

The thought of conducting an investigation made Zi Di frown slightly.

The current situation was that she couldn’t find anyone reliable to handle the inquiry.

Zi Di sighed inwardly, there were still too few capable people she could rely on. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had grown remarkably since its founding, and when assembled, it was truly a gathering of talent. Yet now, scattered across operations, even a slight expansion left them stretched thin.

In truth, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had always lacked intelligence specialists.

No matter how advanced the alchemy components or reconnaissance puppets, they could never replace human agents.

Every major organization maintained an intelligence apparatus. The Lion Dragon Mercenary Group primarily acquired intelligence through external purchases, with the Thieves' Guild serving as the survivors' main source of information.

This arrangement was clearly inappropriate.

From an outsider's perspective, the Lion Dragon Mercenary Group appeared merely a minor power, its strongest members barely reaching silver level, lacking even a single gold level individual.

For such factions or organizations, lacking an intelligence division was perfectly normal. Maintaining an intelligence department carried significant costs, which small factions struggled to bear. From a cost-benefit perspective, outsourcing to the Thieves' Guild was highly advantageous.

Yet Zi Di firmly believed that the current small-scale faction was merely the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' starting point. In the future, their organization would inevitably grow into a major power, even becoming a superpower was entirely possible.

This conviction stemmed not from wishful thinking or overreach, but from one primary reason: the Bloodcore.

With bloodlines, anything was possible!

"For us, establishing an intelligence department is essential. Intelligence personnel must be utterly loyal and reliable, so the sooner we establish it, the better."

“But that won't be my responsibility.”

"I have no clue about intelligence matters, nor is it my area of expertise. This issue is best left for the commander to consider!" Zi Di pondered inwardly.

For the survivors, the immediate priority was contacting the Bai Zhen Clan and building connections with the upper echelons of the Sheng Ming Empire to atone for their sins.

The secondary priority was Zi Di's business plan.

Funding was crucial for any endeavor.

The economy was the foundation for everything.

Establishing an intelligence division would demand enormous expenses. Offering monetary compensation to atone with the Empire's leadership was also a viable approach.

Though the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps held substantial funds, their loot from the Two Eyes Island skirmish had made them rich, their current expenditures were significant and would only grow.

Sitting on their laurels was not an option.

While they still held capital, aggressively expanding, investing, and establishing a stable cash flow was the wisest course.

Even if atonement failed, this foundation would allow the survivors to scrape by for a time.

The Ice Sculpture Kingdom lay isolated offshore, distant from the Sheng Ming Empire. Should the Sheng Ming Empire seek trouble with the survivors in the future, this location could still serve as a temporary refuge.

Once Zi Di successfully expanded her commercial empire within the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, the benefits extended far beyond mere capital. Access to various scarce resources would become significantly easier. Just as before in Snow Bird Harbor, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps leveraged the city lord's connections to secretly acquire vast quantities of rare resources, far exceeding what could be obtained on the open market.

This applied not only to materials but also to human resources.

The dragonman youngster's victory over Teng Dong Lang had made the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps renowned throughout the Ice Sculpture Kingdom.

Should Zi Di's commercial plan succeed, the group's financial standing would gain widespread recognition.

With both fame and fortune, more people would acknowledge the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps as an organization. Consequently, the survivors could leverage the Ice Sculpture Kingdom to access better human resources, such as accomplished alchemists, masterful pharmacists, or genuine intelligence operatives willing to pledge allegiance.

Most talents only became recruitable once an organization possessed sufficient strength.

As the saying went: a wise man finds the best opportunities available, and a wise bird chooses the best tree to nest in.

Without sufficient strength, talented individuals simply would not consider them.

Having a rare talent like Bu Quan join the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was merely an exception.

“Speaking of which, we owe Bu Quan a debt of gratitude this time. She optimized the production process for the dragon rider potion, boosting output severalfold.”

“For her, such refinements are merely foundational skills, right?”

Zi Di knew she herself could never achieve such feats.

Not long ago, when she learned the news through a letter, she was genuinely delighted.

The significant increase in output gave her greater confidence in implementing her business plans.

“All my previous efforts to persuade her were not in vain.”

Zi Di admired Bu Quan's alchemy talent deeply, yet without a trace of envy.

As a merchant, she understood the concept of human resources very well.

With Bu Quan joining them, it was a tremendous boost for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. The current profits were already substantial, and they were likely to grow even higher in the future!

Of course, Zi Di was well aware of Bu Quan's affection for the dragonman youngster.

But the ghost girl placed greater faith in the bond between herself and the dragonman youngster. Their experiences on Mystifying Monster Island and the secrets of the Bloodcore formed the foundation of their relationship, and also the distance between the dragonman youngster and Bu Quan.

“The potion market in Blood Kin City has been thoroughly observed these past few days.”

“Next, we'll select several individuals to remain at the shop in Blood Kin City.”

This was part of Zi Di's business plan, designed to strengthen her connections with each sales point.

Those chosen to accompany Zi Di were all handpicked by her.

Some were mercenaries who joined earlier on Snake Rat Island and Two Eyes Island. Others were snow elves recruited at Snow Bird Harbor. Most were humans and snow elves, though there were also oxmen, dogmen, and water pigmen.

Having their own people embedded to oversee operations was essential; relying solely on the Li Jian Clan's personnel risked being manipulated.

Zi Di decided to station one dogman and one snow elf.

The snow elf could blend into the environment, while the dogman maintained distance, ensuring loyalty to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Over-integration was unwise; it invited manipulation and defection. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were outsiders; this was a snow elf kingdom. Dispatching snow elves to every sales point alone was unsafe.

Overemphasizing loyalty while becoming too isolated from the outside world was equally unwise. Failure to integrate would hinder business expansion. Beyond financial losses, it could spark severe conflicts—even leading to violent incidents like local residents storming shops.

Opening branches was no simple task.

Zi Di had to meticulously construct the team structure, building a moat of authority to lay a solid foundation for long-term, stable business.

Though lacking hands-on experience in this area, Zi Di came from a merchant family and had observed operational methods from her father. Having been immersed in this environment, Zi Di possessed abundant confidence in herself.

"In every city we reach, I'll leave behind several people. By the time we reach the Ice Sculpture Kingdom, our sales network will be largely established."

"Zong Ge disdained the combat strength of the people I selected. Back in the Cold Wind Valley, he even considered using them as bait. Little did he know, I had secretly tested each of them. They are all talents who can become the cornerstone of my business plan."

Forming a partnership with the Li Jian Clan was an absolute breakthrough for the survivors!

None of the survivors grasped its significance, except Fei She. Upon hearing of Zi Di's success, he trembled with excitement, his eyes gleaming as he sighed deeply: Looking at Zi Di before him felt like seeing the old president!

Leveraging the Li Jian Clan, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps spread like vines grafted onto a sturdy trunk, drawing nourishment and flourishing rapidly. This allowed the survivors to leapfrog their initial vulnerable phase, saving precious time, seizing critical opportunities, and positioning themselves more effectively to capture market share across the entire kingdom.

Blood Kin City and Snow Bird Harbor were quite close. The two individuals Zi Di had selected were considered relatively weaker. In the future, when they reached more important cities, Zi Di would select those she deemed more promising to stay behind.

“Once the first caravan from Snow Bird Harbor successfully delivers its goods to Blood Kin City, we can leave,” Zi Di planned.

“Of course, we must remain cautious when departing.”

They were still unaware of the ambush awaiting them.

"Therefore, capital alone is utterly insufficient. In fact, excessive wealth can become a source of trouble. Military might is the true safeguard of everything."

Putting her scheming aside for now, Zi Di retrieved a soul crystal and began her daily cultivation practice.

As she absorbed its essence, the crystal steadily shrank in size.

A peculiar fluctuation emanated from Zi Di's body.

The ghost girl's body trembled slightly. At that moment, her magician level advanced by one level.

She advanced from bronze level to iron level!

Zi Di felt not the slightest surprise.

Her promotion to an iron level necromancer had long been within her expectations.

She had been an iron level magician in life. After death, her soul had been reshaped by negative energy and, through Cang Xu's Soul Summoning Spell, successfully transformed into a ghost.

Her life level had always been iron level. However, her original magic pool remained within her body, while her magician level reset to zero.

She had no choice but to rebuild her magic pool and recommence her magician training.

Her prior experience and iron level life level provided immense assistance to her magician cultivation.

“I'm finally back to the iron level,” Zi Di murmured softly.

Transcendent cultivation usually involves gradual accumulation leading to a qualitative leap.

But considering the time, Zi Di's cultivation period wasn't actually that long. The primary reason she felt it was so long and agonizing was her urgent need for a higher magician level.

Because she was constantly pretending to be a silver level magician while relying on her bronze level magician level, the pressure was immense.

Now that she was iron level again, the hidden pressure had eased considerably.

“On the other hand, it also conserves Pearl Bubbles.”

This was easy to understand.

Pretending to be a silver level magician as a bronze level was much harder than someone who was already an iron level.

“But my soul crystals are running low.”

As a ghost, soul-devouring was an instinctive act of sustenance. Doing so risked memory confusion, personality shifts, even loss of sanity and descent into madness, all negative states.

The fate of most ghosts was to succumb to this primal urge. The more they fed, the deeper they sank into instinct, corrupted by negative energy until they became beasts that despised all life.

Soul crystals were different. They were utterly pure, the perfect substitute. Not only did they satisfy hunger, but they also enhanced a ghost's foundation and strengthened its spirit power without any adverse effects.

Chapter 641: Snake Shadow Ghost and Rat Eye Ghost

Zi Di's current soul crystals had fallen below twenty.

Since obtaining them for the first time, her supply had never been replenished.

In truth, she had no way to replenish them.

Each crystal used depleted her reserves, leaving her with less than twenty now.

The resources Zi Di had gathered for undead cultivation came almost entirely from Cang Xu.

Honestly, her rapid advancement to an iron level necromancer was largely thanks to him.

“To maintain this cultivation pace before my official resurrection, I must find new undead resources,” Zi Di frowned again, sinking into distress.

Gathering undead resources was no simple task.

Necromancers had always been in a precarious position, hunted by the entire world. Just finding reliable channels was incredibly difficult. Numerous factions secretly laid traps, spreading false information about black markets to lure necromancers into revealing themselves.

Zi Di currently had urgent matters to attend to and simply didn't have time for this.

“Acting under my current identity carries too great a risk. If exposed, the consequences for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would be catastrophic, consequences we cannot bear.”

“Therefore, I must still entrust this to Cang Xu.”

Zi Di sighed softly within.

She harbored deep trust and expectations for her comrades.

On one hand, this mindset had been forged by their arduous struggle for survival on Mystifying Monster Island. On the other, it stemmed from her merchant nature.

Merchants excelled at networking, leveraging connections to mobilize resources and generate profits.

'Just because I couldn't do something didn't mean others couldn't.'

'If others could, through cooperation, I too could benefit and transcend my own limitations.'

This was the mindset of a merchant.

It was precisely this mindset that led Zi Di to swiftly agree when the dragonman youngster had persuaded her to share the Bloodcore with others.

This decision stemmed not only from her deep affection for the youngster but also from her own understanding and values.

Zi Di began meditating, gathering magic power to fill her expanded magic pool after the upgrade.

Though magicians could harness external elemental energy, their own magic pool also had to be replenished. It was like a merchant's capital, the larger the principal, the more leverage they gained.

"Compared to bronze level, my iron level undead magic power has expanded over twofold. It's even twice as large as my fire-element magic pool in life, and my spirit has grown stronger too," Zi Di silently compared.

This was a unique cultivation experience one most magicians would never possess.

“But compared to my bloodline-purified body, my current meditation efficiency still falls far short!”

The gap was quite evident.

Zi Di's bloodline had been purified to 100% purity, matching Lan Zao's extreme refinement. Unlike Lan Zao, however, her Gunpowder Flower bloodline was gold level, surpassing the silver level Fish Bladder bloodline.

Originally, Zi Di had planned to create a passive meditation environment to continuously elevate her body's magician level.

Unfortunately, an unexpected incident occurred with Lan Zao, forcing the dragonman youngster to control the Deep Sea Monster Fish to pursue him, and taking her body with him.

Before parting, both had considered having Zi Di take her body on the journey to the Ice Sculpture Capital.

But after thorough deliberation, they both deemed the risk too great. If discovered, the truth about Zi Di's undead identity would be exposed. If danger struck along the way and her body was destroyed, the consequences would be even worse.

Judging by the ambush in the Cold Wind Valley, the dragonman youngster and Zi Di's concerns were justified.

They had made the right choice, leaving Zi Di's body safely aboard the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Through the Soul Send Spell, Zi Di could control her body, though this wasn't true resurrection. Meditation remained difficult, plagued by numerous interferences. Even so, the efficiency of meditation in this state far surpassed her current capabilities.

The gold level bloodline was truly exceptional!

Having experienced it, Zi Di found it unforgettable.

“My current ghost life form likely caps out at iron level.” With more experience, Zi Di could now make a fairly accurate assessment.

Generally speaking, transforming a normal life into an undead ghost didn't necessarily mean an increase in aptitude.

Cang Xu was an exception because he was selected by the soul aspect inheritance hidden within the oil painting. He had been tested and found to possess the aptitude for cultivating necromancy magic.

Zi Di hadn't directly accessed the undead inheritance, but her own dual cultivation achievements proved she was qualified to practice necromancy.

Yet, through practice, Zi Di grew increasingly aware: her aptitude for this path was also limited.

Zi Di wasn't disheartened. In fact, she felt content.

It was precisely this aptitude that saved her from complete death on Mystifying Monster Island. Though now transformed into undead life, a sliver of hope for resurrection remained.

“Besides, my undead aptitude isn't without room for improvement.”

"Once I find the answer, and discover the nature of my undead bloodline, then purify it, as long as my current bloodline concentration isn't 100%, there's potential for growth."

Zi Di could test bloodlines with potions, but her methods were limited to living beings, undead were beyond her expertise.

Cang Xu shared all the undead inheritance he'd acquired from Weeping Pine Island.

Yet these teachings were either foundational or elementary, lacking any bloodline knowledge.

The undead school had developed spells capable of directly detecting undead bloodlines and cultivation aptitude, but such spells were recorded in intermediate undead inheritances. Cang Xu hadn't even managed to obtain them from Jian Gui yet.

Zi Di's meditation efficiency was low.

Partly due to bloodline limitations, partly due to a lack of negative energy.

Typically, suitable locations for necromancers to cultivate are graveyards, mass burial grounds, ancient battlefields, and the like.

Yet she now found herself in the very heart of Blood Kin City.

Fortunately, she still possessed undead resources that could supply negative energy.

This approach was actually quite wasteful. These undead resources could easily serve as alchemy materials or similar uses. Extracting the negative energy for meditation rendered these resources largely useless. Even if some residual value remained, it would be drastically diminished.

Moreover, to conceal the fluctuations of negative energy and prevent her secret from being exposed, Zi Di had to lay down a magic formation for camouflage.

She wasn't worried about her identity being revealed through necromancer cultivation.

Her primary confidence lay in the Deceptive Disguise spell.

To date, this spell had never failed her.

Still, it was a resource drain. The cost was incalculable - the Pearl Bubble was a god level material!

By the time Zi Di replenished her newly expanded magic pool, it was deep into the night.

Yet she felt alert and vibrant. As a ghost, she required no sleep. Only when her spirit reached utter exhaustion would she slip into slumber.

Zi Di summoned several ghosts and began modifying them.

From the undead inheritance obtained from Cang Xu, Zi Di specialized in cultivating the soul aspect.

The core content of the elementary soul aspect inheritance centered on necromancers modifying basic ghosts, enhancing them comprehensively or in specialized areas.

This formed the foundation for a necromancer to build an army single-handedly.

Zi Di modified only two types of ghosts: snake souls and rat souls.

Nearly all these ghosts originated from Snake Rat Island.

On Snake Rat Island, she obtained vast numbers of ghosts by slaying tide snakes and smelt rats.

Following the methods in the elementary undead inheritance, she was transforming snake ghosts into snake shadow ghosts and rat ghosts into rat eye ghosts.

Snake shadow ghosts could blend into shadows for concealment. They could covertly track targets, providing the necromancer with markings within a certain range.

Rat eye ghosts enhance reconnaissance capabilities.

“Reaching iron level has significantly boosted my soul-altering abilities.”

The effects of leveling up were immediate and palpable.

Soon, Zi Di's magic power reserves were depleted.

She meditated once more, drawing negative energy from the undead materials.

Once again, her reserves were quickly exhausted.

After several rounds, the undead materials were heavily depleted, but she gained over twenty iron level modified ghosts.

“Thankfully, modifying ghosts is necromancy, not alchemy.” Zi Di exhaled a cloud of stale breath.

After hesitating, she decided to activate Deceptive Disguise Formation.

She retrieved the formation diagram panels and swiftly assembled them.
