She had practiced this step extensively beforehand, preparing for future emergencies that might require immediate adjustments to Deceptive Disguise spell's actual effects.

Compared to the Bloodline Purification Formation, constructing the Deceptive Disguise Formation was less complex. Back then, Zi Di and Cang Xu had completed the first such formation.

Once the portable formation diagram panels became available, Zi Di immediately began secretly assembling a second Deceptive Disguise Formation.

This too was a precaution against unforeseen circumstances.

If the primary Deceptive Disguise Formation were to fail, leaving survivors without a functioning one when urgently needed, the consequences would be dire.

The ordeal on Mystifying Monster Island had profoundly altered the survivors' approach to action. Those who had survived now sought the utmost preparedness. Only through such thoroughness could they maintain a higher margin for error in this perilous environment and secure greater chances of survival.

After successfully activating the formation, Zi Di gradually channeled all the snake shadow ghosts and rat eye ghosts into it.

Zi Di knew the truth, these modified ghosts remained unchanged. But she was certain the Deceptive Disguise spell had been successfully cast. To the unsuspecting, these ghosts appeared as nothing more than scouting puppets.

To minimize the consumption of pearl bubbles and extend the spell's duration, the modified ghosts were disguised to closely resemble snakes and rats.

"I must conserve when possible. After all, Pearl Bubbles are running low," Zi Di sighed inwardly.

Compared to soul crystals, pearl bubbles were an absolute necessity for survivors, holding immense significance.

Zi Di wouldn't skimp where it mattered.

Like now, casting the Deceptive Disguise spell on these modified ghosts was essential. Using them directly risked discovery and major trouble.

Now, with the Deceptive Disguise spell, Zi Di could deploy them with confidence.

Moreover, as undead belonged to a newly established magician faction, even these lowly iron level ghosts held promising reconnaissance potential.

Take her previous battle in Cold Wind Valley: when she deployed Lan Lu's ghost, enemies never suspected a necromancer's hand at work, mistaking it for a combat-type alchemy puppet.

"If I could modify Lan Lu, my combat strength would increase dramatically. Even facing Zong Ge, I'd have a chance in direct combat."

At this stage, Zi Di wasn't quite ready to attempt modifying Lan Lu.

This ghost had been a two headed ogre magician in life, possessing immense potential. After its successful soul summoning, its room for growth was considerable.

Zi Di had devised numerous modification plans for it, but such alterations typically required substantial time. At this stage, she still needed Lan Lu's combat strength, making the timing inopportune.

“However, I could emulate that light magician and deploy large numbers of alchemy puppets.”

Zi Di's own combat prowess, constrained by her magician level, was limited.

Truthfully, she held little interest in combat herself.

Posing as a silver level, she typically relied on superior gear or spell scrolls to unleash barrages of firepower during battles.

Her foundational skills were solid, a testament to her training at the academy. But when it came to innate combat talent or brilliant improvisation in the heat of battle? That was almost entirely absent.

Zi Di herself understood her true interests and where her genuine talents lay.

She wasn't particularly fixated on her own combat abilities.

The battle in Cold Wind Valley had offered her some enlightenment.

After all, an enemy was often the best teacher.

“Compared to a standard magician, I can now control more alchemy puppets.”

After employing soul crystals and casting the Burden Alleviation Spell and Karma Reduction Spell, Zi Di's mental strain had significantly lessened. Her recent promotion to iron level had triggered a qualitative leap through quantitative accumulation, substantially expanding both her magic pool capacity and mental endurance threshold.

“Moreover, ghosts inherently excel in spirit power prowess.”

This disparity stemmed from their differing life forms.

“Still, acquiring combat-type alchemy puppets will involve Snow Bird Harbor.”

“Purchasing them within Blood Kin City feels unsettling.”

After all, Zi Di and her companions encountered an ambush while en route to Blood Kin City. The enemy's true identity remained unknown.

“I'll draft the letter now and have it delivered first thing tomorrow morning.”

“Hopefully this batch of alchemy puppets can arrive alongside the first shipment.”

“May it arrive in time.”

Zi Di's communication method with the Snow Bird Harbor branch was simple and straightforward: relying on messengers to swiftly travel via teleportation formations.

Tripleblade received Zi Di's letter at dawn.

At that moment, he was discussing recent expenditure matters for the mercenary group's funds with Fei She.

He had already learned of the ambush by mysterious powerful foes that Zi Di, Zong Ge, and their group had encountered in the Cold Wind Valley. Zi Di's plan to acquire a large number of alchemy puppets to enhance their combat strength was a good thing.

Tripleblade saw no reason to object and fully agreed.

“President Zi Di has once again become an iron level magician, truly excellent news.”

"We should inform Magician Bu Quan shortly. She likely knows more about alchemy puppets than we do. Perhaps she can offer better advice on selecting the most suitable models for us to purchase." Tripleblade remarked.

Then he sighed again: "But this means our expenses will increase significantly. What are we to do?"

He looked at Fei She with a worried expression.

Fei She smiled at him: "My stance remains unchanged, Tripleblade. Our current investment situation is sound. There's no need to worry excessively."

Chapter 642: Tripleblade’s Dream

Tripleblade himself was the leader of a mercenary group. He now remained in Snow Bird Harbor, efficiently managing the branch operations behind the scenes.

Yet his experience also had significant limitations.

His original mercenary group had been of modest rank and size, never handling expenditures of such staggering magnitude.

In this regard, he fell far short of Fei She.

Though Fei She appeared ordinary, he was a veteran of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, having handled and witnessed far more money than Tripleblade.

He too remained in Snow Bird Harbor, not accompanying Zi Di to the Ice Sculpture Capital.

His primary duty was overseeing Zi Di's commercial ventures. As the production hub for dragon rider potions, Snow Bird Harbor served as the engine of these ventures, requiring someone with business acumen to hold the fort.

However, Fei She faced a situation similar to Tripleblade's, both were held back by their combat limitations despite talents exceeding their transcendent level.

Tripleblade operated behind the scenes, with Chi Lai and Men Shi appearing as the public faces. Fei She's predicament was worse. As a mere mortal, advancing the business plans and executing commercial maneuvers required Tripleblade's intermediary role.

Seeing Tripleblade still frowning, Fei She sighed inwardly and pressed on with his persuasion.

If Fei She possessed sufficient strength, he wouldn't need Tripleblade to implement his strategies, he could push them through single-handedly. But reality dictated otherwise. He had to influence a more powerful figure to ensure the business plan remained unaffected.

Fei She stated, "You've seen how popular our dragon rider potion is in Snow Bird Harbor. Therefore, introducing this potion to other regions of the Ice Sculpture Kingdom will undoubtedly lead to massive sales."

“Thanks to Magician Bu Quan's refinement of the production process, our potion output has increased exponentially.”

“Our partnership with the Li Jian Clan has spared us the initial store setup work, providing immense assistance!”

“Now, we only need to await the first wave of returned capital to achieve positive cash flow.”

"As for the sudden expense of purchasing alchemy puppets, there's no need for concern. This falls entirely within our means. Based on my market observations and understanding of alchemy puppet pricing, the funds required represent less than one-tenth of our total capital."

This batch of alchemy puppets was for Zi Di’s exclusive use.

She already had numerous ghosts to manage, so she couldn’t handle too many puppets.

Despite Fei She’s reassurances, Tripleblade remained uneasy.

For a goblin like him, things that hadn't happened were merely considered plans, not reality.

Fei She read the room and sighed in resignation.

He had done his utmost to persuade Tripleblade, but the effect was limited.

With his wealth of life experience, Fei She understood that men like Tripleblade were pragmatic, seasoned by hands-on experience. Tripleblade himself had once been a successful leader of a mercenary group. These successful experiences had shaped Tripleblade, but they also bound him, narrowing his perspective and making him reluctant to trust outsiders' words.

Fei She ceased further persuasion. He understood that for someone like Tripleblade, no amount of persuasion could replace the power of reality speaking for itself!

Only when future developments proved Fei She's foresight correct would Tripleblade come to understand.

Fei She took his leave, while Tripleblade remained deeply troubled.

In stark contrast to his gloomy demeanor here, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps thrived!

The branch left behind in Snow Bird Harbor had, in this short span, elevated their mercenary rank once more. Their mission success rate stood at 100%, and their customer satisfaction rating was equally perfect!

Such achievements were nothing short of astonishing.

But a closer look at the missions they'd completed revealed the reason.

The tasks taken on by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps branch were all quite “small.” So “small” as to include helping with moving, repairing furniture, finding lost cats, slaughtering chickens and ducks, and so on.

Yet despite the “simplicity” of these tasks, the rewards were exceptionally generous, resulting in considerable income for the branch.

“All of this is thanks to the commander.” Tripleblade understood clearly that all this success stemmed from the dragonman youngster.

Ever since the dragonman youngster had publicly slain the gold level Teng Dong Lang in a heroic duel, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ fortunes had completely turned around.

Following that battle, the Bayonet Gang was directly annihilated by the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, prompting factions large and small to express goodwill toward the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Numerous factions proactively issued mercenary contracts, specifically requesting the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps by name. These assignments were remarkably easy yet offered exceptionally generous rewards.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' detachment successfully completed these missions with only a handful of low level transcendent individuals.

Not only did they earn substantial rewards, but the corps' rank also rose significantly.

“Though we're still a minor force, our leader can slay a gold level, so external factions almost universally regard us with respect.”

Tripleblade understood this treatment was exceptionally rare.

Having served as a mercenary leader for years with unwavering dedication, he had never received such favorable treatment. Nor had he witnessed any other mercenary group enjoying comparable regard.

It wasn't that the Sheng Ming Empire lacked powerful mercenaries.

Rather, the dragonman youngster's feat of defeating a gold level opponent as a silver level fighter was exceptionally rare.

Crucially, the dragonman youngster had vanquished the leader of the Bayonet Gang, a triumph of justice over evil! Such a righteous cause made public acceptance far easier.

The dragonman youngster's fame throughout the Ice Sculpture Capital stemmed not only from his astonishing achievements but also from the public goodwill earned through his righteous deeds.

Having become the true leader behind the scenes of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' branch, Tripleblade's motivation was fully ignited after such a smooth start.

Originally a mercenary leader himself, he had always harbored the ambition to lead his group to greater heights.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' current standing now matches that of the mercenary group he once commanded.

This string of successes fueled Tripleblade's work with boundless enthusiasm, filling each day with relentless drive. As he lay awake at night, his thoughts overflowed with both reflection and anticipation: Could it be that his long-held dream would finally be realized in this foreign land, through such an extraordinary path?

Thus, when Tripleblade witnessed such a massive expenditure, his heart skipped a beat, and intense worry gripped him.

He refused to let this promising situation crumble under his watch!

On one hand, it was his sense of responsibility. Appointed to secretly lead the mercenary company's branch, he certainly didn't want to botch it. On the other hand, this was his... one could say, his life's ambition. He deeply desired to see his mercenary company reach the pinnacle.

Of course, he knew the limits of his bloodline. Thus, in leading the Snow Bird Harbor detachment this time, he eagerly anticipated achieving his ambition indirectly through this unconventional approach.

Tripleblade was tense even though Fei She had offered extensive explanations during their conversation.

He was well aware of Fei She's background and had heard about his business acumen.

“But what if he miscalculated?”

“Better safe than sorry! Even if the odds are slim!”

“No, I have to do something. Even if it doesn't work, I have to act.”

Tripleblade knew he couldn't sit idle; he needed work to ease his anxiety.

But making money... had never been his forte.

He racked his brain: “To secure funds, it boils down to increasing income or cutting expenses.”

“For income, naturally, we'll take on more mercenary contracts.”

“As for cutting expenses...” Tripleblade's gaze fell on the ledger, which detailed nearly every financial transaction of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Currently, the largest expense was procurement.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps constantly purchased alchemy materials externally, in significant quantities and through numerous channels. The main sources were the six merchant alliances and contacts connected to the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord.

Among these, the largest expenditure was buying the dragon beasts and wyverns.

Tripleblade was unaware this was specifically for the dragonman youngster to absorb bloodlines. His alchemy knowledge was non-existent; he couldn't discern the trickery and genuinely believed these were ingredients for potions.

“These obviously can't be touched.” Tripleblade turned his attention to the second largest expenditure.

This portion of funds was allocated for purchasing equipment and items.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps now have money. Transcendent cultivation rarely involved sudden mutations; it required time to accumulate. Thus, acquiring a single high-quality piece of equipment could often lead to a dramatic surge in combat power.

Even before the duel with Teng Dong Lang, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had begun aggressively purchasing equipment.

After the duel, the procurement efforts didn't decrease. Instead, they increased further, fueled by the booming sales of the dragon rider potion.

The survivors genuinely craved high-end gear.

As the capital of alchemists, the Ice Sculpture Kingdom boasted a highly developed alchemy industry. Here, the survivors could more easily acquire equipment and items offering superior value for money.

“This is a bit excessive. We could scale it back a bit,” Tripleblade concluded after analysis, forming a different perspective.

In his view, the current focus on acquiring silver level gear was generally too high for most of the survivors.

After all, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps didn't have many silver level members. Among the true core members, only the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were silver level.

"Acquiring so many silver level items is unnecessary. Besides, there's also magician equipment. Among our own ranks, both magicians are necromancers—these items aren't suitable for them."

"Also, should we really be collecting gold level gear and items? We already have plenty of gold level equipment, and the defensive gold gear we truly need remains unavailable."

“Regarding this suggestion... I'll write to them shortly.”

Following this, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps proceeded to procure alchemy arrows, bullets, and other supplies.

Tripleblade had no objections on this front.

He'd witnessed the high level escape battle against the Bayonet Gang. That conflict had cost the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps dearly, their flagship, the Love Triangle, had been in the repair shop for days.

Tripleblade continued reading.

The third critical expense category was daily operations.

The largest portion was food cost!

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps purchased vast quantities of transcendent ingredients and employed numerous magic chefs to prepare transcendent food.

The current branch boasted a large number of transcendent individuals, and many of the newly recruited snow elf members possessed spellcasting abilities. The two squads currently in training underwent grueling daily drills, and these nutrient-rich meals ensured they wouldn't be worn out by the training.

“Wait, what's going on here?”

“The big guy's food expenses alone rival those of the Quick Knife Squad?”

Tripleblade stared wide-eyed, utterly baffled.

“That big oaf sure has a huge appetite, doesn't he?”

"I remember his appetite wasn't this massive. Could someone be secretly embezzling funds?"

Tripleblade couldn't sit still. He immediately set out to investigate personally.

Chapter 643: The Most Gifted Survivor

Tripleblade had long known that the big guy had ascended to the transcendent realm, and that his appetite had consequently skyrocketed.

Under the care of the dragonman youngster, the big guy could now automatically circulate the Mindless Sleeping Posture battle qi technique while sleeping, thus formally embarking on the path of cultivation.

After ascending to the bronze level, he had also trained aboard the ship alongside Zong Ge and the others.

His performance during training caught Zong Ge's attention, though the latter expressed regret, had the big guy been able to cultivate battle skills, he would undoubtedly have been a formidable weapon on the battlefield. Unfortunately, the big guy's intelligence wasn't up to par; he couldn't learn any battle skills.

"Advancing to the transcendent realm does indeed cause one's appetite to skyrocket. This is because the essence of life itself grows, demanding greater nourishment from the outside world. A mouse and an elephant require different foods and quantities. As the life level increases, so does the cost of sustaining vital functions."

“But shouldn't a transcendent's appetite stabilize after advancement? Why does it keep increasing?”

Tripleblade wanted to investigate this, not to skimp on the big guy's meals.

He would never do such a thing.

On one hand, Tripleblade knew: the old shipwright had entrusted the big guy to the commander on his deathbed, and the commander had always looked out for him. On the other hand, during their time on Mystifying Monster Island, it was the big guy who protected the newly built ship, and Tripleblade had indirectly received life-saving favors from him.

Tripleblade wasn't the type to bite the hand that fed him.

His concern was corruption. He'd witnessed it firsthand in the mercenary group he once led. Tripleblade understood that wherever profit existed, parasites bred if left unchecked.

Especially with the big guy's intellectual limitations, the survivors all knew he was easily deceived.

With no trustworthy subordinates available, Tripleblade resolved to investigate personally.

His method was straightforward, he confronted the big guy directly, demanding a clear explanation.

The big guy's response: "Eat? I like to eat."

“Not full... I want more food.”

"Father! I want Father more."

Tripleblade's attempts at communication proved fruitless; the big guy answered nothing like his questions.

“The big guy's grown taller again, but his IQ seems inversely proportional to his height, it appears to have dropped further?” Tripleblade sighed in resignation.

Over this period, the big guy had shot up at least another 0.5 meters. Had the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps not acquired their new flagship, the forecastle of their previous vessel, the Love Triangle, would have been too cramped for him.

Noticing it was nearing noon, Tripleblade decided to stay and observe personally.

“Just eat. Don't look over here.”

The goblin hid in a shadowy corner of the forecastle, carefully suppressing his aura.

Soon after, the meal delivery person nervously knocked on the cabin door.

Then, five or six people filed into the forecastle, bringing food for the big guy.

My word!

The trays they carried, held aloft with both hands, were nearly the size of a normal person's footbath, overflowing with magic dishes.

The wooden barrels held enough rice to feed fifty people. This was magic rice, emitting a faint bronze scent.

Large quantities of green and blue stir-fried vegetables filled the air with an enticing aroma. Fruits like pineapples as big as human heads displayed vivid colors.

The main course was a pig's hind leg nearly half a man's height, sparks flying from it. The sparks formed heart shapes in the air. Tripleblade immediately recognized it as the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' head chef's signature dish - Heartfire Ham.

The big guy grabbed the pig's hind leg and buried his face in it, devouring it.

In no time, the plump, glossy ham was reduced to a single large bone.

The big guy wolfed it down.

He lifted a foot-basin-sized soup bowl, swallowed several times, and drained it completely.

He hoisted the wooden rice barrel above his head and poured its contents directly into his gaping mouth.

After two pours and a few chews, he devoured the meals meant for fifty men.

Though the lunch portions were substantial, the giant finished everything in just two or three minutes. He then extended his massive tongue, licking every tray clean.

Finally, he glared at the five or six meal deliverers with wide eyes: “Hungry!”

“More. I want more...”

The giant's massive frame towered over the delivery crew even while seated.

The delivery mercenaries trembled in terror, their bodies quivering slightly. Their leader, drenched in cold sweat, frantically waved his hands, “There's none left! You ate all of today's lunch. We didn't prepare any more...”

The giant remained unsatisfied, yet he wasn't violent. He could only smack his lips, sucking the last bits of food from his teeth. Leaning his back against the wall behind him, he slowly closed his eyes.

Several delivery staff witnessed this and promptly began clearing the tableware with careful haste. They then scrambled to leave the forecastle, closing the door softly behind them.

Tripleblade overheard their voices outside: “I'm genuinely worried that one day while delivering food, I'll be devoured by a starving giant!”

“I truly never want to do this job again, every shift feels like a life-or-death ordeal!”

“I think we should report this situation and request higher-ups increase this giant's rations.”

“We've done this many times before, but getting approval from above keeps getting harder.”

"It's strange. I've cooked for giants before. I've never encountered anything like this. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps' giant has an extraordinary appetite; he devours all those magic dishes without any sign of indigestion! He's only bronze level..."

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is truly extraordinary. Even the giant they secretly feed is strangely powerful!”

The delivery men's voices faded with their footsteps.

Tripleblade fell silent.

The facts before him contradicted his earlier assumptions.

There was no embezzlement, and the giant’s portions hadn’t been skimped on. Quite the opposite, the big guy hadn’t eaten his fill!

Tripleblade watched the big guy drifting in and out of sleep, his hands resting on his slightly rounded belly as he mumbled discontentedly: “Food... meat...”

Soon, he fell into a deep sleep, snoring loudly.

He seemed to dream of the dragonman youngster, beginning to talk in his sleep: “Father, Xiao Guai isn't full... Where are you, father? Me... hungry...”

Hearing this sleep-talk, Tripleblade's eye twitched.

He fixed a skeptical gaze at the big guy, his mind racing with speculation: What bloodline could this giant possibly possess? The Gluttonous bloodline? The Big Eater bloodline? Or perhaps the Abyss bloodline?

All bearers of these bloodlines shared one defining trait: insatiable appetites!

The next moment, Tripleblade's eyes widened in disbelief.

Unbelievable!

"What am I seeing?!"

He saw the big guy cultivating, refining his battle qi.

Tripleblade knew the big guy practiced the Mindless Sleeping Posture Technique, a method the captain had painstakingly taught him, allowing the big guy to automatically cultivate passively while asleep.

Tripleblade also knew the big guy had already joined the ranks of the transcendents, advancing to bronze level.

But what was he seeing now?

He stared in shock as a faint iron hue began to shimmer within the battle qi radiating from the big guy's body.

"How long has it been? Has the big guy already reached peak bronze?"

“Iron hues in his battle qi? Does that mean he's about to ascend to iron level?”

“Did I see that correctly?”

Tripleblade began doubting his own eyesight.

But upon closer inspection, he confirmed he hadn't misread it!

This discovery delivered such a massive shock to his mind and body that he left the forecastle and closed the door in a daze.

“No wonder his appetite keeps growing, he's constantly absorbing external nutrients to propel his life level upward.”

“Bloodline!”

“What exactly is the big guy's bloodline?”

“This progress is absurd!”

Tripleblade racked his brain, he'd never seen another fighter advance this rapidly!

The big guy's performance shattered his understanding, truly broadening his horizons.

"The commander nurtured the big guy, and his cultivation has yielded the most unexpected results!"

“The big guy's bloodline is definitely no ordinary thing. He might just be the most gifted among us.”

“If I hadn't seen it with my own eyes, it would be hard to believe... that such a seemingly dim-witted fellow possesses such cultivation talent!”

“Fate... how unfair you are...”

Tripleblade's emotions were incredibly complex at that moment.

Envy, helplessness, regret—all mingled with bitter reflection on his own fate.

His bloodline was far from ideal. His ceiling was iron level; a plateau he'd stagnated at for years.

And now, before Tripleblade's very eyes, the big guy. that incredibly fortunate fool. had ascended to bronze level under his watch. Soon, he'd reach iron level, standing shoulder to shoulder with him!

Tripleblade didn't even know how he made it back to his cabin.

It took him a long time to steady his emotions.

Once calm, he immediately began writing a letter. he wanted to share this good news with Zong Ge and Zi Di.

But after finishing it, he promptly destroyed the letter.

“Can't be so careless.”

"Letters can be intercepted, especially with mysterious powerful enemies lurking outside."

"The big guy's cultivation speed like this proves his bloodline is at least gold level. No, perhaps even saint level..."

Any higher, and even Tripleblade wouldn't dare imagine it!

“He relies heavily on the leader. If such a giant grows stronger, his value to us will be immense...”

"Should we fail to atone to the Empire's higher-ups in the future, the big guy's importance to us will only grow!"

“We must protect him at all costs.”

“Do everything to conceal his cultivation speed. Hmm, we must also control those delivery personnel, bind them with contracts!”

"No, they don't know the secret yet. From now on, use alchemy puppets for deliveries. That's the safest approach."

"This information needs to be encrypted before sharing it with others. With my current privileges, I can't allocate more cultivation resources to the big guy."

Tripleblade began drafting the encrypted letter.

But after finishing it, he destroyed it once more.

“My encryption measures are only at the level of ordinary mercenary groups, easily cracked.”

"Perhaps I should deliver this message personally? That would undoubtedly be the safest approach!"

Chapter 644: The Mother Nest’s Ocean Beast’s Secret

“Yes, I have to do this.”

“Even at the bronze level, the big guy’s physical prowess rivaled the silver level. If he advances to iron level, I won’t be able to hold him back at all.”

“Once he reaches iron level, if he goes berserk again, even Chi Lai and Men Shi might not be able to restrain him.”

The mere thought of the big guy’s madness intensified Tripleblade’s sense of urgency.

He summoned Fei She once more, left a brief message for Chi Lai and Men Shi, then immediately set off. Using the teleportation formation, he headed toward Blood Kin City.

Leveraging his connection with the Snow Bird Harbor City Lord, Tripleblade cut through the queue and swiftly arrived at Blood Kin City.

Seeing Tripleblade arrive secretly, looking as though he had urgent news to report, Zi Di's heart skipped a beat. She feared some mishap had befallen the sub-corps: “Could someone be sabotaging our alchemy workshop, undermining my business plans?”

Instead, what she heard concerned the big guy's cultivation secret.

Zi Di expressed genuine astonishment.

This was no act.

Though she knew the big guy possessed a high bloodline grade, she hadn’t anticipated his cultivation progress would be this rapid!

Like Tripleblade, Zi Di’s eyes were opened.

Upon hearing this, Zong Ge remained unflappable: “The big guy's bloodline grade is exceptionally high, at least saint level, possibly even legendary.”

“If saint level, his bloodline concentration must be over 75%.”

Tripleblade gaped in disbelief.

Unbelievable!

That clumsy oaf was at least a potential saint cultivator?

"What kind of luck have we stumbled into? We just picked up a saint cultivator like this?" Tripleblade was stunned by sheer happiness.

Zi Di, meanwhile, observed Zong Ge discreetly.

His unflappable composure once again hinted at his extraordinary background.

Based on the big guy's cultivation speed, Zong Ge had swiftly assessed his bloodline grade, a level of insight beyond ordinary people.

Zong Ge mused, "This is a blessing, but it carries risks. The big guy has mental instability; his intelligence falls far below average. The stronger he grows, the greater the hidden danger."

“Exactly,” Tripleblade nodded in agreement.

Zi Di remarked, "I believe the big guy's intelligence issues will ease as his life level increases. I believe this issue will resolve itself without our intervention."

But Zong Ge looked at her and countered, "That is precisely the problem, President Zi Di. As the big guy's intelligence grows, his perception of things and the outside world will change. How will he view his own situation? How will he view us? Will he become dissatisfied with his treatment? Will he still rely on our leader? What if one day he becomes a saint, and our strength falls below his? What will he think then?"

These questions plunged both Zi Di and Tripleblade into silence.

At that moment, Zi Di realized with a jolt: “Yes! Why hadn't I considered this?”

She had tested the big guy's bloodline and deduced his intelligence issues would resolve themselves, but she'd never anticipated the subsequent complications.

“Not just me, even the commander failed to foresee this. This really shouldn't have happened...”

“No, it's because we know about the Bloodcore! That's why none of us saw this as a problem!”

The thought of the Bloodcore instantly calmed Zi Di.

Even if the big guy's bloodline reached legend, or even the god level, so what?

With the Bloodcore, why would they fail to achieve the god level?

The Bloodcore gave the dragonman youngster and Zi Di boundless confidence, fearing nothing in the big guy's growth.

“But we still need to consider the details.”

“Although the commander's Flaming Dragon King bloodline is legend level, its concentration isn't high. His cultivation speed pales in comparison to the big guy's.”

“The safest approach is to proactively suppress the big guy's growth rate somewhat.”

With this thought, Zi Di turned her gaze back to Zong Ge.

Zong Ge gave her a slight nod. "It seems you've already considered this. We're heading to the royal capital now, and the commander is still missing. We can't let the big guy grow like this unchecked. Don't forget he has that madness! Once he reaches iron level and his madness flares up again, the sub-corps won't be able to suppress him!"

Zi Di fell silent.

Tripleblade followed suit.

None of them wanted to suppress the big guy, but reality was forcing them to do so.

Zong Ge grew impatient, looking at Zi Di urgently, “What are you still hesitating about?”

A bitter expression crossed Zi Di's face, “I'm wondering... if the commander were here and learned of this situation, how would he handle it?”

Ocean Mother Nest.

The fishman youngster was unaware his son was about to be suppressed. His entire focus was now fixed on observing the magic projections.

The precision eel driller combined with the dual-eared detector had finally allowed him to glimpse beyond the Mother Nest.

To his utter astonishment, a coalition army of undersea races had gathered outside the Mother Nest, a development far exceeding his wildest expectations.

Now, this army had commenced another blood sacrifice.

Hordes of sea beasts were herded onto the altar, where they were obliterated by the altar and the vast magic formation.

A saint level octopusman priestess chanted prayers in a loud voice: “Gulrgurluglurglur...”

Two gold level octopusman priests stood beside her.

Beneath the altar knelt a vast congregation of believers, fishmen, octopusmen, nagas, mermaids, and even colossal sea giants.

In truth, since the moment the fishman youngster first glimpsed the outside world, six grand blood sacrifices had already been performed.

This was the seventh.

And this time, it was distinctly different.

Not only was the scale more grandiose, with a saint level priestess personally presiding, but the believers kneeling around the altar had been carefully selected and arranged beforehand, unlike the random seating of previous ceremonies.

This unusual detail sparked a suspicion in the fishman youngster's mind: “Perhaps... they're about to teleport in again!”

After two days of observation, the fishman youngster had gained deeper insight into this undersea army.

He learned the saint level octopusman priestess was named Zhou Zhang. The other two octopusmen were: a short one with a head as large as an umbrella, called Zhang Yu Wan; and a towering one with jaw tentacles as thick as arms, known as Jiu Hua Bi.

These three were undoubtedly the commanders of the great army.

Though other gold level members and numerous other races existed, all trusted these three octopusmen priests and followed their directives.

Thanks to the tower spirit's post-translation, the fishman youngster had learned more.

He now confirmed that this undersea army shared a common faith - Charming Blue Goddess.

Whether in the octopusman priestess's prayers or the divine script inscribed within the magic circles, there was only one object of worship: Charming Blue Goddess!

“Charming Blue Goddess's primary artifact is the Mermaid Fairy Tale. Its disturbance caused me and the Deep Sea Monster Fish to be teleported here.”

“As for those fishmen teleported in earlier, it was likely the combined operation of the saint level priestess, the altar, and the magic circles.”

The fishman youngster had already formed several conjectures in his mind. He couldn't help but ask the tower spirit, “Have all the Rubber Swallowers been deployed?”

The tower spirit responded immediately, “All six frog-type Rubber Swallowers have been deployed and confirmed to have reached their designated positions.”

These alchemy puppets had been urgently crafted by Zhong Yong specifically for this operation.

“They're here.” The fishman youngster's expression brightened.

In the magic projection, the saint level priestess raised both arms, chanting hymns at the top of her lungs. The surging aura of saint level divine magic, amplified by the ritual formation, ultimately converged upon the altar.

The altar erupted in blinding radiance, illuminating the kneeling faithful below.

The light vanished in an instant, and a large number of believers disappeared, along with numerous sea beasts.

The next moment, violent spatial fluctuations erupted within the Ocean Mother Nest. The believers and sea beasts that had vanished were teleported back inside.

“Just as I suspected!” the fishman youngster thought excitedly.

Such commotion immediately roused the slumbering saint level sea beast within the Mother Nest, the Snow Pear Octopus.

The octopus immediately launched its hunt upon the prey delivered to its mouth.

In an instant, the sea churned violently, its waters stained crimson.

The believers huddled in corners, desperately trying to avoid being swept into the carnage.

“Now!” The fishman youngster gave the command. Six frog-type Rubber Swallowers, already positioned throughout the area, sprang into action.

They selected their targets and swallowed them whole.

Empowered by the Deceptive Disguise spell and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, these Rubber Swallowers were disguised as seawater. Only the informed fishman youngster could discern their incomplete frog-like forms.

To the other believers, they witnessed only surging waves mysteriously vanishing nearby believers.

Not one of the six Swallowers missed; all successfully consumed their targets.

On one hand, this was because these Swallowers were all iron level, targeting bronze level prey, a clear level advantage.

On the other hand, Deceptive Disguise spell ensured their ambushes were utterly unforeseeable. The targets had fixated their attention entirely on the saint level sea beast.

Not even their disappearance was noticed by all of the believers.

After securing their prey, the Swallowers immediately turned back.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish was no longer a reef; it now bore the appearance of a cluster of pearls.

Even as it opened the landing pod and let the Swallowers enter one by one, neither the sea beasts nor the believers detected the slightest anomaly.

The fishman youngster had long been waiting inside the landing pod.

The Swallowers lined up, each approaching him in turn to regurgitate their unconscious targets.

These targets had been carefully selected, all fishmen.

The fishman youngster raced against time, using the Bloodcore on these fishman, swiftly absorbing multiple fishman bloodlines.

“Got it! Looks like my luck's holding!” A flash of delight crossed the fishman youngster's face.

From one fishman bloodline, he had gained knowledge, information about the fishman language!

Now, he could understand their conversations.

The information inherent in transcendent bloodlines wasn't limited to racial magic; it also encompassed racial knowledge, environmental data, and, of course, languages.

“Too bad there aren't any octopusman...”

After absorbing five fishmen captives, the youngster brought the sixth into the depths of the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

He swiftly reached a compartment and handed the captive over to the tower spirit.

After the tower spirit scanned him, the youngster once again activated his Bloodcore, reducing the final captive to ash.

Carrying the captive's data, the tower spirit manipulated the Deceptive Disguise Formation to empower a silver level alchemy puppet.

Soon, the puppet took on the appearance of the sixth captive.

Of course, to the knowledgeable fishman youngster, this puppet's appearance was a chaotic assemblage of puppet and fishman parts, riddled with flaws.

“Deploy immediately!” the fishman youngster ventured.

The disguised alchemy puppet was first swallowed by the Swallower, which then, like seawater, reentered the Mother Nest.

The saint level sea beast was at its hunting climax. The believers were tense, their attention severely diverted.

The Swallower opened its maw, and the disguised alchemy puppet swam out, joining the believers gathered in another corner. Those believers glanced at it hurriedly before fixing their gaze back on the saint level sea beast.

“The first step succeeded,” the fishman youngster's heart settled.

His attempt primarily aimed to verify whether the Deceptive Disguise spell could deceive the outer magic formation and the saint level priestess.

If it could deceive them, it meant he too could temporarily leave the Mother Nest using this method.

“Next, I just hope it gets teleported out,” the fishman youngster watched the magic projection intently, filled with anticipation.

The Snow Pear Octopus' hunt concluded. It greedily sucked all the blood into its belly before slowly approaching the believers.

The believers trembled in fear, their prayers rising in unison.

Massive, snow-white octopus tentacles drifted slowly around their bodies.

Suddenly!

A tentacle shot out, snatching one of the believers and retracting with lightning speed.

In the next instant, the believer was swallowed whole by the saint level octopus.

The fishman youngster's face turned ashen.

The swallowed believer had been his disguised puppet.

His attempt had failed completely midway.

“Wait, I think I get it!” A bolt of insight struck the fishman youngster's mind, “I've figured out the pattern this Snow Pear Octopus uses to choose its victims.”

Chapter 645: Salty Cotton Candy

“It's faith!” the fishman youngster realized the answer.

"Among those praying fishmen killed by the saint level sea beast, the unlucky ones were likely those with weaker faith."

"The alchemy puppet I deployed had no issues with its disguise. But even when it pretended to pray, it lacked true faith. The saint level sea beast instantly recognized it and killed it outright."

The gruesome death of the alchemy puppet sent shockwaves through the praying believers. Through the magic projection, the fishman youngster saw many bodies convulse violently in that instant, as if struck by lightning.

After that, the saint level sea beast, the Snow Pear Octopus, killed several more believers before reluctantly halting the slaughter.

In the end, the believers' life force surged significantly on the spot. Some even achieved breakthroughs beyond their current level. Yet the fishman youngster also noticed that a portion of the believers still showed little increase in their life aura.

Though he had witnessed this phenomenon before, the youngster still expressed a hint of astonishment.

Typically, cultivation was a process of accumulation—quantitative change leading to qualitative transformation or building up strength for a breakthrough. Yet this scene defied that rule.

In truth, however, this was merely superficial; the reality did not conflict with the laws of cultivation.

“It's divine power,” the fishman youngster murmured inwardly.

Deities stood at the pinnacle of all life in the world.

Their majestic power knew no bounds.

This was not mere rhetoric, nor exaggerated praise, it was fact.

Divine power represented the highest known form of energy, capable of perfectly replacing magic power, magic energy, elemental forces, electricity, and all other forms of energy. Divine power excelled in every domain—healing, damage, teleportation, creation, and more.

Divine spells were the methods to wield this power, amplifying its effects to their fullest potential.

“The believers performed a blood sacrifice, praying to the Charming Blue Goddess. They receive rewards in return, causing their strength to surge.”

This outcome was entirely expected.

A successful grand ritual often drew the attention of the gods. Divine blessings usually followed. These blessings manifested in various forms: elevating certain beings to transcendent level, bestowing items, the highest being divine artifacts, and even resurrecting the dead.

From what the fishman youngster knew, two classic examples stood out.

One involved an elf king without an heir. He held a grand ritual and conceived on the spot, eventually giving birth to an exceptional successor.

Another occurred when a dwarf tribe plunged into war. Their chieftain performed a ritual, beseeching the gods for peace. His sacrifices were truly immense. The gods intervened, altering the hearts and minds of both warring factions. The two tribes achieved genuine peace, never to fight again. To this day, these two tribes form the most steadfast alliance within the Dwarf Kingdom.

The gods were omnipotent, the difference lay in the cost.

Take creation, for instance: crafting something entirely from scratch versus modifying something existing demanded vastly different sacrifices from the gods.

“These fishmen, nagas, and other believers received divine feedback, achieving a leap in power.”

“But not all believers fared thus, those with wavering faith were slain outright by the saint level sea beast!”

"The saint level sea beast was likely deliberately placed here, primarily to sift the faithful. They also prevent leaks, hasn't the Charming Blue Goddess already fallen into slumber? Her situation is quite precarious."

Suddenly, flashes of insight erupted in the fishman youngster's mind as he connected the scattered clues.

While the fishman youngster pondered, the other Charming Blue believers were teleported away.

The fishman youngster continued his musings: “My teleportation circumstances differ from theirs.”

"The Mermaid Fairy Tale is at work here. Those believers relied on the power of the magic formation outside the Mother Nest."

"The Mermaid Fairy Tale is the main artifact of the Charming Blue Goddess. Undoubtedly, it far surpasses the external magic divine script formation and this saint level sea beast in scale. Therefore, it can also draw divine power here."

Since the Charming Blue believers could achieve rank leaps, it proved divine power was infusing their bodies.

With divine power descending, it was only natural for the Mermaid Fairy Tale to absorb it.

As for evidence, the fishman youngster had obtained it days ago - the Mermaid Fairy Tale had gained a vast amount of pearl bubbles!

"This artifact seems to possess an innate ability to actively absorb external Charming Blue divine power. This instinct intensifies especially when divine power is scarce. Much like a starving person seeking sustenance."

The most direct evidence supporting the fishman youngster's conjecture lay in the previous Two Eyes Island.

At that moment, the bishop of Charming Blue Goddess cast a divine spell to disguise the crucial fishman. However, when the fishman stealthily approached the Deep Sea Monster Fish, the spell was suddenly broken, revealing its true form. It then suffered a direct hit from the Golden Flash's light cannon. This series of unexpected events ultimately made the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps the biggest hidden winner of the battle on Two Eyes Island.

After the battle, survivors reviewing the events offered their own speculations.

“The divine spell was likely broken because the Mermaid Fairy Tale absorbed the divine power sustaining it.”

The cultivation methods of clergymen differed from those of fighters and magicians. They dedicated their faith, absorbing descending divine power through prayer to achieve advancement. As divine power infused and accumulated within them, both body and soul underwent transformation. Ultimately, they would take on the form of the deity they worship. This facilitated the infusion, storage, and utilization of divine power. Upon death, they were more readily transformed by the deity into holy spirits, recruited into the divine domain.

Thus, the believers' sudden advancement was, at its core, divine assistance.

"It's like converting battle qi. With a skilled practitioner, the conversion process can be completed swiftly."

“Attempting it alone, however, is an arduous journey.”

For clergymen, this conversion was far more straightforward than for fighters transforming their own battle qi.

Fighters who absorbed another's battle qi had to first fully digest and purge the foreign qi. But the divine energy within the clergyman, being a higher level energy source, could be perfectly converted into lower level energy. It directly transformed into the clergyman's battle qi or magic power, etc., without any confusion or rejection from foreign energy types.

Understanding this, the fishman youngster once again fell into deep worry.

He had originally used the alchemy puppet to test the effectiveness of his Deceptive Disguise spell. If it worked, he could disguise himself and infiltrate the fishmen army. This would be nothing short of an escape route.

“The crucial question now is: can the Deceptive Disguise spell mimic faith?”

The fishman youngster harbored deep doubts.

The recent failure of the alchemy puppet wasn't entirely definitive. When designing the disguise, the fishman youngster hadn't incorporated the aspect of faith.

"Theoretically, faith can be faked too. But the problem is, how do you design that?"

The fishman youngster was deeply troubled.

He could use the Deceptive Disguise Formation to alter the perceived appearance of target objects, but faith involved the intangible, ethereal realm of the mind.

He had no idea how to proceed!

Soon, the fishman youngster realized: “The Deceptive Disguise Formation we use comes from the War Merchant. It simply doesn't possess the capability to disguise faith.”

The core material for the Deceptive Disguise spell and Deceptive Disguise Formation was the Pearl Bubble.

This god level material originated from the divine artifact, Mermaid Fairy Tale.

Divine artifacts were meant for deities; other transcendent beings could scarcely unleash their full power.

War Merchant's ability to wield pearl bubbles already demonstrated his legend level magician prowess.

“Truthfully, even if I could mimic his divine faith, it would be far too risky.”

Using the Deceptive Disguise spell to trick the Charming Blue Goddess?

That would be dumber than showing off in front of a master.

Legend had it that the Charming Blue Goddess was in deep slumber, but what if that was not true? What if she had already awakened? What if she was drawn by the blood sacrifice and turned her attention here? What if she was roused by the aura of a divine artifact?

When dealing with a deity's presence, the fishman youngster had to be cautious.

“Looks like I need to find a more reliable way out of this mess,” the fishman youngster sighed, utterly disheartened.

Lady Sea Snake, however, was freed from her long-standing gloom.

“Finally, they're here.” She cast a spell to observe the sea surface from afar.

The Heroism Pirates was sailing toward her.

The death of her gold level sea serpent familiar had caused her immense trouble.

This was a world of the strong, where status depended on power.

While Wei Ying had sacrificed the sea serpent and walked away, Lady Sea Snake had to personally deal with her predicament.

She seethed with hatred toward Wei Ying but ultimately had no recourse.

Thus, Lady Sea Snake could only maintain a façade, pretending her sea serpent pet still existed, while secretly detaching from her pirate crew. She discreetly contacted reliable contacts, aiming to capture another gold level sea serpent to actively fill this gap in combat strength.

As for why she sought only one gold level pet, and specifically a sea serpent, that was determined by her unique method of taming and the characteristics of her bloodline.

The silver level was her absolute limit.

Her spirit could only sustain one gold level pet, or multiple silver level ones.

Clearly, however, a single gold level creature was a far superior choice to multiple silver level ones.

On the one hand, this represented a qualitative leap in combat power!

On the other hand, Lady Sea Snake herself is a pirate leader. The ocean battlefield was vast and boundless, offering unobstructed visibility. Escape was easy, and small-scale combat scenarios were the most common. Possessing one gold level pet was far more suitable for this kind of battlefield than several silver level snakes.

“Lord Ba Qi has finally arrived,” Ruan Tang Ren sighed inwardly.

He certainly didn't want the Heroism Pirates to show up so soon.

“Have some,” Ruan Tang Ren nudged closer, offering her a piece of cotton candy, “I made this especially for you.”

His eyes held undisguised admiration as he looked at her.

Lady Sea Snake glanced at the white fluff and shook her head, “I don't like sweets.”

“I know, I know,” Ruan Tang Ren replied, “This is specially made for you, it's a salty cotton candy.”

The Sea Serpent Girl sighed inwardly, reluctantly taking it. She tasted it, it was indeed salty, and surprisingly good.

But her focus was elsewhere entirely: “Lord Ba Qi has arrived. Let's disembark and greet him.”

Chapter 646: Weakening Fishing Line

The Heroism Pirates officially joined forces with the Powerless Pirates and Lady Sea Snake.

Beneath the mainmast of the Heroism Pirates' flagship, Ba Qi still sat sprawled casually, leaning against the mast.

As the leader of the Heroism Pirates, the gold level fighter Ba Qi disliked confining himself to the captain's quarters.

His bloodline stemmed from the air element, making him naturally drawn to open environments. Constantly interacting with the various energies of the outside world brought him comfort and contentment.

Bloodlines were the essence of life, determining distinct life forms. Different life forms often exhibited different biological habits.

Generally speaking, a transcendent’s bloodline profoundly influenced their personality and behavioral patterns.

For instance, those with vampire bloodlines typically shun daylight, preferring nocturnal activity.

Ba Qi leaned against the sturdy mast behind him, tilting his head slightly to gaze at the sky.

The sea breeze swept across his face. The sky was as clear as washed blue, save for a single white cloud suspended high above.

Ba Qi knew well that this was the Fluffy Cloud's disguise.

When the sky was filled with clouds, the vessel's camouflage was nearly flawless. But under such clear skies, a solitary drifting cloud stood out conspicuously, easily arousing suspicion.

Under Ba Qi's watchful gaze, two figures descended from the cloud.

One was male, the other female.

The man was none other than the captain of the Fluffy Cloud, a silver level fighter - Ruan Tang Ren. He possessed a long, flat face like a pancake, an ordinary appearance, and sparse yet large black freckles that resembled chocolate chips.

The woman wore a form-fitting leather outfit, her curvaceous figure drawing male attention effortlessly. She was none other than the leader of the Petal Snake Pirates, a silver level water magician - Lady Sea Snake.

The gold level Ba Qi was the most crucial figure in their current operation. Facing such a gold level expert, the two silver level individuals dared not be overconfident and immediately flew down to greet him.

There were significant differences even among gold level fighters.

In fact, the higher the level, the greater the disparity in combat strength between peers.

Ba Qi was no ordinary gold level.

His legendary background was unparalleled. Rumors suggested Ba Qi's bloodline potential transcended mere gold level. Regrettably, his prolonged confinement within the Abyssal Hanging Tower had exposed him to the abyss's corrupting influence, leaving his injuries unhealed. Were it not for these debilitating wounds, he might well have ascended to the saint level!

“Lord Ba Qi, it is my profound honor to receive your support this time!” declared Lady Sea Snake as she landed on the deck.

Though not a gold level, her ability to fly was natural for a magician.

What was unusual was Ruan Tang Ren.

Merely a silver level fighter, he had descended without relying on Lady Sea Snake—he had actually “flown” down.

"Lord Ba Qi, we welcome you. I have prepared a welcoming banquet for you." Ruan Tang Ren bowed in greeting.

As a magic chef, he had indeed meticulously prepared a feast to honor Ba Qi.

Ba Qi glanced at the two, then fixed his gaze above Ruan Tang Ren's head.

There, he detected a thread.

The thread was so fine it was nearly invisible. It was this thread that had suspended Ruan Tang Ren as he descended before Ba Qi, creating the illusion of “flight.”

Ba Qi saw through Ruan Tang Ren's trickery at a glance, causing the latter's heart to tighten slightly.

Ba Qi waved his hand dismissively at the two. "A welcome banquet? Not necessary! What is your plan? When can we begin?"

On Flower Skirt Island, Ba Qi received treatment from Physician Shuang Tu and now felt much better.

Yet, burdened by chronic injuries, he understood his days were numbered, his condition incurable. Thus, he always treasured time, acting with decisive urgency.

Lady Sea Snake and Ruan Tang Ren exchanged a swift glance. They had long heard of Ba Qi's style and prepared accordingly.

The former was naturally delighted by Ba Qi's decisive nature.

She had already chosen her target and was inclined to act sooner rather than later.

In truth, she had grown impatient waiting for Ba Qi's arrival, spending entire days cooped up aboard the Fluffy Cloud.

On this point, however, Ruan Tang Ren felt quite the opposite.

What Ruan Tang Ren cherished most was crafting dishes.

Originally a restaurant chef, he'd been thrust into leadership by a series of misfortunes, unexpectedly becoming captain of the Powerless Pirates.

Ruan Tang Ren wasn't an ambitious pirate. Though he'd led the Powerless Pirates for years, his fleet had always consisted solely of the Fluffy Cloud.

He rarely bothered with raiding in everyday life, even now, amidst a golden age where piracy ran rampant and plundering carried minimal risk.

Often, he disguised the Fluffy Cloud as a white cloud drifting in the sky, hovering over a stretch of ocean, biding his time.

Due to the steady currents, vast shipping routes crisscrossed the open seas.

Thus, even though the Fluffy Cloud employed a wait-and-see approach, it still managed to regularly raid merchant ships, sustaining itself through plunder.

Ruan Tang Ren didn't fall for Lady Sea Snake at first sight either. Of course, any man would likely develop feelings for a beauty like her.

Lady Sea Snake wasn't just a simple water magician. Beyond her gold level magic pet, the sea serpent, she excelled at socializing. In these interactions, she fully leveraged her striking beauty.

Through a series of subtle maneuvers and suggestive hints from Lady Sea Snake, Ruan Tang Ren gradually became one of her admirers.

The days spent awaiting Ba Qi's arrival were the happiest for Ruan Tang Ren.

Each day, he could exchange good mornings and good nights with Lady Sea Snake, share the same vessel with the goddess he admired, and meticulously prepare delicacies for her. He even developed exquisite dishes tailored specifically to her palate.

Ba Qi's decisive and swift approach left Ruan Tang Ren deeply regretful. For the sooner this matter was resolved, the less time he would have with Lady Sea Snake.

Lady Sea Snake, reading his expressions, quickly confirmed that Ba Qi spoke with genuine sincerity.

She immediately adopted a serious expression and declared, “Lord Ba Qi, our target has already been selected.”

“My plan is as follows.”

“Step 1: I will command a fishman clan, secretly under my control, to attack and drive away our target.”

“Step 2: Once the sea serpent enters our ambush zone, Captain Ruan Tang Ren will personally intervene, using his fishing line to entangle it.”

“Step 3: Captain Ba Qi, you will then engage the sea serpent, aiming to weaken its combat strength and subdue it.”

“Step 4: I will then use the contract to forcibly bind it as my magic pet.”

The plan was straightforward and clear.

Ba Qi also knew about Lady Sea Snake and Ruan Tang Ren.

He understood that Lady Sea Snake possessed unique methods to forcibly contract a gold level serpentine magic beast.

He also knew that Ruan Tang Ren possessed a Weakening Fishing Line. This fishing line was a gold level artifact, an equipment created by the legendary magic chef Bu Kong Jun. Paired with his specially made dried earthworm spicy strips, it had once reeled in many powerful sea beasts.

Ba Qi nodded in approval of the plan: “Excellent. We can begin immediately!”

Chapter 647: The Necromancers are Ready

Having secured Ba Qi's approval, Lady Sea Snake immediately sent her message.

Soon, aboard the Fluffy Cloud, Qi Ling received her orders.

She had remained on the flagship of the Powerless Pirates, under Lady Sea Snake's personal supervision. Though Qi Ling had been coerced into signing a magic contract, Lady Sea Snake remained uneasy about its validity. She feared the undead faction's nascent advantage might secretly undermine the pact, prompting her to adopt a cautious approach, keeping Qi Ling under her direct supervision whenever possible.

Upon receiving the message, Qi Ling's expression shifted slightly. She sighed inwardly: “What was bound to happen has finally come.”

She had long dreaded this moment, yet now that her fears had materialized, a strange calm settled over her, easing the constant anxiety that had gripped her before.

"If I were alone, I'd be at Lady Sea Snake's mercy now. But thankfully, Lord Jian Gui has arrived. I entrusted myself to the right person."

Though merely a silver level, Qi Ling possessed extraordinary intelligence.

After being taken captive, she had been scheming to escape.

During the Weeping Pine Naval Battle, she deliberately revealed her traitor identity and intentionally let Jian Gui and his group slip away.

On one hand, she genuinely couldn't bring herself to harm them. She had been summoned by Jian Gui and served as his alchemy assistant for a long time. During that period, she received Jian Gui's cultivation. He had even secretly broken certain orthodox principles of necromancy, imparting advanced knowledge of the soul aspect to her prematurely. Jian Gui was Qi Ling's benefactor. Her innate goodness remained intact. By consistently using soul crystals, she had effectively resisted the erosion of negative energy, preventing her from betraying the kindness shown to her.

On the other hand, Qi Ling also knew she couldn't rely solely on herself. She needed external aid to break through her current predicament.

Days prior, through a method she had secretly embedded within the water ghost's body, she had discreetly received contact from Jian Gui. The moment she received Jian Gui's message, Qi Ling was nearly overcome with excitement. The sliver of hope she had carefully preserved amidst despair suddenly burst into brilliant light.

“I understand,” Qi Ling transmitted back, acknowledging the message.

She immediately began making connections.

First, she contacted Jian Gui and others, then reached out to her former clan, the Da Ji Clan.

It should be noted that although Qi Ling was a necromancer, she had never revealed her true nature. Lady Sea Snake's disguise magic was exceptionally potent. Qi Ling had remained aboard the Fluffy Cloud for many days without anyone discovering the truth.

The ghost ship lay concealed somewhere within the White Hair Trench.

Ever since Jian Gui and Qi Ling established their secret communication, they had not launched any further attacks. Instead, they remained hidden here, patiently awaiting the right moment.

On one hand, this was because their combat strength had nearly reached its peak. The room for improvement was minimal, and they could no longer progress as rapidly as before. On the other hand, the primary purpose of Jian Gui and his companions coming here was to rescue Qi Ling. They did not wish to expose themselves and lose more than they gained.

Jian Gui had long been awaiting news of Qi Ling. Now, upon receiving it, he immediately rallied the others and began the operation. Xiao Wang Zi was the first to emerge from his cabin onto the deck. He raised his arms and shouted, “This day has finally come!”

He was the least patient among them, nearly bursting with anticipation.

Ku Feng was the second to appear on deck. As a zombie fighter and undead ronin, his sword training had been suspended, yet he maintained remarkable composure.

These past days, he had paused his active cultivation, instead honing his stillness practice in his cabin - meditating while embracing his long sword.

This training method truly opened Cang Xu's eyes.

In Cang Xu's view, meditation had always been a practice reserved for magicians.

Driven by the curiosity of an accomplished magician, Cang Xu couldn't resist asking Ku Feng about it when the opportunity arose.

Ku Feng replied: Though he wasn't a magician and couldn't absorb external energy through meditation, pursuing the blade path required integrating both movement and stillness. Dynamic practice meant wielding blades, refining chopping techniques, honing movement angles, and testing them in combat. Static practice involved constant meditation, forging an unseen bond with one's weapon, and tapping into the user's inner spiritual power.

He further explained to Cang Xu that this philosophy of the fighter originated from the Eastern Sheng Ming Empire.

Ku Feng himself was rather taciturn, but over these days, Cang Xu had earned their recognition through his interactions with them. That recognition deepened steadily with time.

Thus, Ku Feng explained it to Cang Xu in detail.

“The Eastern Empire...” Cang Xu murmured in admiration. He knew that the martial arts cultivation in the eastern regions of the Empire had always been underpinned by meticulous theoretical frameworks. Compared to other parts of the Empire, these theories possessed distinct characteristics—they were richer and more refined.

Cang Xu couldn't help but think of Rou Zang.

This formidable gold level fighter was the first powerful being the survivors encountered after leaving the Mystifying Monster Island. To this day, Cang Xu remembered him vividly.

Rou Zang hailed from the Great Pain Temple, a powerful religious organization situated in the Eastern Empire.

Jian Gui joined the discussion, stating, "It is true that some martial warriors practice stillness cultivation. Though lacking a magic pool, they can indeed tap into their innate spiritual power through meditation."

"More broadly, this spiritual power is termed spiritual energy. Spiritual energy also forms the foundation of a clergyman's strength. Only those with exceptional spiritual energy talent can advance further in the clerical path."

Cang Xu heard this explanation for the first time and pondered, “Battle qi, magic power, spiritual energy, do these correspond to fighters, magicians, and clergymen?”

He couldn't help but press Jian Gui for more.

Jian Gui shook his head, refusing to elaborate further. He flatly declined Cang Xu, stating: This knowledge belongs to the advanced soul aspect inheritance. A brief mention is acceptable, but detailing it would violate orthodox principles.

Cang Xu, however, surmised more in secret: "The core spells of the elementary soul aspect inheritance are the Burden Alleviation Spell and Karma Removal Spell. The spell for detecting undead aptitude should be included in the intermediate undead inheritance. And in the advanced soul aspect inheritance, since it relates to the clergy, it must be about becoming a blasphemous priest, right?"

He firmly committed this conjecture to memory.

This was also one of his reasons for joining Jian Gui's faction.

Becoming a blasphemous priest would naturally require the use of memory crystals. The survivors possessed one alchemy device capable of creating memory crystals.

"If we could obtain the advanced inheritance and master all memory crystal techniques directly, it would be immensely beneficial for everyone. Alas, I can't even secure the intermediate inheritance now, let alone the advanced one."

Cang Xu felt utterly helpless about this.

He had obtained an elementary inheritance solely because the undead orthodoxy permitted trading such items.

But what followed demanded both innate talent and unwavering loyalty.

Cang Xu had been ruthlessly rejected on the very first criterion alone.

Despite his current bloodline mutation, he knew the trajectory of his transformation meant he still wouldn't meet that initial requirement.

“This is going to be tough,” Cang Xu sighed inwardly.

He wasn't particularly invested in saving Qi Ling; he merely wanted to participate in the mission to stay close to Jian Gui and the others.

Cang Xu was the third to appear on deck, and soon after, Qing Xin followed.

The wind magician Qing Xin had endured days of utter darkness.

Deprived of sunlight, her complexion was pale.

As a normal human, she had to constantly breathe to survive underwater. Even the simple act of eating became exponentially more difficult. More troublesome still, as the necromancers' power grew, Qing Xin faced increasingly dense negative energy.

Thus, life aboard the ghost ship became an ever-greater ordeal for her.

Despite her earnest efforts to prove herself, she had inevitably been marginalized.

As the necromancers grew stronger, Qing Xin had become virtually invisible.

Yet she demonstrated resilience and endurance, enduring this harsh existence without complaint.

Cang Xu gave Qing Xin a slight nod. "For now, stay safely aboard the ship. Unless absolutely necessary, do not reveal yourself. Otherwise, it will greatly interfere with your future public activities."

Qing Xin forced a faint smile at Cang Xu, assuring him she would follow his instructions and not cause trouble. But when help was needed, she would give her all at a moment's notice.

In short, Qing Xin positioned herself firmly within her role.

Clatter, clatter, clatter.

The sound of bones colliding filled the air.

Xiao Wang Zi was mobilizing his skeletal subordinates, concentrating large numbers of elite skeleton archers along the gunwales, bow, and stern to serve as long-range firepower.

Numerous ghosts emerged from their bodies only to swiftly merge back into the ghost ship - Jian Gui making temporary, subtle adjustments.

Soon, all these ghosts returned to the ghost ship, fully retracted.

Jian Gui finally appeared before everyone. His pre-battle address was brief: "We've prepared long for this battle. Now, follow my commands, and victory is assured!"

Clearly, he was neither Zong Ge nor the dragonman youngster, and he wasn't skilled at pre-battle speeches.

Yet among this group, his authority was undeniably paramount. Thanks to his daily leadership, everyone looked to him for guidance.

Cang Xu and the others nodded solemnly, vowing to fight fiercely.

Even Xiao Wang Zi, who harbored strong reservations about Qi Ling, was willing to cooperate fully with Jian Gui.

Seeing this, Cang Xu couldn't help but reflect: "Though necromancers endure an utterly harsh environment, it has forged their unity. Of course, that assumes the problem of negative energy corruption has been resolved. Those gradually corrupted necromancers have their wills twisted, becoming mad, hating all living things, their sanity steadily declining until they can no longer communicate or cooperate normally."

Jian Gui immediately sank into the deck, vanishing in an instant. Cang Xu understood: he hadn't descended to the next deck below, but had merged directly with the ghost ship itself.

The ghost ship's aura faintly expanded before contracting again. Its aura level steadily declined, dropping rapidly from gold to silver. Within seconds, it plummeted to iron, continuing its fall until it reached its limit at bronze.

Chapter 648: Da Ji Clan

Most crucially, the aura unique to undead had been completely purged.

Unless they detected the ghost ship itself, most would assume it was merely a colossal bronze level sea beast.

After completing this step, the ghost ship began silently ascending, gradually approaching the position of the Fluffy Cloud.

Cang Xu and the others also returned to the ship's cabin one after another.

As magicians, their primary task was to conceal themselves.

Cang Xu's thoughts stirred: “Is there no way to apply further camouflage?”

“In this state, if the ghost ship were detected, our identity would still be immediately recognized.”

There was no choice. Jian Gui possessed neither the Mermaid Fairy Tale nor any Deceptive Disguise Formation to employ. This level of concealment was already the limit of his abilities.

That was precisely why the ghost ship had deliberately kept its distance from the Fluffy Cloud over the past few days, showing no inclination to draw near.

In terms of disguise capabilities, even the gold level Jian Gui couldn't match the silver level Lady Sea Snake.

Approaching in this state still carried risks. The closer they got, the greater the chance of discovery. But now that they'd received Qi Ling's message, battle was imminent. Such risks no longer mattered.

Only after the ghost ship began its advance did the Da Ji Clan, to which Qi Ling had belonged in life, finally receive her message.

“Lady Qi Ling has sent word!” announced Qi Miao, the iron level fishman priest.

"I've said it many times, Qi Ling has fallen and become a necromancer. Stop addressing her as ‘Lady’!" Qi Shu snorted coldly, his expression displeased.

Tall and imposing, he was the Da Ji Clan's foremost fighter, his level reaching silver.

The clan leader Qi Ren shook his head. "Though Qi Ling became a necromancer, she's secretly protected us all along. She's secretly eliminated many dangerous sea beasts for the Da Ji Clan. She has contributed to the clan."

"Clan Leader! If not for Qi Ling this time, would we have been poisoned and controlled by that despicable, treacherous human pirate woman? She did this solely to capture Qi Ling. So we've all been dragged down by this damned necromancer!" Qi Shu shouted in a low voice.

Qi Ren shook his head again. "This is not what Qi Ling would have wanted. The true culprit is Lady Sea Snake, not her."

Qi Miao quickly lightened the mood: "No need for excessive worry, both of you. While we appear to be held hostage, Lord Zhou Zhang stands firmly behind us. This crisis will surely be overcome!"

Qi Miao held unwavering confidence in the tribe's future.

Her assurance stemmed from the octopusman, Zhou Zhang.

Lady Sea Snake had invited Ba Qi as a backup plan. Qi Ling and Jian Gui had secretly made contact, also holding a reserve strategy. As one faction within the tribe, they had secretly secured a formidable ally.

On the surface, the Da Ji Clan hunted alone in the White Hair Trench. In reality, they had covertly reached out to the octopusman priestess Zhou Zhang through other tribes.

At the deepest reaches of the White Hair Trench, Zhou Zhang had erected an altar. Surrounding the Ocean Mother Nest, she continuously performed blood sacrifices.

She commanded numerous intelligent marine species, with fishman constituting the vast majority. Octopusman numbers, conversely, were quite sparse.

Zhou Zhang naturally had no objection to assisting the Da Ji Clan within her capabilities.

As a saint level Charming Blue Archbishop, she possessed a powerful drive for proselytizing. This was especially true upon discovering that over 50% of the Da Ji Clan's fishman already worshipped the Charming Blue Goddess.

To Zhou Zhang, such a fishman tribe was essentially her own kind.

But after learning the details, Zhou Zhang did not immediately move against those aboard the Fluffy Cloud. Instead, she secretly ordered the Da Ji Clan to play along with Lady Sea Snake, turning the tables on her.

Thus, the Da Ji Clan had no choice but to keep up the charade until now.

Frankly, Qi Shu was dissatisfied with this arrangement. He stated bluntly, “Though we have Lord Zhou Zhang's backing, she intends to use us as bait to lure more prey for her blood sacrifice.”

Not a Charming Blue believer, Qi Shu harbored wariness toward Zhou Zhang.

Devoted to his tribe, he voiced his concern: “We're about to face a gold level sea serpent. We'll suffer heavy casualties.”

Qi Ren sighed deeply. "Simply preserving our tribe is already a great achievement. Relying solely on our own strength, we have no chance of turning the tide. Bear it. Survive this ordeal, and the Da Ji Clan will have boundless horizons."

With limited strength, this was the only option.

Qi Miao and Qi Shu exchanged glances, each letting out a sigh.

The fishmen immediately used conch shells to send a message to Zhou Zhang.

This was the contact method Zhou Zhang had specifically reserved for them.

Minutes later, the conch shell sounded, conveying Zhou Zhang's orders.

As Qi Ren, Qi Shu, and Qi Miao had anticipated, Zhou Zhang commanded the Da Ji Clan to mobilize and temporarily cooperate with Lady Sea Snake's plan.

Qi Ren sighed bitterly and issued the order: “Assemble the entire tribe. We depart immediately.”

Qi Miao couldn't help but plead, “Clan Leader, let the elders, women, and children remain behind.”

Qi Shu responded with a hint of coldness: "We march together. If our main force perishes, those left behind, the old, weak, and sick, will have no chance of survival either. Even if they're absorbed by other fishman tribes, their lives will be unbearably miserable. They'll become cannon fodder sooner or later. Better to face it now, at least this time it's for our own kin."

Qi Ren shook his head, ultimately revising the order to allow elderly fishman to join the battle while keeping women and children behind.

The Da Ji Clan mobilized.

Following coordinates provided by Qi Ling, they swiftly reached the coral reef where the sea serpent lurked.

Several hunter fishman ventured cautiously into the reef and successfully located the gold level sea serpent.

It was a silver-ringed, emerald-scaled sea serpent, measuring 13 meters in length. Coiled in the shadows of the coral, its belly slightly swollen, it had clearly fed recently and was now resting to digest its meal.

Qi Ren and Qi Shu, both silver levels, personally took action, hurling a fishing net at the sea serpent.

It was a gold level net.

Though the Da Ji Clan lacked gold level members, generations of accumulation had granted them access to gold level equipment.

This net represented the clan's greatest asset.

Caught off guard, the sea serpent was swiftly ensnared, along with the surrounding coral.

The net contracted rapidly as the remaining fishmen closed in, hurling their first volley of harpoons.

The sharp spearheads cleaved through the water, nearly all striking the serpent's body.

Yet the effect was poor. The spears barely pierced the serpent's scales. Only a few thrown by silver level fishman managed to draw a trickle of blood.

Caught off guard, the sea serpent began thrashing violently within the net.
