Hundreds of fishmen strained with all their might, pulling on the ropes attached to the net's edges.

The four main ropes at the corners were firmly secured to giant sea snails anchored to the ocean floor.

These sea snails were enormous, resembling small hills.

Strictly speaking, the Da Ji Clan members were nomads of the deep sea. The giant sea snails served as the main structures for their shifting pastures and temporary homes.

The snail shells were enormous, hollowed out to create numerous rooms and windows.

The Da Ji Clan carefully selected specific minerals to feed them daily.

After consuming these minerals, the snail shells hardened to iron-like rigidity and became incredibly heavy.

To confront the gold level sea serpent, the Da Ji Clan deployed their full arsenal—not only utilizing the gold level fishing net but also mobilizing every mountain snail at their disposal.

The snails moved slowly in four directions, like four massive pillars, firmly anchoring the nets to prevent the sea serpent from escaping.

Wave after wave of harpoon attacks followed, with Qi Ren, Qi Shu, and Qi Miao joining the assault.

The cumulative damage from the silver level attacks steadily increased, leaving visible wounds across the sea serpent's body.

The sea serpent lashed out in fury, spewing streams of sickening green venomous water.

The venom contaminated the surrounding seawater, spreading rapidly like a thick fog in the ocean.

Fishmen who touched the water were instantly poisoned.

It was extremely potent!

The Da Ji Clan reacted swiftly, consuming seaweed antidotes, to little effect.

After all, this was a gold level racial magic, the sea serpent's ultimate survival tactic.

At the critical moment, Qi Ren gave the order. Veteran fishmen from the tribe charged into the toxic fog, one after another. They carried water bladders, using them to absorb the surrounding seawater. Many even opened their mouths wide, using themselves as water bags to absorb the surrounding poisonous seawater with all their might.

Soon, every one of these veteran fishmen lay motionless, their bellies turned skyward, eyes wide and unblinking.

Qi Ren and Qi Shu wore grim expressions.

Qi Miao looked utterly heartbroken.

A tragic atmosphere swiftly spread. Soon, the second wave of elderly fishmen charged toward the sea serpent.

Wave after wave of these suicidal attacks successfully mitigated the surrounding toxic waters.

Most embraced death with a hearty laugh. Yet some, facing imminent death, fled the battlefield.

These deserters were swiftly beheaded by Qi Shu and his comrades.

Yet even towards these fleeing elders, the fishmen of the Da Ji Clan showed no scorn or ridicule. The fear of death was inherent to sentient beings. When they themselves grew old, no one could guarantee they wouldn't act similarly.

Wave after wave of elderly fishmen traded their lives for a turning point on the battlefield.

Their voluntary sacrifice was also for the Da Ji Clan, to secure sufficient living space for their kin and alleviate resource pressures.

Even in environments like the White Hair Trench, the fishman tribes still faced immense survival pressures.

Under such pressures, the oceanic intelligent species continually adapted, evolving optimal survival strategies.

The sea serpent relentlessly spewed venom, yet the Da Ji Clan's precise countermeasures neutralized most attacks without altering the serpent's fate, it remained bound by the fishing net.

Four sea snails flew in simultaneously, dragging the net and pulling the sea snake forcefully toward the ambush site.

By the halfway point, the Da Ji Clan had already sacrificed over three hundred fishmen, and even one of the sea snails had been poisoned.

With nearly all the elder fishmen dead, Qi Shu and the others could only lead the tribe's elite warriors into the fray.

After over three agonizing minutes, another sea snail collapsed.

The transcendent fishmen had also suffered over fifty percent casualties, causing Qi Ren immense heartache.

Chapter 649: Ruan Tang Ren Catches a Snake

With only two mountain snails left, they could no longer restrain the gold level sea serpent.

The sea serpent whipped its body, quickly shaking off the fishing net wrapped around it, and charged furiously toward the fishmen.

The transcendent fishmen scattered in panic, but the sea serpent opened its maw and sucked in the three nearest fishmen, swallowing them whole on the spot.

Qi Ren rushed into battle personally, while Qi Miao, having exhausted all magic and divine power, could only temporarily withdraw.

The gold level sea serpent lashed left and right, killing fishmen one by one, either by poison or swallowing them whole.

Its belly soon bulged, revealing the uneven shapes of fishmen and weapons beneath. Yet these were swiftly dissolved by the serpent's formidable digestive power.

For the Da Ji Clan, the losses were devastating.

Qi Ren's eyes reddened with anguish, but he could do nothing. Neither Lady Sea Snake nor Zhou Zhang had issued a retreat order.

This was the world of the strong.

No matter how numerous the weak were, they were merely seen as more cannon fodder.

Silver level fishmen like Qi Ren and Qi Shu were, in the eyes of Lady Sea Snake and Zhou Zhang, nothing more than high level cannon fodder.

Fortunately, the sea serpent's intelligence was limited. After breaking free from the fishing net, it locked onto these fishmen, driven by vengeful fury, determined to fight to the death.

Qi Ren and the others fought a retreating battle, luring the sea serpent toward the ambush site.

“The casualties are too heavy!” Qi Ren nearly shed tears of blood.

Though the ambush site was still a short distance away, most of the Da Ji Clan's extraordinary fishmen had already perished. To preserve the remaining extraordinary combat strength, he had no choice but to sacrifice many of the weaker members, women and children included.

“Am I to die here in battle?”

“I cannot die. No more transcendents must be sacrificed!”

Qi Ren was gravely wounded, not out of fear of death, but because the current casualties had pushed the Da Ji Clan to the brink of collapse.

Fortunately, at that moment, Qi Ling relayed Lady Sea Snake's new order: “The Da Ji Clan may retreat.”

Ironically, it was not Zhou Zhang, on whom Qi Ren and others had pinned their hopes, who ultimately spared the Da Ji Clan, but Lady Sea Snake.

However, her mercy stemmed not from compassion.

On the one hand, the Da Ji Clan had lured the sea serpent close enough for them to strike. On the other hand, Lady Sea Snake needed the Da Ji Clan to survive, it was her leverage against Qi Ling.

“We retreat!” Qi Ren bellowed. The fishmen cheered as if granted a pardon, while he volunteered to hold the rear.

"Damn it, damn it!" On the Fluffy Cloud, Qi Ling manipulated numerous water spirits for reconnaissance and knew full well the Da Ji Clan had suffered heavy casualties.

Her heart burned with hatred and fury, yet bound by the magic contract, she couldn't lash out at Lady Sea Snake.

Jian Gui whom she'd pinned her hopes on had quietly approached but hadn't intervened.

Like the fishmen tribe, the backup Qi Ling had counted on failed to materialize at the critical moment.

The ships of the Heroism Pirates had already retreated far away. Now, only the Fluffy Cloud, disguised as a white cloud, hovered above the battlefield.

Ba Qi, Lady Sea Snake, Ruan Tang Ren, Qi Ling, and others gathered on the deck.

“Captain Ruan Tang Ren, it's up to you now,” Lady Sea Snake said, her large eyes glistening with plea, revealing both expectation and entreaty.

Ruan Tang Ren instantly perked up, his spirits soaring. He pounded his chest and vowed, "With me giving it my all, I won't let you down! Even if it means dying, I'll make this plan succeed."

Lady Sea Snake suddenly pressed her index finger gently against Ruan Tang Ren's lips, “No, Ruan Tang Ren. I won't let you die.”

In that instant, Ruan Tang Ren's body trembled violently, his eyes losing focus.

When he snapped back to himself, his face flushed crimson. Two puffs of air burst from his nostrils as he declared loudly, "Lady Sea Snake, you watch this! Just watch!"

Watching this scene, Ba Qi couldn't help but sneer.

Though not old, he'd seen much and carried the shadow of death, making him far more mature than Ruan Tang Ren. He saw right through Lady Sea Snake's tricks.

Ruan Tang Ren had never been this driven in his entire life!

Feeling motivated, he pulled out a large coil of rope and wrapped it around his waist.

The rope was thick, its diameter akin to an adult's fist, yet nearly transparent, its surface merely shimmering with faint golden threads of light.

It was Bu Kong Jun's Weakening Fishing Line.

Such thickness and length were natural, for Bu Kong Jun was neither human nor elf, but a mushroom treeman. In the underground world, colossal mushroom trees thrive. Bu Kong Kun was awakened by druidic enlightenment, then cultivated step by step to become a legend level magic chef.

As a giant, the fishing line Bu Kong Kun used appeared thick and enormous to ordinary people.

The gold level Weakening Fishing Line was not only transparent in material but also possessed an extremely muted aura, the essence of gold level was nearly imperceptible.

Ruan Tang Ren drew out the line's tip, infusing it with his silver level battle qi.

Though silver level battle qi couldn't exist independently in the air like gold level could, it could still separate from the body and be channeled into weapons or armor to enhance their power.

After the infusion, the line's tip immediately curled upward, as if suddenly alive.

Ruan Tang Ren flung it with all his might, sending the Weakening Fishing Line flying far out.

Dragged by the line's tip, it shot toward the boat's edge, then plummeted straight down. Within seconds, it plunged into the sea, heading directly for the sea serpent.

The fishing line's aura was concealed to near-invisibility, yet careful detection could still detect a faint trace.

After all, this was a gold level artifact. Ruan Tang Ren, being only silver level, struggled to harness its full power with his battle qi, like a small horse pulling a heavy cart, his strength insufficient.

Only a gold level battle qi could conceal the line's aura completely.

Still, for the current situation, this level of concealment was entirely sufficient.

Though the sea serpent was a formidable gold level creature, its intelligence paled in comparison to that of a fishman. Moreover, now in a state of frenzied rage, it hadn't detected the fishing line's approach at all.

As if possessed by eyes of its own, the fishing line effortlessly coiled around the gold level sea serpent, binding it tightly.

The sea serpent, sensing its renewed confinement and severely restricted movement, grew even more enraged, churning up massive waves in the ocean.

On the Fluffy Cloud, Ruan Tang Ren fought the gold level sea serpent single-handedly. He was frantically channeling his battle qi, his face flushed crimson as he strained every muscle, his entire body trembling slightly.

He desperately harnessed the power of the Weakening Fishing Line, steadily sapping the sea serpent's strength and deepening its state of exhaustion.

The gold level sea serpent's resistance grew weaker by the moment.

A flicker of joy crossed his face as he looked toward Lady Sea Snake, “I've got it under control. Maybe I won't need Lord Ba Qi to intervene. I might just...”

He didn't realize that the serpent's weakened state was merely a feigned lull, the calm before the storm.

In the next instant, the gold level sea serpent unleashed a spell, doubling its strength beyond measure!

Ruan Tang Ren's face paled. Before he could react, the fishing line yanked him away.

Thud! He slammed into the mast, quickly wrapping his softened, elongated arms around it.

The line coiled around his waist, still dragging him toward the water below.

At the other end, the gold level sea serpent swam with all its might, plunging into the ocean depths.

An ordinary silver level fighter would likely have been torn apart alive by this force. But Ruan Tang Ren possessed a unique bloodline and a compatible battle qi technique. His bones and flesh could temporarily soften, stretch, and even undergo minor deformation.

“See? I've got this under control. Don't... don't worry!” Ruan Tang Ren boasted to Lady Sea Snake.

“That move was a bit excessive for him,” Lady Sea Snake rolled her eyes inwardly, though her expression remained outwardly concerned.

Her worry wasn't entirely feigned.

Of course, her concern wasn't for Ruan Tang Ren himself, but rather that he might screw up and cause the entire operation to fail.

Still, Lady Sea Snake was well-prepared. Should this sea serpent slip away, she had backup targets ready.

Only, there was no second Da Ji Clan to serve as cannon fodder this time.

Half a minute later, Ruan Tang Ren's expression darkened. Despite his all-out tugging, the gold level sea serpent continued diving deeper into the ocean.

He was forced to release several coils of fishing line from his waist.

The gold level Weakening Fishing Line could stretch, thinning under tension yet retaining remarkable resilience, unlikely to snap anytime soon.

“Damn it, my battle qi's down to less than a third!” He finally shouted after another thirty seconds, “Quick, feed me the potion!”

With both arms and legs wrapped around the mast and his other hand gripping the slack fishing line, he couldn't take the potion himself.

The first mate hurried over, opened the bottle, and poured it into Ruan Tang Ren's open mouth.

Ruan Tang Ren swallowed the potion successfully. Its effects kicked in immediately, dramatically increasing the rate at which battle qi was generated within him. Despite the constant depletion, his reserves began to rise.

But the next instant, the gold level sea serpent erupted again.

Ruan Tang Ren wasn't torn apart, but the mast could no longer bear the strain and snapped clean off.

His face paled. Without anything to cling to, he was yanked straight out.

Just as he was about to crash through the ship's rail and be dragged into the waterway by the gold level sea serpent, at the critical moment, a thunderous roar filled the air. Ba Qi suddenly appeared at the ship's rail, extending his massive hand to grab Ruan Tang Ren's shoulder.

Only then did a wave of air surge over.

Ba Qi's intervention was as steady as mountains, instantly securing Ruan Tang Ren firmly against the ship's rail.

Still shaken, Ruan Tang Ren hurriedly thanked him, finally comprehending why Lady Sea Snake had spared no expense to summon Ba Qi's aid.

“Captain Ruan Tang Ren, how are you?” Lady Sea Snake asked anxiously.

Her concern, of course, wasn't for Ruan Tang Ren, but for the sea serpent lurking beneath the waves.

Yet, well-versed in human psychology, she didn't voice this fear. Instead, she inquired about Ruan Tang Ren's condition.

Ruan Tang Ren felt a twinge of embarrassment, but the warmth of his beloved's concern outweighed it. He quickly replied, “I'm fine, cough cough, I'm fine. The sea serpent is under my control too.”

He answered rapidly, blood spurting from his mouth as he spoke.

His struggle against the gold level sea serpent had already pushed him to his limits. The impact from their clash had been immense, inflicting serious damage.

Lady Sea Snake's brows furrowed deeply. Her assessment of the situation left her far from optimistic.

Chapter 650: Ba Qi’s Strength

Ruan Tang Ren was desperate to prove himself, but actions spoke louder than words, gradually eroding Lady Sea Snake’s faith in his claims.

Yet what followed unfolded exactly as Ruan Tang Ren had predicted: the gold level sea serpent’s strength steadily waned.

Lacking wisdom, the serpent had recklessly squirted venom and slaughtered fishmen early on, severely depleting its power. Later, during its struggle with Ruan Tang Ren, it failed to take proper measures against the nearly transparent fishing line, instead persistently dragging the submersible. As time stretched on, the effect of the Weakening Fishing Line accumulated, steadily draining the sea serpent's strength.

Ba Qi held onto Ruan Tang Ren with one hand while planting his foot on the gunwale, leaning over to observe the sea surface.

Activating his battle qi to investigate, he observed the thrashing sea serpent deep below and murmured praise: “You've chosen your target well this time.”

This constituted early-stage intelligence gathering.

Lady Sea Snake's choice of target wasn't arbitrary. It followed extensive reconnaissance and observation, revealing the nature of the serpent's racial magic. They prioritized those posing minimal threat to fishing lines.

The sea serpent she was currently attacking clearly lacked any line-severing racial magic.

“Weakening Fishing Line, quite impressive,” Ba Qi remarked with a hint of admiration.

Regardless of the situation, the silver level Ruan Tang Ren was genuinely using this equipment to take on a gold level sea serpent beyond his tier.

Ruan Tang Ren's lips trembled, wanting to respond to Ba Qi's praise, but he couldn't manage it.

His face grew paler by the second. Though he'd taken stimulants earlier, their effects had worn off. His battle qi reserves were plummeting, and he was on the verge of collapse.

“I'm about to act.” Ba Qi pressed a hand down on Ruan Tang Ren, aware of his condition, and spoke calmly.

The next moment, he began a long, deep inhalation.

This breath lasted over ten seconds, stirring the air in the windless sky. The residual force caused the Fluffy Cloud to sway several times.

When Ba Qi finished inhaling, his chest swelled, and his entire body seemed to grow several centimeters taller.

He leaned forward, gazing at the sea surface, then gently parted his lips to exhale.

His lips tightened into a thin slit. The compressed air and battle qi surged through the opening, erupting outward.

A piercing, shrill whistle nearly ruptured the eardrums of those nearby.

Battle skill - Single Breath: Needle Form!

Ba Qi cultivated the Air Pressure battle qi, excelling at compressing air currents. The air he expelled was violently compressed, turning milky white and forming a slender needle.

Thinner than silk, the needle plunged into the sea in an instant, piercing hundreds of meters through the water to strike the sea serpent's body.

In a normal battle, Ba Qi would naturally aim for the gold level sea serpent's vital points, its skull, throat, and so on. But this battle aimed to capture the sea serpent alive, so he redirected the qi needle to pierce its spine instead.

The wound was too small, causing at most a slight tingling sensation.

The gold level sea serpent continued to struggle fiercely. Its massive size was its advantage, as long as the attack didn't hit a vital spot, its combat effectiveness remained largely unaffected.

Ba Qi's expression remained unchanged as he continued to blow steadily.

Yet silently, he activated his second battle skill.

Battle skill - Finger-Circling Softness.

This auxiliary battle skill altered the form of other battle skills.

Single Breath·Needle Form, originally linear and only capable of straight paths, could now bend and curve under Finger-Circling Softness's influence.

The qi needle extended and lengthened, piercing into the sea serpent's body, then emerging before thrusting back in again, as if stitching a long strip of flesh.

With each penetration, the needle pierced through the serpent's bones, sinking deep into the marrow.

The skeleton was the toughest, most resilient part of the gold level sea serpent's body, yet it offered no defense against the qi needle's assault.

Lady Sea Snake, observing the battlefield through her spell, witnessed this scene and couldn't help but reveal a look of shock.

On the ghost ship silently drawing nearer, Jian Gui also felt a slight sinking in his heart.

Ruan Tang Ren indirectly sensed Ba Qi's strength, for the gold level sea serpent had once again increased its strength, becoming extremely frenzied.

Yet the pressure Ruan Tang Ren endured was significantly reduced compared to before.

This was because Ba Qi used his needle to assist Ruan Tang Ren, substantially lightening his burden. Most of the resistance was absorbed by the interwoven air threads of the needle.

The needle continuously pierced through, sewing countless white threads onto the gold level sea serpent's body.

Each penetration of the needle precisely avoided the Weakening Fishing Line. The number of threads rapidly increased, soon outnumbering the fishing line.

Ba Qi's mastery of these two battle skills was profoundly intricate, his micro-control truly breathtaking.

The transparent Weakening Fishing Line remained invisible, but the white air threads grew increasingly conspicuous.

They densely entangled the gold level sea serpent, as if knitting it a sweater.

Soon, the density of the air threads surpassed that of the gold level fishing net.

The sea serpent struggled desperately, its blood spilling continuously, staining the surrounding sea red.

“You can try reeling it in now,” Ba Qi instructed.

Ruan Tang Ren obeyed, beginning to reel in the line spool by spool.

After all, the Weakening Fishing Line was physical; the compressed air needles and threads couldn't haul the sea serpent up.

After a few pulls, Ruan Tang Ren was already panting heavily.

He had to order the first mate to pour several more bottles of potion into him.

The potions swiftly restored his strength, allowing him to slowly and painfully haul the sea serpent upward.

The sea serpent naturally resisted, attempting to dive deeper into the ocean.

Ba Qi coordinated his efforts, slightly opening his mouth. The expelled airflow instantly swelled, causing the air threads to expand and thicken five or six times over. The damage inflicted upon the sea serpent surged fifty to sixty times!

The sea serpent let out a piercing shriek, the excruciating pain rendering it nearly immobile for an instant.

Ruan Tang Ren seized the opportunity to reel in the line forcefully, dragging the sea serpent upward by over ten meters.

Ba Qi controlled the airflow, shrinking the air threads back to its original thinness.

The sea serpent regained its ability to move and resumed struggling. Ba Qi once again thickened the air thread's diameter, inflicting fresh wounds and instantly crippling the serpent's mobility.

Ruan Tang Ren seized the moment to reel in another dozen meters.

After repeating this cycle multiple times, the sea serpent was now just over 100 meters from the surface.

As if sensing its impending doom, the gold level sea serpent unleashed its most ferocious counterattack yet.

It desperately unleashed every racial magic it possessed, wildly attacking the surrounding sea surface, even striking itself in the process.

Countless air threads shattered, and its own attacks left its body torn and bleeding.

Ruan Tang Ren saw black spots before his eyes, nearly fainting, as the fishing line was pulled out another 80 meters.

Witnessing this brutal tug-of-war and exhaustion, the Sea Snake Woman's face clouded with worry. She couldn't help but warn, “Lord Ba Qi, at this rate, the sea snake will kill itself!”

Ba Qi nodded slightly.

If this battle were merely about eliminating the sea serpent, it would be far simpler.

But they needed to capture it alive and subdue it, a task exponentially more difficult.

This challenge did not faze Ba Qi.

His eyes widened. While maintaining the first two battle skills, he effortlessly unleashed his third battle skill.

Battle skill - Airtight Seal!

In the next instant, the air threads expanded a hundredfold, fusing seamlessly with the surrounding threads.

Yet the strands embedded within the sea serpent's body remained unaffected, retaining their original thickness.

The external air threads instantly enveloped the serpent, forming wide, bandage-like strands that shielded it from its own suicidal assault.

The gold level racial magic possessed extraordinary power, but the airflow bandages continuously drew sustenance from Ba Qi. After over a minute of stalemate, the sea serpent collapsed first, utterly drained of the strength to cast any further racial magic.

Its expenditure had been too immense; the magic crystal within its body could no longer fuel a single racial magic.

The air-flow bandages continued spreading, layering over it like a mummification process.

The sea serpent's vitality plummeted to its lowest point, leaving it utterly unable to struggle.

Ruan Tang Ren was nearly drained of strength too, but under the encouraging, expectant gaze of Lady Sea Snake, he squeezed out a fresh surge of power.

Finally, gritting his teeth, he succeeded in pulling the sea serpent up to the surface.

The mummy-like sea serpent floated motionless on the sea surface, helpless and vulnerable.

Ba Qi continued to blow air, sustaining the three battle skills.

In contrast, Ruan Tang Ren slumped against the ship's rail, gasping for breath. His entire body was drenched in sweat, as if he'd just been pulled from the water.

For the sake of his beloved, he had truly given his all this time.

“It's my turn now,” Lady Sea Snake said as she flew down from Fluffy Cloud, not forgetting to express her gratitude, “Thank you both for your tremendous help.”

Hearing these words, a new spark of vitality suddenly burst forth in the weary, lifeless eyes of Ruan Tang Ren, and he felt his strength returning.

Lady Sea Snake landed on the water's surface near the sea serpent and began its examination.

Though she had observed the battle, this close-up inspection required different spells to truly gauge the sea serpent's condition and determine if it was suitable to be taken as a magic pet.

The results were promising.

Lady Sea Snake immediately produced a spell scroll.

The scroll radiated a silver level aura. Unfolding it revealed a hexagram-centered magic formation diagram, densely packed with contract spell runes. Flanking it were crescent and square magic formations. Most distinctive was the serpentine pattern at the hexagram's core.

These were no ordinary magic lines; it was a totem!

Lady Sea Snake employed not only conventional spell scroll crafting techniques in this scroll but also incorporated beastman totem alchemy.

This magic scroll was meticulously transcribed and crafted by her own hand. A scroll of this caliber typically consumed at least a month of her time. Of course, she did not neglect other duties during this month, but rather dedicated five to six hours daily to crafting spell scrolls.

The alchemy materials alone consumed exceeded 200 gold coins. Factoring in time and labor made it even more costly. Yet these exorbitant expenses were entirely justified.

For such a scroll enabled a silver level Lady Sea Snake to command a gold level sea serpent beyond her own level.

Commanding higher level creatures was not unheard of, but the significance and value grew exponentially with each tier.

A silver level controlling a gold level was exceptionally rare.

The former belonged to the lower echelons of the transcendent hierarchy, while the latter had already reached the middle, a clear distinction in social standing.

Many coveted such scrolls, willing to pay any price, yet they remained elusive.
